# NeoSpace — holistic audit, round 2

_Generated 2026-10-09 against `7bcc4e7` (two later commits, `eb41f08` and `b82137e`, landed during the pass; the high-impact items were re-checked against them). Ten parallel code audits, one per area, each read its files in full. Then a live pass on neospace.ibm.io as a guest at 1024px and 375px. Then an adversarial verification of every high-impact item. Every item cites `file:line`, and line numbers will drift as fixes land._

This is round 2. Round 1 was [AUDIT-2026-10.md](./AUDIT-2026-10.md), with 744 items, nearly all fixed. Auditors skipped anything round 1 already fixed. Items marked **REGRESSED/NOT FIXED** were ticked in round 1 but are still broken.

**Format:** `[LENS·Impact·Effort]`. Impact is **H**igh, **M**edium or **L**ow; Effort is **S**mall, **M**edium or **L**arge. LENS is one of UX, DESIGN, A11Y, CODE, PERF, SEC or TOOLING. The tail tag says who should do it (see below). ✓ means an independent verifier confirmed it. *Verifier:* notes correct an item that was real but overstated; 18 such items were downgraded from H to M.

**Totals:** 1091 items: 66 high impact, 464 medium and 561 low. By lens: UX 592, CODE 166, DESIGN 101, A11Y 96, PERF 55, TOOLING 47, SEC 34.

You asked for 700. Like round 1, this lists everything that held up instead of cutting to the number. For a ~700 cut, take every H and M item (530), then add `mech` L items from the areas you care most about until you reach 700.

**Verification:** all 74 high-impact items were re-checked against current code: 52 confirmed, 22 real but overstated (corrected inline), 0 wrong. Before that, I spot-checked 6 items by hand and found 1 false positive (removed). M/L items were not independently re-verified, so whoever fixes one should confirm it in the code first.

---

## Who does what

Every item carries a tag:

- **`mech`**: isolated and mechanical. The line alone says what to change. Good for Cursor, or a cheap batch run.
- **`judgment`**: needs reading the surrounding code, cross-file reasoning or design taste. Better for Claude.
- **`decision`**: needs you to choose a product direction first. Nobody should start these until you've decided.

| Tag | H | M | L | Total |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mech` | 23 | 276 | 444 | **743** |
| `judgment` | 37 | 161 | 87 | **285** |
| `decision` | 6 | 27 | 30 | **63** |

Suggested split: give Cursor the `mech` items area by area (each area section is a self-contained work order). Claude takes `judgment`, especially every H item and anything security-related (sanitizeCss, OAuth, feedback API, handoff origins). You go through the `decision` list first, since a few of those unblock other items.

### Decisions for you (H and M)

Short form. The full item, with its suggested fix, is in its area section.

- [M] `app/pages/explore.vue` — Route is `/explore` but the page title, nav and h1 all say "Search". _(Seen on the live site)_
- [M] `app/stores/columns.ts:43` — Home is labelled "For You". _(Board & columns)_
- [M] `app/stores/columns.ts:722` — Profile sync is disabled, but `initialize()` still *reads* the public `neospace_columns` field. _(Board & columns)_
- [H] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:885` — The ⋯ menu has no Delete, Edit, "Delete & redraft", Pin or "Mute conversation" for your own posts (`deleteStatus` exists in the store but only DMs use it). _(Posts & compose)_
- [M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:930` — There's no Translate action for posts in other languages, even though `status.language` is known and Mastodon exposes `POST /statuses/:id/translate` (gated by `configuration.translation.enabled`). _(Posts & compose)_
- [M] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:28` — The dirty-close confirm offers only Discard or stay, even though `useDraft` already autosaves. Users who want to come back later must discard. _(Posts & compose)_
- [M] `app/components/SuiteMenu.vue:28-32` — The only way in is the bottom-right waffle, which is hidden while compose is open (`layouts/default.vue:252`). _(Edward)_
- [M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1459-1473` — `applyEdgeHoverScroll` scrubs the stream whenever the mouse rests in the outer 13%/11% bands. _(Edward)_
- [M] `app/components/edward/EdwardWatchActions.vue:161-176` — Heart, boost and stash go through `usePostActions`, which acts as the account that fetched the post (`usePostActions.ts:131-140`, with a one-time "Acting as…" toast). _(Edward)_
- [M] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1375-1386,1424-1435,1454-1462` — The Posting panel mixes two save models: "Collapse duplicate reposts" and "Photo upload size" apply instantly, while visibility and sensitive media wait for "Save Posting Defaults" further down. _(Settings)_
- [M] `app/stores/settings.ts:128-130` — `reading:expand:media`, `reading:expand:spoilers` and `reading:autoplay:gifs` are fetched and typed but nothing in `app/` reads them, and there's no in-app equivalent. _(Settings)_
- [M] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2425-2429,1081-1085,425-436` — The hover/focus preview outline (`is-previewing`) looks exactly like the selected outline (`active`), so while the pointer is over a swatch you can't tell which theme is actually set. _(Settings)_
- [M] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:280,346` — Brutal's `$mark` (danger) is `#000000`, the same as ink (1.00:1). _(Settings)_
- [M] `app/layouts/default.vue:85` + `MobileTabBar.vue:88-151`: the Messages and Activity tabs route to `/messages` and `/notifications`. _(App shell)_
- [M] `app/plugins/pwa-rescue.client.ts:23-38` + `nuxt.config.ts:43`: every refocus (throttled to 60s) calls `reg.update()`. _(App shell)_
- [M] `app/layouts/default.vue:394-404` + `SuiteMenu.vue:187-194`: on phones, the 44px waffle floats permanently over the right edge of the content above the tab bar on every page except threads. _(App shell)_
- [M] `app/stores/insights.ts:201` — Followers-only posts are excluded from your own charts as well as from export (also `utils/insights.ts:191`). For people who mostly post followers-only, every KPI reads near zero. _(Profile)_
- [M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:662` — `fetchThread` only asks the viewer's server. Replies your server never received are missing, and the only way to see the full remote thread is the globe link out of the app. _(Threads)_
- [M] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:280-286,465` — Enter always sends, including on touch keyboards that have no Shift key, so mobile users can't type a line break in a DM. _(Threads)_
- [M] `app/pages/notifications.vue:352-360` — Leaving the page marks every loaded notification read, even if the user only looked at the Likes filter or a search result, so unseen mentions lose their unread state. _(Threads)_
- [M] `app/components/NeoReport.vue:82-85` — `forward` defaults to `true` (`app/stores/overlay.ts:166`), so remote admins get a copy unless the user unticks a vaguely worded box ("Forward to the remote server (when applicable)"). _(Threads)_
- [H] `app/stores/instances.ts:660` — `addInstance` accepts auth-gated hosts for watching and reports "Watching mastodon.social". _(Accounts)_
- [M] `app/pages/login.vue:155` — A signed-out visitor gets no "Continue without signing in" path, though guest browsing exists (`DEFAULT_PUBLIC_INSTANCE`). The only exits are Explore and an external sign-up link. _(Accounts)_
- [M] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:240` — "Make this main" and InstanceManager's "Make main" (`:245-252`) have no functional effect. _(Accounts)_
- [M] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:82-86` — Signed-in users get no explanation that a "group" is a followed hashtag: there's no lede, Join doesn't say what it does, and nothing says other people can't see membership. _(Groups)_
- [H] `functions/api/feedback.ts:221-244,287` — Only the `Origin` check guards issue creation, and non-browser clients can fake that header. _(Platform)_
- [H] `functions/api/feedback.ts:335-348` — NOT FIXED (audit #873, "upload to R2"). _(Platform)_
- [H] `app/utils/feedback.ts:73-76` — Up to 40 log lines go into every public issue: home-server URLs (`notifications.ts:256` `${inst.url}`), instance ids and stack traces. _(Platform)_
- [H] `public/_headers:7` + `app/composables/useLoomHandoff.ts:108-123,233-244` — With `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin`, NeoSpace opened by a cross-origin Loom, bruh or Throughline tab gets `window.opener === null`. _(Platform)_
- [M] `nuxt.config.ts:43,97-98` — REGRESSED (audit #913 says "already registerType:'prompt', skipWaiting:false"). _(Platform)_
- [M] `nuxt.config.ts:49-89` — The manifest has no `share_target`, though the app already accepts `?compose=…&text=` and a social client is an obvious share target. _(Platform)_
- [M] `package.json:19` — `lint` just prints "eslint not configured" and exits 0, so any human or bot running `npm run lint` sees green. _(Platform)_
- [M] `package.json:23` — NOT FIXED (audit #927, "deploy from CI only"). `cf:deploy` still passes `--commit-dirty=true`, and no workflow uses `cf:deploy:ci`. _(Platform)_

## Start here: top 5 per area

These are each auditor's top 5. Several high-impact items were softened by the verifier; check the inline *Verifier:* note under the full item before starting.

### Board & columns
1. `TimelineColumn.vue:1189` — Switching a column's feed shows the old feed's posts under the new label, and can keep them there indefinitely (see A1).
2. `TimelineColumn.vue:779` — since_id polling only fetches the newest 20 posts, so busy feeds silently lose everything in between, with no gap marker (see A2).
3. `TimelineColumn.vue:1096` — At the end of Local/Federated, the server fallback appends another server's newest posts and paging never ends (see A3).
4. (dropped on verification — `o` works via PostHeader thread link)
5. `columns.ts:536` — `ensureFocusedView` silently evicts a column when the board is full, and its Undo toast can never work (see D1).

### Posts & compose
1. Mute, block and report target the wrong person when a post was served by another server, because a relative `acct` is resolved on your server (`RealPostCard.vue:599`, `status.ts:404`).
2. `resolveAccount` falls back to a fuzzy search and takes `results[0]` without checking it, so mute/block/report can hit a lookalike account (`status.ts:347`).
3. Replies and profile taps use the relative `acct` too: you mention, or open the profile of, a same-named local user instead of the remote author (`RealPostCard.vue:449`, `RealPostCard.vue:142`).
4. A CW'd post still shows its link-preview card (image and title) and its poll options, because both render outside the CW gate (`RealPostCard.vue:970`, `RealPostCard.vue:1036`).
5. REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: polls can't be voted on, and expiry and your own vote aren't shown (`RealPostCard.vue:1036`).

### Edward (lean-back 3D discovery mode)
1. Cancelling a reply leaves Edward stuck: dimmed, can't be clicked, and ignoring Esc (`EdwardWatchActions.vue:127`, `EdwardPostModal.vue:242`; the components that would un-dim it unmount at `EdwardMode.vue:395`/`1442`). See "Watch actions" and "Post peek".
2. Ghosting or blocking someone doesn't keep them out. Their queued posts in the backpack still drip back onto the stage, and the drop matches the wrong people across servers (`stores/edward.ts:573`, `useEdwardStream.ts:70`).
3. Replies mention the wrong person. `@bob` (a name that only makes sense on the post's own server) is put into a reply sent from your server (`EdwardWatchActions.vue:131`, `EdwardPostModal.vue:246`).
4. When the network is down, re-seeding never backs off. Every 12 s it fans out to every server plus home, tags, trends and the guest fallback, forever (`useEdwardStream.ts:474-507`).
5. The WebGL loop draws full frames at 60 fps even when nothing moves: speed `still`, reduced motion, the peek open, or recessed under compose (`EdwardCanvas.vue:1496-1525`).

### Settings, theming & design system
1. `app/components/NeoRadioGroup.vue:30-49` — Arrow keys select the next option but never move focus, so keyboard users get stuck after one step in every segmented control (Font size, Corners, Density, Lines, Flip).
2. `app/stores/settings.ts:166,361-367,845-862` — Posting defaults (visibility / sensitive) are one global local override that shadows every account's server preference; saving "Followers only" on account A makes account B post followers-only too.
3. `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:389` — `--neo-focus` is defined only in the Beta theme; `AlgorithmSheet.vue:749,899,944` and `ChatComposer.vue:829` use `var(--neo-focus)` with no fallback, so those controls have no focus ring in the other 11 themes.
4. `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1912-1916,2237-2241` — Settings inputs and search set `outline:none` with a scoped selector that outranks the global `:focus-visible` and forced-colors rules; the only indicator is a 12%-alpha accent glow, which disappears completely in Windows High Contrast.
5. `app/utils/appearance.ts:273-280` — Nothing listens for `prefers-color-scheme` / `prefers-contrast` changes, so "Auto" stays on the old scheme until reload when the OS switches light/dark (e.g. at sunset).

### App shell, navigation & shared UI primitives
1. `app/layouts/default.vue:247`: "Leave a note" does nothing on desktop subview routes, then the dialog appears later by itself (see Layout).
2. `app/components/NeoMenu.vue:128-135`: ArrowUp on a menu trigger puts focus on the first item instead of the last (see NeoMenu).
3. `app/components/shell/MobileHeader.vue:26,50`: the `chrome-hint` portal cue never shows on the header because no style exists there (see MobileHeader).
4. `app/composables/useAppShellHeight.ts:18-25` + `app/layouts/default.vue:240,251-261`: with the iOS keyboard open, the tab bar and the bottom dock ride up above the keyboard (see Layout).
5. REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/utils/appearance.ts:282-286` + `DesktopSidebar.vue:519-527`: the shell theme control still cycles one way only and can never go back to Auto (see DesktopSidebar).

### Profile, Explore & Search
1. [SEC·H·S] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:78` — The `position:fixed` guard can be bypassed by putting a blocked token right before `position` (for example `a{javascript:position:fixed;inset:0}`), so another user's profile CSS can draw a fixed overlay over the whole app.
2. [SEC·H·M] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:30` — `position:absolute` is still allowed and `#main-content` is not a containing block, so scoped profile CSS can cover the sidebar, nav and dialogs. An empty selector part can also restyle `#main-content` itself.
3. [UX·H·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:294` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: the edit form fills the bio with `stripHtml(note)`, which collapses all paragraphs and line breaks. Every save turns a multi-paragraph bio into one run-on line and turns remote mentions into local ones.
4. [CODE·H·M] `app/pages/explore.vue:528` + `app/composables/useFediverseSearch.ts:538` — Switching accounts while Explore is open keeps the old server's results and relationships. Follow then sends server-A account ids to server B.
5. [UX·H·M] `app/pages/profile.vue:620` — Other people's profiles have no Mute, Block, Report, Unmute or Unblock action, even though the page shows "Muted" and "Blocked" chips the user can't act on.

### Threads, notifications & direct messages
1. `app/pages/status/[id].vue:930`: on phones, the find-in-thread field unmounts as soon as the soft keyboard opens, so search can't be used on touch devices.
2. `app/pages/status/[id].vue:78-81` + `app/pages/messages.vue:93`: group DMs open on `lastStatus`, and the context call returns only that post's ancestor chain. Messages on sibling branches never appear.
3. `app/components/NeoReport.vue:9-13` + `app/stores/status.ts:442-448`: "Policy violation" reports are sent without `rule_ids`, which Mastodon rejects, so the report fails.
4. `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1145-1146` + `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:43`: switching to the Profile pane on mobile (or crossing 1024px) unmounts the DM composer and drops the typed text and photos. There is no draft persistence.
5. `app/stores/notifications.ts:122-130` + `app/pages/notifications.vue:507-520`: notification filters only look at the 30 loaded rows per account. "Mentions" or "Polls" says "Nothing here" and never pages further back.

### Accounts, auth & server picking
1. [SEC·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:764` — The cached OAuth app is reused forever and only dropped on `invalid_client` during the token step. A server reset sends you to the server's own error page, which never redirects back, so sign-in to that server stays broken for good (see A1).
2. [UX·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:688` — Sign-in needs `addInstance` to succeed, and that only tries `/api/v2/instance`. Servers without v2 (Mastodon <4.0, older Pleroma/Akkoma, Friendica, Mastodon-API shims) can't sign in at all (see C1).
3. [CODE·H·S] `app/composables/useMasto.ts:139` — `publicClient` uses the *first* slot on a host. With two accounts on one server, reads run as the wrong account, or fall back to fosstodon as a guest (see B1).
4. [UX·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:1069` — 401s are only noticed during the startup verify. A token revoked mid-session leaves the account showing as active while every column says "Sign in required" (see B2).
5. [UX·H·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:799` — "Add account" on a server you're already signed in to sends no `force_login`. Mastodon silently re-approves the same browser user, which creates a duplicate slot (see A3).

### Groups (hashtag groups) & ranked-feed algorithms
1. `app/stores/groups.ts:1027-1037` + `:836-848` — Joining a group from **search results** never flips the card to "Joined" (the result object isn't in the store). (see Groups store #1)
2. `app/stores/groups.ts:583-628` — Switching accounts (or logging out) keeps the previous account's group memberships in the sidebar, /groups and compose picker. (see Groups store #2)
3. `app/utils/rankers.ts:264` + `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:95-110` — X-style silently drops home-timeline posts from people you follow whenever the follow list is truncated (>400), failed, or `currentUser` wasn't loaded yet. (see useRankedFeed #1)
4. `app/utils/rankers.ts:199,208-213` — "Mentions you" / "you follow" match on the local username only, so any `@alice@elsewhere` counts as you or your follow (+45 score for "the author may reply to you"). (see Rankers #1)
5. `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:99,328` — Posting to a group with a non-public default visibility (hidden selector on mobile) still shows the post in the group feed, though it never reaches the hashtag timeline. (see Group detail #2)

### Platform, security, handoffs, build/deploy, tests & docs
1. `app/composables/useLoomHandoff.ts:190-199`: Loom postMessage shares from `https://loom.ibm.io` get sent to the notebook handler and dropped, because `isNotebookOrigin` trusts every `*.ibm.io` (see C1)
2. `public/_headers:7`: `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin` cuts `window.opener`, so the whole postMessage handoff path (ready pings, ACKs, Loom image buffers, bruh caption upgrade) never runs in production (see C3)
3. `nuxt.config.ts:43,97-98`: REGRESSED. `autoUpdate` with `skipWaiting` force-reloads every open tab whenever a deploy lands (see D1)
4. `functions/api/feedback.ts:335-348`: NOT FIXED. Screenshots go into the issue as `data:` URIs, which GitHub strips, so the screenshot feature silently does nothing (see A3)
5. `app/utils/feedback.ts:73-76`: client logs, including the user's home-server URLs, are posted into public GitHub issues and the dialog never says so (see B1)

## Seen on the live site (neospace.ibm.io, 2026-10-09, guest, desktop 1024 + 375px mobile)

### Guest flows
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:452` — Guests can never open a profile: `onMounted` pushes `/login` whenever there's no authenticated account, even for `?user=` on a public account, while the board happily shows that person's public posts. Deep links to profiles from shares/search dead-end at Sign in. → Load public profiles via the guest/preview client (`lookup` + public statuses) and only gate follow/message/edit. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:400` — `router.push('/login')` (also :459) adds a history entry, so Back from Sign in bounces straight back to the redirect. → `router.replace`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:654` — Signed-out users get every settings section (Profile, Privacy, Notifications, Posting defaults, Filters, Account). Profile shows an editable Display name/Bio/Discoverable form and a Save button that can't work. → For guests, show Appearance only plus a "Sign in to edit your profile and account" row; hide/disable server-backed panels. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:662` — Opening Settings on a phone focuses the search box immediately, popping the soft keyboard over the section list. → Autofocus search only on fine pointers / desktop; on mobile focus the dialog heading. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/shell/MobileDrawer.vue:220` — The guest drawer shows two "Sign in" CTAs (guest card at :227 and footer at :370) within one screen. → Keep the card; drop the footer button for guests. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:92` — "Log in to join groups" / "Log in" (also `GroupCard.vue:163`, `groups/[tag].vue:302,412`) while the rest of the app says "Sign in". → Standardise on "Sign in". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue` — Guest thread with no replies says "No replies yet — be the first." directly above a "Sign in to reply" button. → For guests: "No replies yet." · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/login.vue` — The sign-in page has no way back to the board for someone who was browsing as a guest (no back/close/home link; only browser Back). → Add a "← Keep browsing" link to `/`. · mech

### Board & posts
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1191` — The focused post on a thread page is still clamped with "See more" (RealPostCard's `contentExpanded` starts false). The one post you navigated to read is truncated. → Pass an `expanded`/`focused` prop that disables the clamp for `thread-post--focus`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:953` — On 375px the clamp cuts posts after ~4 lines, so even short 5-line posts get "See more"; nearly every card in the live Local feed showed it. → Raise the clamp on mobile (e.g. 8–10 lines) or only clamp when overflow > ~1.5 lines. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·H·M] `app/layouts/default.vue` — At 375px the bottom 300px stacks five floating layers: install banner, notes FAB (left), drag handle (right), "Local (fosstodon.org)" feed pill and the tab bar; the banner covers post actions. → Suppress the install banner on first visit / until the 2nd session, and hide FAB + handle while it shows; consider moving the feed pill into the header. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/InstallAppBanner.vue:29` — Android copy "Chrome may hide Install in the menu — tap below for steps." is awkward, and the button is beside it, not below. → "Add NeoSpace to your home screen for a full-screen app." + "How to install". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue` — Mobile column header still shows a ≡-style "switch feed" icon directly under the global ☰ (two hamburger glyphs stacked); the old audit marked this merged. REGRESSED/NOT FIXED. → Make the column title itself the switcher (chevron) and drop the extra icon on mobile. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue` — Mobile shows the feed name twice: header "Local (fosstodon.org)" and the bottom pill "Local (fosstodon.org)". → Shorten one (pill can show just "Local"). · decision
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue` — Avatars have no placeholder background, so lazily loading cards show a blank 44px hole at the left of the first posts on mobile. → Give `.status-avatar` a `--neo-bg-tertiary` background + initials fallback. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1008` — Every alt-less image wears a "No description" badge; on a media-heavy feed it reads as noise and slightly shames posters. → Show only on hover/focus or in a setting ("Flag images without alt text"). · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·S] Add-column menu — For a guest, "For You" (Home) is offered under Add column but greyed out in the Switch feed menu with no explanation. → Hide it for guests or label "Sign in for your Home feed". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] Feed naming is inconsistent: sidebar "Local", menus "Local (fosstodon.org)", algorithms "X-style For You" / "Facebook-style Feed" / "TikTok-style For You". → One pattern ("For You · X-style"); decide whether to use third-party trademarks in UI. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] Sidebar "Algorithms · New" — "New" in accent colour reads as a "new feature" badge, not a create action. → "+ New" or an icon button labelled "Create algorithm". · mech

### Settings (live)
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue` — Profile › Account flags: "Discoverable" puts its checkbox under the description, "This is a bot account" puts it right-aligned on the label row — two layouts in one group. → One toggle-row pattern (switch on the right). · mech

### Platform (live headers)

## Board & columns

### A. TimelineColumn — loading, polling, paging
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1189` — Changing the feed (watcher at 1189–1195) calls `fetchTimeline(true)`, which keeps the current `statuses` visible because `showLoader` is false when the list is non-empty (990). Until the new page lands, the header already says "Federated" over Local's posts. If the fetch fails, the error is suppressed because `statuses` isn't empty (1035), so the wrong feed stays up for good under the new label. Scroll offset is kept, and the next poll uses the *old* feed's top id as `since_id` (782). → On a feed-key change, clear `statuses` and `pendingNew`, set `scrollTop = 0` and `isNearTop = true`, then fetch. Keep the soft-refresh path only for pull-to-refresh and auto-retry. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [CODE·H·M] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:779` — `fetchNewSince` uses `since_id`, which returns the *newest* 20 posts after the cursor. When more than 20 arrive between polls (busy Federated/Local, or the catch-up poll after a hidden tab at 909–912), everything between the old top and those 20 is skipped with no gap marker, and `pendingNew` merges as if the feed were continuous. → Page forward with `min_id` until caught up (with a cap), or insert a "Load N missing posts" gap row when a since-page comes back full. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1096` — When load-more reaches the end of Local/Federated, `fetchMergedTimeline` returns nothing and then calls `fetchPublicTimelineFallback(type, limit, legacyMaxId)` (`instances.ts:1274-1277`). The cursor here is an object, so `legacyMaxId` is undefined, and the fallback returns the fallback server's *newest* page. For Federated that is a different server, so unrelated posts get appended. `hasMore` stays true because `newStatuses.length > 0` (1143), so the column loops on "Nothing new on that page / Keep looking" forever. The same fallback also fires on guest since-polls (820). → Skip the fallback whenever a cursor or `since` is supplied, and return `[]` so `hasMore` goes false and "All caught up" shows. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:858` — `pollForNew` bails when `!statuses.value.length`, and `mergeIncoming` returns on an empty list (699). An empty column ("Your feed is quiet", a fresh hashtag, a new account's Home) therefore never picks up new posts until a reload. → When the list is empty, poll by re-running `fetchTimeline(true)` quietly instead of bailing. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:837` — Liked, Saved and algorithm columns never poll. On desktop there is also no way to refresh any column: pull-to-refresh is hidden at ≥1024px (2253–2255), the header has no refresh control, and the title only scrolls to the top (612). Only a full page reload brings in new content. → Add a refresh button to the column header (busy state while it runs), plus an `r` key in `useFeedKeyboard`. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:923` — When a mobile column stops being recessed (it becomes the parked feed), the watcher calls `startPolling()`, which waits up to 8s of jitter plus a full 45s interval and runs no catch-up poll. Swiping back to a feed that was parked for 20 minutes shows stale posts for about a minute. → Call `resumePolling()` here, as the `paused` watcher at 931–937 already does. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:803` — In the multi-instance guest path for Local/Federated, the top post's id goes in as `since_id` for `targets[0]` only (823), even when that post came from another instance's id space. The other instances get no cursor and re-download a full page every 45s. → Build per-`_instanceId` since cursors (the newest id per instance, mirroring `seedFeedCursors`) and pass the whole map. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:702` — `mergeIncoming` drops any fresh post whose `createdAt` is ≤ the current top's `createdAt`. That throws away remote posts that federated late, and posts from servers with clock skew in merged feeds, even though `since_id` already guarantees they are new. → Drop the `createdAt` cutoff and rely on identity dedupe, or compare per-instance ids with `compareId`. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:505` — Every prepend or poll re-derives `statusListKey` / `statusIdentity` (each a `new URL()` parse) for the whole list several times: the before/after sets (505, 508), `mergeIncoming` (700–701), `displayRows` (771–773) and `loadMore` (1131). With 400 rows across 8 columns, that is thousands of URL parses every 45s. → Memoize identity per status object in a `WeakMap` inside `statusIdentity.ts`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:684` — Algorithm columns are exempt from the 400-post cap, and ranked recipes keep "every candidate seen this session" in `pool` (`useRankedFeed.ts:59`), so a long-running algorithm column grows without bound. → Cap algorithm lists (trim the tail and let load-more re-scan), and LRU-trim the ranked pool and `served` set. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:214` — `isFlip` depends on `props.focused` on desktop (201–203), and the `watch(isFlip)` scrolls to the top. Focusing *or* unfocusing a column whose saved mode is Flick therefore throws away the reading position both ways. → Reset scroll only on an explicit mode change (`setViewMode`), not when focus toggles; otherwise scroll the previously visible card back into view. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:2020` — The new-posts pill is an in-flow sticky block with `margin: 0.75rem auto`. Showing or hiding it inserts about 44px above the posts. Safari has no scroll anchoring, so the text being read jumps. In Flick mode it adds a non-snapping block at the top of the snap container. → Put the pill in a zero-height sticky wrapper and position it absolutely. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1733` — Every column has its own polite live region (312–323), and all desktop columns keep polling. A board with several busy feeds makes screen readers announce "N new posts in X" from each column about every 45s. → Announce only for the active or focused column, or aggregate into one board-level region in `index.vue`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:998` — A guest Home column throws "Log in or add an instance…". None of `isLoginRequiredError`'s keywords match it (371–379; there's no "sign in" or "requires login"), so the error branch shows "Couldn't load posts" with an alert icon and Retry. The friendly "Home needs a sign-in" block (1775–1786) sits after `v-else-if="error"` and is unreachable. → Don't throw for a guest Home; let the template's sign-in branch render (or add "log in" to the matcher). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1036` — A 422 from a tag or public timeline on an auth-gated host goes through `mapErrorToMessage` (`friendlyError.ts:74-79`) and comes out as "Something in the request wasn't accepted. Check and try again." under "Couldn't load posts" or "Sign in to see this feed". That copy describes a failed save, not a read. → For read paths, map 422 to "This server only shows this timeline to signed-in people." · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:719` — `pendingNew` is silently capped at 400, but the pill (306–310) still says "400 new posts". → Show "400+ new posts" when the cap is hit. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1770` — "Trying again automatically in about 20s" is set once and never counts down, so it reads as frozen. → Tick `autoRetryIn` every second, or say "Retrying shortly…". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:456` — Hashtag (group) columns fall through to the generic "No posts yet — Try another timeline, or find a different server to watch" with an Explore CTA. → Add a `group` branch: "No posts tagged #x yet", with "Browse groups". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:445` — The algorithm empty state ("No matches in X / Try loosening filters") has no action, although the editor is one call away (`algorithmsStore.openEditor(id)`). → Add an "Edit algorithm" button (the `EmptyAction` type needs an `onClick` variant). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:406` — Any guest column that is empty says "You're looking at public posts — no account needed", even when it is showing no posts at all (for example, a quiet Local). → Only use the guest copy as a hint; keep a real empty message. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:418` — A signed-in user's empty Home offers "Explore servers". What they need is people to follow. → Point the CTA at `/explore?tab=people` with the label "Find people to follow". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1719` — Inline compose renders only when `isFirst`. If column 1 is a PanelColumn (Notifications, Messages, Search), no TimelineColumn is first and the desktop board has no compose box at all. → Pass a `isFirstTimeline` prop from `index.vue` (the first index where `isTimelineFeed`). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:612` — The title button is labelled "{feed}. Scroll to top" but does nothing when already at the top, with no feedback. → When at the top with nothing pending, make it a no-op without announcing an action, or have it refresh instead. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1776` — The guest Home state uses a 👋 emoji where every other state uses a `NeoIcon` in `.column-state__icon`. → Use `<NeoIcon name="lock">` like the other states. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1297` — Unmount removes `mobile-chrome-collapsed` from `<html>`, but nothing in `app/` ever adds that class. → Delete the line. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:2484` — Dead CSS: the `.dropdown-*` and `.groups-expand-*` transitions (2484–2508) aren't used by any `<Transition>`, and `.feed-dropdown__groups` (2224–2227) isn't in the template. → Delete them. · mech

### B. Feed menus & feed switching
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/pages/index.vue:1366` — NOT FIXED: `FEED_CATALOG` (`columns.ts:42`) isn't used anywhere outside the store. The feed menu is still hand-copied four times: desk-tabs (`index.vue:1366-1410`), mobile sheet (1494–1603), desktop panel (1963–2124) and the column switcher (`TimelineColumn.vue:1370-1594`). The copies have already drifted on items, icons, titles and gating. → Build one `<FeedMenuItems>` from `FEED_CATALOG` (with sections and gates) and use it in all four places. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* Confirmed: `FEED_CATALOG` is used only to build `FEED_LABELS` in the store, and four hand-written menus exist. This is maintainability debt with no specific user-facing breakage shown. [Impact Medium.]
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1355` — In Tabs density, the Add menu offers only For You, Local, Federated, Liked, Saved and algorithms. There's no Search, Profile, Notifications, Messages or Groups, so you can't add a hashtag column without switching density. → Give it the same items as the other two Add menus (falls out of the item above). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1370` — The "Algorithms" section header (also at 1498, 1972 and `TimelineColumn.vue:1393`) groups Local, Federated, Liked and Saved, none of which are algorithms. → Use a "Timelines" section for the built-in feeds and keep "Algorithms" for recipes only. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:43` — Home is labelled "For You". In the industry that means a ranked feed, and NeoSpace now ships X/Facebook/TikTok-style ranked algorithms, yet this column is chronological from people you follow. → Rename it "Following" or "Home", or rank it for real. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:609` — `updateColumnFeedType` skips the singleton rules that `addColumn` enforces (493–498). Switching a timeline column to Search, Notifications or Messages from its menu (`TimelineColumn.vue:1528`, `1557`, `1572`) creates a duplicate panel. A `group` switch with no tag also saves a column that `normalizeColumns` drops on reload (244). → If a singleton already exists, jump to it (or swap the two), and reject `group`/`algorithm` without a param. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/columns.ts:491` — Home, Local, Federated and the same hashtag can be added any number of times. The add menus (for example `index.vue:1574-1594`) don't show which feeds are already on the board, so duplicates double the polling and look like a bug. → Mark items already on the board ("On board ✓") and have them jump to the existing column. Allow a duplicate only through an explicit option. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:302` — Custom tags must match `/^[a-z0-9_]+$/i`, so #café, #日本語 and #ÜberTag can't be added as columns even though Mastodon supports Unicode hashtags. → Use `/^[\p{L}\p{N}_]+$/u`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1355` — At 8/8 columns every Add button just disappears (`v-if="columnsStore.canAddColumn"` at 1355, 1481 and 1948), with no hint why or that a limit exists. → Keep the button but disable it, labelled "Board full (8/8) — remove a column to add another". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1368` — "For You" is offered to guests in all three Add menus (1368, 1496, 1965), and adding it lands on the error state. Meanwhile the column switcher disables Home for guests (`TimelineColumn.vue:1377`) and hides Liked, Saved, Notifications and Messages (1430, 1442, 1551, 1566). → Gate For You with `hasAuthenticatedInstance` everywhere, and pick one convention (hide or disable) for both menus. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:480` — "Profile" in the column switcher is a `menuitemradio` that actually navigates to `/profile`. The Add menus (`index.vue:1540`, `2044`) add a real Profile column. The same label does two different things. → Decide whether Profile can be a column and make both menus match. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1490` — The column switcher lists only *joined* groups. Featured groups and custom `#tag` (which the Add menu supports, `index.vue:1563-1594`) aren't reachable, so retargeting a column to a new tag means removing it and adding a new one. → Reuse the Add menu's filter and custom-tag row here. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1367` — The menu title is "Add feed" in tabs (1367), "Add Feed" on mobile (1495) and "Add Column" on desktop (1964). The footer says "feeds on this device" (1601) in one and "columns on this device" (2122) in another, and the trigger labels mix "Add feed" and "Add column". → Pick one term with sentence case throughout. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:1412` — The menu item says "Local" while the header and the board tab say "Local (host)" (`feedLabel` 356–358, `index.vue:1378`). → Show the host in the switcher too. · mech

### C. Board page — carousel, focus, tabs, portals (`index.vue`)
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:621` — Leaving focus mode (`toggleColumnFocus`) doesn't scroll the strip back to that column. While focused, the other columns are 1px and absolute, so the scroller's `scrollLeft` clamps to 0. Unfocusing the 6th column drops you at columns 1–3. → After the toggle, `nextTick(() => scrollToColumn(idx, 'auto'))` on desktop. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:945` — The capture-phase wheel handler takes every horizontal-dominant wheel gesture inside the board. A horizontal trackpad swipe over nested horizontal scrollers, such as the linked-accounts strip (`PanelColumn.vue:1323-1334`) or wide media and code in posts, pans the board instead. → Skip when `e.target` has a horizontally scrollable ancestor inside the column that can still scroll in that direction. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1210` — On an account switch, `initialize()` may null `focusedColumnId` (`columns.ts:750-757`). Nothing calls `ensureTabsFocus()` unless the column count changes (1188–1200), so Tabs density is left with no selected tab and no column hidden. → Call `ensureTabsFocus()` at the end of `initialize()`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useBoardNav.ts:79` — On phones, `openBoardFeed` → `ensureFocusedView` saves `focusedColumnId` to localStorage (`columns.ts:529-531`, `545-547`), even though focus is a desktop-only concept (`isColumnHidden` ignores mobile). The next desktop or iPad-landscape session opens with a single focused column for no visible reason. → Don't set or persist shelf focus from mobile navigation; just request the column jump. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/pages/index.vue:991` — Carousel `touchstart` ignores gestures that start on any `a, button, [role="button"]`. Display names, reblog labels, links in post text and the whole action bar are excluded, so on Chrome Android a horizontal swipe that starts on much of a card never switches feeds. → Track the gesture from links and buttons too (exclude only form fields and menus), and swallow the click if the gesture locked to x. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:2417` — The portal slides use `justify-content: center` with `overflow-y: auto` (2421). On landscape phones the Inbox and Activity portals overflow, and the centred overflow makes the title and kicker unreachable at the top. → Use `justify-content: safe center`, or `margin-block: auto` on a wrapper. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:769` — Opening a DM from the Inbox portal goes straight to `/status/:id` without `markRead`, unlike `PanelColumn.vue:399`, so the unread badge and dot stay. → Pass the conversation and `void conversationsStore.markRead(c.id)` when it's unread. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/pages/index.vue:686` — The Activity portal builds its own verb map. It produces "Alice poll ended" and "boosted you", and every other type (`status`, `update`, `follow_request`, quotes, admin) becomes "interacted". `notifLabel` in `utils/notifHelpers.ts:25` already covers all of these. → Use `notifLabel(n.type)` and the same phrasing as the Notifications column. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1874` — Each Activity portal row opens the generic `/notifications` page, not the post or profile it refers to, and rows show no unread state. → Reuse PanelColumn's `openNotification` routing, and mark unread rows with `notificationsStore.isUnread`. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1677` — Profile portal stats print raw integers with fixed plurals: "1 posts", "1234567 followers". → Use the app's compact number formatter and singular/plural labels. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1622` — Every portal shows the kicker "Edge" (1622, 1664, 1748, 1799, 1847, 1901). It's an internal layout term with no meaning to users. → Remove it, or replace it with a hint like "Swipe back for your feeds". · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:135` — Naming drifts between surfaces: the chrome title says "Messages" where the portal h2 says "Inbox" (1801). The Activity portal stands in for Notifications. The communities slide has aria-label "Find communities" (1899) while its heading says "Groups" (1902). → Use one name per destination across the chrome, portals and aria-labels. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1769` — The "#fediverse" chip searches the plain word "fediverse", not the tag. → Open `/groups/fediverse`, or search `#fediverse`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1917` — Portal group chips `router.push('/groups/…')` directly, bypassing `useBoardNav().openGroup` (`useBoardNav.ts:95-98`), which reuses an existing board column for that tag. → Call `openGroup(group.tag)`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1417` — The mobile Flick toggle stays enabled when the parked column is a panel (Notifications, Messages, Search, Profile). It does nothing visible but still persists `viewMode: 'flip'` to storage. → Disable it when `!isTimelineFeed(activeColumn.feedType)`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/index.vue:1421` — The toggle's tooltip says "Feed — scroll a list" while its aria-label says "Flick view". The code and comments call the mode Flip/Flow while the UI says Flick/Feed. → Settle on one user-facing pair and make the tooltip follow `aria-pressed`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·M] `app/composables/useFeedKeyboard.ts:14` — NOT FIXED (left open in the previous audit): keyboard users have no way to move between columns. j/k work only inside the focused column's `role=feed`, and reaching the next column means tabbing through every card and action. → Add board-level `[`/`]` (or Alt+←/→) in `index.vue` to focus the next or previous column's feed root, and park it on mobile. · judgment

### D. Columns store & persistence (`columns.ts`)
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:536` — When the board is full, `ensureFocusedView` (reached from sidebar or drawer nav) removes a panel or the *last* column, which may be the user's Home or a curated tag column. The Undo it offers can never work: the new column has already taken the slot, so `onAction` returns early at 597. → Ask before evicting, or have Undo remove the just-added column and restore the evicted one. Never evict timeline columns automatically. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:722` — Profile sync is disabled, but `initialize()` still *reads* the public `neospace_columns` field. For anyone with that stale field, every reload (unless local is dirty) replaces the layout with the decoded profile version. `generateId()` gives new column ids each time, so Tabs focus, pending jumps and undo ids reset on every load, and the field stays public on the profile. → Stop reading it, or migrate once and offer "Remove old layout field from my profile". · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/columns.ts:345` — Layouts have no cross-tab sync (no `storage` listener). With two tabs open, a reorder in one silently overwrites a column added in the other, because each save writes the whole account bucket. → Listen for `storage` on `neospace_columns_v2` and re-run `initialize()` (or merge) when the active account's bucket changes. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:284` — `parseStoredLayout` returns null for any list longer than 8, even though `normalizeColumns` already truncates (267). That layout is discarded, and `saveToStorage` (360–363) then *deletes* other accounts' oversized layouts on the next save. → Remove the `list.length > MAX_COLUMNS` rejection. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:142` — `deskDensity` and `focusedColumnId` live in one global key (`neospace_desk_layout`) while columns are per account. Focus ids from one account are meaningless in another, and switching back and forth loses the focus. → Store focus per account bucket; keep density global if that's intended. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:583` — The undo toast says "Algorithm removed" or "Profile removed" instead of the recipe name or @handle the user recognises. → Resolve the recipe name through `useAlgorithmsStore().getRecipe`, and use `@profileAcct` for profile peeks. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:460` — In Tabs density, pressing "Show all views" on the active column silently switches density to Roomy and persists that, discarding whether the user came from Packed. → Remember the previous strip density and restore it. · decision
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/columns.ts:625` — Dead API: `peekProfileInColumn` (625), `focusExistingColumn` (453), the `maxColumns` and `isTabsDensity` getters (301, 305), and `useBoardNav().jumpToColumnId` (`useBoardNav.ts:54`) have no callers. Since nothing creates a profile peek anymore, the ~150-line remote-peek UI in `PanelColumn.vue:162-264` and `565-619` is only reachable from old stored layouts. → Wire the peek back up (from avatar taps) or delete it all. · decision

### E. PanelColumn
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:96` — The Notifications column reads `notificationsStore.filteredNotifications`, which applies the global filter *and* sort order from `/notifications`. The Activity portal chips (`index.vue:1863-1871`) set that filter too. After tapping "Mentions" once, the board column shows only mentions, possibly oldest-first, with no indicator. → Give the column its own filter (or show and reset the active filter in the panel toolbar). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:816` — The panel never shows `notificationsStore.error`. When the fetch fails (expired token, 5xx), the list is empty and it says "All caught up." → Show the error with a Retry button before the empty message. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:775` — Post search results show `stripHtml(status.content).slice(0,120)` and ignore `spoilerText` and `sensitive`. Content-warned text is revealed in the results, and it's cut off with no ellipsis. → Show the CW text when one is set, and add "…" on truncation. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:298` — `resolve` is only sent for `@user@host`. Pasting a post or profile URL, the most common way to pull in a remote object, returns nothing. → Also resolve when the query matches `^https?://`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:458` — Panels don't react to an account switch. Search results stay from the old account, and a remote peek keeps `remoteAccount.id` from the old server while `loadRemoteStatuses` (247–248) now calls the new account's client, so "Load more" fails or fetches the wrong person. → Watch `instancesStore.activeAccountId`, re-run the search, and reload the peek. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:143` — "Open full profile" on the own-profile column, with a non-active linked account selected, calls `setActiveAccount(peekId)`. That silently switches the global account and refetches every column, the same side effect the previous audit removed from notification clicks. → Route with `?account=` the way `openNotification` does. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:846` — Display names are interpolated as plain text in notification rows (846), search people (744), search posts (774) and chats (917), and in `index.vue:688`/`1674`. Custom emoji show up as raw `:blobcat:` shortcodes, while cards use `emojify` (`utils/emojify.ts`). → Render names through `emojify` with the account's emojis. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:282` — Search needs at least 2 characters, which blocks meaningful single-character CJK queries. → Allow length 1 when the character is non-Latin, or adjust the hint. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:789` — The notifications Refresh button gives no feedback: no busy state, no spin, no disabled state while `isLoading`. Repeated clicks fire repeated fetches. → Bind `:disabled` and `aria-busy` to `notificationsStore.isLoading` and spin the icon. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:988` — The `.column-drag-handle` (988–991) and `.column-reorder` (1003–1008) rules are scoped to PanelColumn and can't reach ColumnChrome's internal elements. The panel drag handle has no `cursor: grab` or `touch-action: none`, and the reorder arrows lack the flex, gap and margin TimelineColumn applies, so headers don't line up. → Move these rules into `ColumnChrome.vue`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/PanelColumn.vue:1397` — Profile post cards use `border-radius: 4px`, while TimelineColumn uses `var(--neo-radius-md)` (2421) along with `content-visibility: auto`. → Share the same card rule (token radius plus containment). · mech

### F. Column chrome, DnD, composables & utils
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useFeedKeyboard.ts:33` — When focus is on the feed root (for example after Escape, or tabbing in), `j` sets `idx = -1` and focuses card 0. `scrollIntoView` then yanks a reader who had scrolled 50 posts down back to the top. → With no focused card, start from the first card that intersects the scroller's viewport. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/composables/useFeedKeyboard.ts:31` — Only lowercase `j`/`k`/`o` match, so the shortcuts silently stop working with Caps Lock on. → Compare `e.key.toLowerCase()` and ignore when Shift is held. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/ColumnChrome.vue:42` — Icon buttons set both `title` and `aria-label`. The global `.neo-chrome-btn[aria-label]::after` pill (`main.scss:347-351`, "aria-label only (no title) avoids double tooltips") then shows two tooltips on hover. This affects `ColumnChrome.vue:42`, `71` and `84`; `TimelineColumn.vue:1323`, `1602`, `1614`, `1669` and `1687`; and `PanelColumn.vue:514`, `531` and `550`. → Drop the `title` attributes on `.neo-chrome-btn`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useColumnDnd.ts:24` — `dragover` accepts any drag (selected text, links, images from posts, files) and sets `dropEffect='move'`. `drop` then falls back to `text/plain` (35–36), so dragged text is treated as a column id. Every column advertises itself as a drop target for content it can't take. → Only `preventDefault` when `e.dataTransfer.types.includes('application/x-neospace-column')`, and drop the `text/plain` fallback on read. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/ColumnChrome.vue:14` — `isLast` defaults to `true`, while both parents default it to `false`. Any caller that omits the prop gets a permanently disabled "Move right" and a dead ArrowRight. → Default it to `false`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useHorizontalRail.ts:48` — The ResizeObserver watches only the scroller's own box. Items appended later (load more, async trending) change `scrollWidth` without resizing the box, so the next arrow never appears. → Also observe the rail's children (or use a MutationObserver), and call `update()` when items change. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useHorizontalRail.ts:21` — In RTL `scrollLeft` is ≤ 0, so `canScrollBack` is always false and `canScrollForward` is wrong. → Use `Math.abs(el.scrollLeft)` and flip the `scrollBy` sign when `getComputedStyle(el).direction === 'rtl'`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/linkHeader.ts:28` — `new URL(match[1])` throws on a relative `Link` target, which some proxies and servers send. The catch returns null and pagination silently stops after page 1. → Resolve against a base: `new URL(match[1], 'https://x.invalid')`. Only the query is read. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·M] `app/utils/statusIdentity.ts:108` — Reblogs collapse only when they are *consecutive*. The same post boosted by three followees a few posts apart still repeats in Home, which is the complaint the earlier "A and 2 others reposted" item was meant to fix. → Collapse within a sliding window (say 20 rows), keeping the first occurrence's position. · judgment

---

## Posts & compose

### RealPostCard — identity & cross-server correctness
- [ ] [SEC·H·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:599` — `handleMute`/`handleBlock` (624) and `handleReport` (640) pass `displayStatus.account.acct`. That value is *relative to the server that served the post*: a local user on a foreign server has a bare `alice`. `status.ts:404/410/435` then resolves `alice` on **your** server, so a different person gets muted, blocked or reported. → Pass the full `@user@host` (the `accountHandle` computed already has it, or derive the host from `_instanceUrl`). Refuse to act when the host can't be determined. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [SEC·H·S] `app/stores/status.ts:347` — When `accounts.lookup` 404s (common for remote accounts your server hasn't seen), `resolveAccount` falls back to `accounts.search.list({ q, resolve: true, limit: 1 })` and returns `results[0].id` unverified. A fuzzy match ("alice2", "Alice Cooper") gets muted, blocked or reported. → Accept the hit only if `results[0].acct` (with the local domain normalized) equals the requested acct, case-insensitive. Otherwise throw. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:449` — The reply prefill is `@${displayStatus.account.acct}` (462). For posts fetched from another server's API (multi-account, Edward, explore), `acct` is relative to that server, so the reply mentions and notifies a same-named local user on your server, and the real author never sees it. → Build the mention from the full handle (acct@host of `account.url`/`_instanceUrl`), the same way `accountHandle` does. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:142` — `openProfile` (called from the avatar at 806, the name via PostHeader at 146, and the reblogger at 788) pushes `/profile?user=<relative acct>`. Tapping the author of a foreign-served post opens a different user, or "not found". → Pass the full handle (reuse `accountHandle.value` minus the leading `@`). · mech ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:332` — `isOwnPost` matches on `account.id === user.id || account.acct === user.acct` across servers. A foreign-served post by a local "michael" there, or an id collision, counts as yours, which hides Mute/Block/Report for a stranger. → Compare full acct@host (or `account.url` to `user.url`), not the relative acct or a cross-server id. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] `app/composables/usePostActions.ts:157` — The last-resort `fetchStatus(status.id)` treats any local status with that id as the same post. A foreign snowflake/ULID that happens to exist locally gets liked, boosted or bookmarked on the wrong post. The same pattern is in `status.ts:323` (`resolveReplyId`), so a reply can attach to an unrelated post. → Accept the fallback only when `fetched.uri === status.uri`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/status.ts:268` — `resolveStatusOnce` returns `results.statuses[0]` without checking it is the requested post. Akkoma/Pleroma full-text search can return a post that merely *contains* the URL, and actions then land on it. → Match the hit's `uri`/`url` against the candidate before caching and returning. · mech

### RealPostCard — content, CW, polls, media
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:970` — `PreviewCard` sits outside the `<details>` CW gate, and so does the poll (1036). A CW'd post shows its link thumbnail, title, description and poll options in the clear, and `sensitive` link-card images aren't blurred either. → Render card and poll inside the CW body (or hide them while `hasSpoiler && !cwOpen`), and blur `card.image` when `sensitive`. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1036` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: the poll is still read-only. There's no vote form or `polls.$select(id).votes.create` anywhere in the app, no expiry time (`expiresAt`), no "you voted" (`voted`/`ownVotes`), and no multiple-choice hint. → Add radio/checkbox voting for open, unvoted polls, a "Closes in 3h" line, an own-vote check, and a refresh button. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:885` — The ⋯ menu has no Delete, Edit, "Delete & redraft", Pin or "Mute conversation" for your own posts (`deleteStatus` exists in the store but only DMs use it). You can't remove a post you just made. → Add an own-post section with confirm-delete (remove the card on success) and edit via the composer. · decision ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:151` — `status.filtered` is never read. Filters created with action "warn" in Settings (`settings.ts:972`) do nothing, so filtered keywords show in full. → When `filtered?.some(f => f.filter.filterAction === 'warn')`, collapse the card to "Filtered: <title> · Show anyway". · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:933` — Incoming quote posts (`status.quote` on Mastodon 4.5+, `quote`/`reblog`-style on Misskey/Akkoma) are never rendered. Readers see only a "RE: link" or nothing. The old audit item 278 said quotes would render. → Add a nested, non-interactive quote card under the content, respecting the quoted post's CW. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:841` — There's no "Edited" marker (`editedAt` isn't referenced anywhere in app/) and no edit history, so silently changed posts look original. → Show "· edited" next to the time, with a title of the edit date, linking to `/statuses/:id/history`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:930` — There's no Translate action for posts in other languages, even though `status.language` is known and Mastodon exposes `POST /statuses/:id/translate` (gated by `configuration.translation.enabled`). → Add "Translate" to the menu (or under content) when the language differs from the UI locale and the server supports it. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:709` — The hashtag/mention interception also matches **any** link whose href contains `/tags/` or `/@x` (718). Blog tag pages, `youtube.com/@channel` and `medium.com/@author/post` get routed to `/groups/…` or a bogus `/profile?user=channel@www.youtube.com` instead of opening the link. → Intercept only anchors with the `mention`/`hashtag` class (or ones that match `status.mentions`/`status.tags` URLs). · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:713` — `decodeURIComponent(fromHref || fromText)` (and 723) throws `URIError` on a malformed `%` in remote HTML, so the click does nothing and an uncaught error is logged. → Wrap it in try/catch and fall back to the raw text. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:984` — Attachments of type `unknown` (remote media the server failed to fetch) match no branch and disappear silently. Images with `url`/`previewUrl` null but a `remoteUrl` render an empty `src`. → Add a fallback tile "Media unavailable · Open original" linking `remoteUrl`, and use `remoteUrl` as the last src fallback. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:992` — There's no `@error` on media images. A 404 or expired cached image shows a broken-image glyph in a reserved box. → On error, swap in a muted "Image failed to load · Retry" placeholder (or the blurhash). · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1011` — Videos and gifv get no `aspect-ratio` (only images use `mediaAspectRatio`). With `preload="none"` the poster loads late and shifts the feed. → Apply `:style="{ aspectRatio: mediaAspectRatio(media) }"` to `<video>` as well. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1844` — Two columns kick in at 2+ items, so 3 attachments leave an empty bottom-right cell. Mixed aspect ratios also give a ragged grid. → For 3 items span the first item across both rows (Mastodon layout), and give grid cells a fixed ratio with `object-fit: cover`. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:996` — `object-fit: cover` with `max-height: 350px` (1958) center-crops tall images and ignores `meta.focus`, so faces and screenshot headers get cut off. → Set `object-position` from `meta.focus` (x,y ∈ [-1,1] → %), defaulting to top for very tall images. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1015` — gifv loops render with full `controls` (scrubber, volume for a silent clip), unlike every other client, which treats them as images. → Drop `controls` for gifv, and toggle play/pause on tap and when reduced motion is on. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:742` — The lightbox only takes `type === 'image'`. Video and gifv can't be enlarged, though old audit item 271 called for it. → Include video/gifv items and render `<video>` in NeoLightbox for them. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1057` — For multiple-choice polls `pollDenominator` is `votersCount`, but the label says "N votes". "Open" also gives no end time. → Say "N people" when `poll.multiple`, and append the expiry ("Closes in 2h", or "Closed 3d ago"). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:939` — The CW summary (and the media gate label at 980), the reblog label (198) and poll option titles (1047) print raw `:shortcode:` instead of custom emoji. → Run them through `emojify` (with `poll.emojis`/`account.emojis`) plus the existing sanitizer. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:980` — The media gate repeats the CW text shown just above, and says "Tap to reveal" on desktop. After a reveal there's no way to hide the media again. → Use "Show media" / "Hide media" wording that works for both pointer types, and add an eye toggle once revealed. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:940` — CW'd content isn't wired to `contentEl`, so it's never clamped. A long CW post fills the screen once opened, while normal posts clamp. → Apply the same clamp and "See more" inside the details body. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:367` — `formatNumber` shows `1.0K` and `1000.0K` (for 999,950), has no billions, and isn't locale-aware. → Use `Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { notation: 'compact', maximumFractionDigits: 1 })`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1840` — `.status-media` clips at 4px while each image is 8px (`--neo-radius-md`, 1960) and the CW tile is 12px (1859). Outer and inner corners don't match. → Use one radius token for the media container and its children. · mech

### RealPostCard — actions, menu, a11y
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:399` — Repost is offered on followers-only and direct posts. Mastodon rejects those, so you get an optimistic green state, then a rollback and an error toast. → Disable or hide boost when `visibility` is `private` (unless it's your own post) or `direct`, with title "Can't repost followers-only posts". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:566` — "Quote" appears in the Share and Repost sheets (577) for private and direct posts, and ignores Mastodon 4.5's `quoteApproval` policy. You can publicly quote a followers-only post, or get a 422. → Offer quote only for public/unlisted posts whose quote policy allows you. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:462` — A reply mentions only the author. Other participants (`status.mentions`) drop out of the thread, unlike the thread page (`status/[id].vue:170`), and replying to your own post prefills `@yourself`. → Prefill the author plus `mentions`, minus yourself, de-duplicated (reply-all, as on the thread page). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:597` — Mute and block give no success feedback, and the person's posts stay in every open column until refresh, so it looks like nothing happened. → Show "Muted @x · Undo", and emit an event so columns drop that account's rows. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1069` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: Reply still uses `:disabled="isOpeningReply"`. While the cross-server resolve runs, focus drops to `<body>` and the screen reader loses its place. (Like, Boost and Bookmark were moved to `aria-disabled`.) → Use `aria-disabled` plus the existing guard. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:483` — `openQuoteComposer` has no in-flight guard or try/catch. A double tap opens two resolves (the second remounts the sheet), and a thrown resolve is an unhandled rejection with no toast. → Add an `isOpeningQuote` flag and a failure toast like the reply path. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1102` — The same toggle is labelled "Bookmark" in the action bar and "Save"/"Unsave" in the ⋯ menu (868), so it's duplicated with two names. → Pick one term (Mastodon says "Bookmark") and drop the menu copy, or keep the menu item labelled the same. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:692` — The card's keydown treats Space like Enter. With a card focused, Space opens the thread instead of scrolling the page. → Open on Enter only. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:418` — `stripHtml(s.content).slice(0, 280)` (and `slice(0, 200)` for share at 536) can cut an emoji surrogate pair in half, leaving a "�" in the reply context or shared text. → Use `clipGraphemes` from `stripHtml.ts`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:536` — Share via… sends the CW-hidden body text and ignores `spoilerText`. → Share the CW text (or just the URL) when `hasSpoiler`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:517` — When the clipboard is unavailable, the fallback is a blocking `window.prompt`, which looks broken in an installed PWA. → Show a toast with a selectable read-only field, or the share sheet. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:645` — Report resolves `getActionContext()` (possibly via another linked account's client), then `reportStatus` ignores that id and re-resolves `statusUrl` (`status.ts:429`). That's two network round-trips and a mismatched account. → Pass the resolved local id and skip the second resolve when the action client is the active one. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:2067` — `.status-action { transition: all 0.15s }` animates every property (layout included) on hundreds of buttons. → Transition only `background-color, color, transform`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:2171` — `.status-action--replying` (2171-2182) and `.menu-fade-*` (1744-1753) are dead CSS: nothing applies those classes, and NeoMenu owns its transition. The "Boosted = success green" comment at 2147 also sits above the bookmark rule. → Delete them and fix the comment. · mech

### usePostActions & stores/status.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/status.ts:84` — A fresh Idempotency-Key is minted per `postStatus` call, so tapping Post again after a timeout or lost response (the realistic duplicate case) sends a new key and creates a duplicate. → Generate the key once per compose session in RealComposeBox, pass it in, and rotate it only after success or an edit. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/usePostActions.ts:238` — `toggleBoost` doesn't check `visibility` and always reposts publicly. It also can't do Mastodon's boost visibility (`reblog({ visibility })`). → Guard private/direct (see RealPostCard:399), and optionally offer "Repost to followers only". · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/status.ts:177` — Every abort, including the per-file timeout, becomes "Upload timed out — tap the image to retry". The overlay then appends " · retry" (`RealComposeBox.vue:1133`), giving "…tap the image to retry · retry", and says "tap" on desktop. → Return a short "Timed out", and let the overlay add the single "Retry" action. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/status.ts:198` — `deleteStatus` errors read "Couldn't delete message", which is wrong once posts use it. → Say "Couldn't delete (status)". · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/status.ts:454` — The `useTimelineStore` alias has no importers. → Delete it. · mech

### RealComposeBox
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:651` — A scheduled post doesn't emit `posted`. The form silently resets with only an sr-only announcement, and in ComposeSheet the sheet stays open and empty, so sighted users can't tell it worked. → Toast "Scheduled for {date} · View scheduled" and emit a `scheduled` event that closes the sheet. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:386` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: once within 10% of the limit, `counterAnnounce` changes on every keystroke ("49 characters left", "48 …"), so the polite region chatters continuously. → Announce only when crossing thresholds (e.g. 50, 20, 10, 0, over) and on over-limit changes. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1461` — The compact reply dock has **no focus indicator**. `.compose--compact.compose--expanded { box-shadow: none }` (same specificity, later) cancels the `:focus-within` ring, and `.compose-input:focus { outline: none }` (1410) beats the global `textarea:focus-visible`. → Give the expanded compact shell its own `:focus-within` border/ring. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:2077` — Visibility select, schedule input (2102) and alt textarea (1857) set `outline: none` on `:focus` and just tint a 1px border, which overrides the global ring. Focus is nearly invisible. → Remove those `outline: none` rules, or add a 2px ring on `:focus-visible`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:403` — The language picker is 12 hard-coded languages with English names. There's no "Automatic/none" option and no Arabic, Hebrew, Ukrainian, Hindi, Turkish, etc. Defaulting to `navigator.language` mis-tags posts written in other languages. → Build the list from `/api/v1/instance/languages` with `Intl.DisplayNames` (native names), put recent picks first, and add an "Auto" option that omits `language`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:348` — `canPost` counts the prefilled "@handle " as text, so a reply consisting only of the mention can be posted (and the header Post button is enabled immediately). → Use `hasOwnContent` (or media/quote) instead of `hasText` when `initialText` is set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:553` — The settings watcher applies `defaultVisibility` whenever the box is empty. In a sheet reply (content empty when `mentionAccts` is used) or DM-locked flow, a settings sync can flip a followers-only reply back to Public. → Skip when `props.initialVisibility` is set, or after the user has touched the select. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:373` — `canSwitchPostingAccount` blocks switching for media and replies but not for a quote. `quoteContext.id` was resolved on the old server, so the post 404s and falls back to a URL-only quote, or quotes the wrong post. → Also block on `props.quoteContext?.id` (or re-resolve on switch). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:603` — The quote URL is always appended to the body, even when `quotedStatusId` succeeds. Native-quote servers show the embedded quote *and* a dangling link that eats 23 characters. → Append the URL only on the fallback path (or when the server lacks native quotes), and adjust `characterCount`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:976` — Reply and quote composers hide the header, so with several accounts there's no indication which account is replying (and no switch). → Show a compact "Replying as @me@host" line in reply/quote mode. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:985` — The account control cycles blindly to the "next" account. When disabled (media attached) it keeps `title="Switch account"` with no reason. → Open the account switcher menu, and set the title to "Remove attachments to switch accounts" when disabled. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1407` — The compact dock relies on `field-sizing: content` to grow. Safari (iOS, the dock's main target) and Firefox don't support it, so multi-line replies stay one row tall and scroll inside a 1-line box. → Add a JS autosize fallback (set height from `scrollHeight` on input) when `CSS.supports('field-sizing','content')` is false. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1999` — The toolbar is `overflow-x: auto` with the scrollbar hidden. On phones the schedule/language controls are pushed off-screen with no hint they exist. → Wrap to a second row, or move schedule and language into a "⋯ More" popover. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1819` — In the composer a red "ALT" badge means *missing* alt. On post cards an "ALT" badge means *has* alt (`RealPostCard.vue:1004`). Same label, opposite meaning. → Label the composer badge "No ALT" (or use a warning icon) and keep "ALT" for present alt text. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:584` — The missing-alt confirm says "images" for video and audio. - [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1159` — The alt placeholder "Describe this image…", the remove label "Remove image N" (1138) and the tool "Add photo" (1196) are wrong for video and audio attachments. → Use type-aware copy ("Describe this video for people who can't see it", "Remove attachment N", "Add media"). · mech

- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1249` — The schedule tooltip reads "Schedule — place this cell in time" (internal jargon). Its clear button (2117) is 1.5rem ≈ 22.5px, under the 24px target. → Title it "Schedule for later", and make the clear button ≥24px with a NeoIcon x. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:2107` — `:not(:placeholder-shown)` always matches a `datetime-local` with no placeholder, so the schedule field always has the "active" accent border even when empty. → Bind a `compose-schedule-input--set` class from `hasSchedule`. · mech
