After the composer has been open a while, the picker allows times under 5 minutes away, which the store then rejects. → Recompute on focus of the input, or tick with a minute timer. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:1133` — The error overlay prints the full server error in 0.6875rem text inside a 96px square (long 422 messages clip), and offers "retry" even for non-retryable errors such as unsupported type or size. → Show a short label ("Failed") with the full reason in `title`/alt row, and hide Retry for 4xx validation errors. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/components/RealComposeBox.vue:942` — `syncMentions` runs on both `@input` and `@keyup` (944) for every keystroke, firing two search calls (debounced) and two placement measures. → Drop the keyup path except for caret-only keys (Home/End/arrows without the open list). · mech

### ComposeSheet & stores/composeSheet.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:24` — `requestClose` (Escape, backdrop, Cancel) still works while `isPosting`. The sheet asks "Discard this draft?" for a post already in flight, and closing unmounts the box so `onPosted` (reply count, "Reply posted" toast, timeline insert) never runs. → Ignore close requests while `composeRef.isPosting`, or keep the box alive until the request settles. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:184` — The reply context shows `stripHtml(content)` of a CW'd post with the warning omitted (`contextFromStatus`, `RealPostCard.vue:418`), so replying reveals the hidden text. → Carry `spoilerText` in `ComposeContextPost`, and show "CW: …" collapsed behind a toggle. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:28` — The dirty-close confirm offers only Discard or stay, even though `useDraft` already autosaves. Users who want to come back later must discard. → Add "Keep draft" (close without `discardDraft()`), and make it the default for non-destructive closes. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:187` — "Show more" appears when `text.length > 120` while the clamp is 3 lines, and the text was already cut to 280 characters, so "Show more" never shows the whole post. → Detect overflow (`scrollHeight > clientHeight`), and pass the full text or link to the thread. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/composeSheet.ts:35` — `onPosted` callbacks are stored in Pinia state, where devtools snapshots and HMR serialize them and they leak closures over unmounted cards. → Keep the callback in a module-level variable (or a `markRaw` holder) outside reactive state. · mech

### useDraft
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useDraft.ts:84` — Drafts restore silently, with no "Draft restored · Discard" notice, and are keyed without the account. Text typed as account A reappears when composing as B, and `reply:<id>` can collide across servers. → Prefix keys with the active account id and show a dismissible "Restored draft" chip with Clear. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/useDraft.ts:69` — `neospace_draft:reply:*` / `quote:*` / `dm:*` keys never expire. Every abandoned reply leaves a key in localStorage forever. → Store `savedAt` and prune drafts older than ~14 days (or keep the newest 50) on startup. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useDraft.ts:60` — Drafts over 8KB are silently truncated. On 5k–10k-character servers a long draft comes back cut short, and media, schedule, language, sensitive and group tag aren't persisted at all. → Raise the cap (or warn on truncation), and persist the small scalar fields. · judgment

### useComposeMedia, prepareComposeImage, composeConstants
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useComposeMedia.ts:247` — One size cap is used for every file, and it's `max(imageSizeLimit, videoSizeLimit)` (`instances.ts:339`). A 30MB PNG passes the client check on a 16MB-image/99MB-video server and fails after a long upload. → Check images against `imageSizeLimit` (after `prepareComposeImage`) and videos against `videoSizeLimit`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/composeConstants.ts:12` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old item 474): HEIC/HEIF and AVIF are still rejected, which is common for drag-drop from macOS Photos/Finder and Android. Audio (mp3/ogg/m4a/wav/flac, all supported by Mastodon) can't be attached at all. → Add `image/heic|heif|avif` and `audio/*` types to both the regex and the `accept` list (16), passing HEIC through unconverted where `createImageBitmap` can't decode it. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/composables/useComposeMedia.ts:278` — Every image is downscaled sequentially *before* any attachment is shown or uploaded. Picking 4 large photos shows nothing for several seconds, then all appear at once. → Push a "Preparing…" item immediately and start each upload as soon as its own prep finishes. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useComposeMedia.ts:310` — The rejected-files toast waits on `Promise.all(uploads)`, so "File too large" for one file appears only after the other files finish uploading (up to 2 minutes). → Show the rejection toast before awaiting the uploads. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/composables/useComposeMedia.ts:363` — An alt-text save failure toasts "tap to retry" with no action and no per-item state. `flushAltDescriptions` (384) swallows failures via `allSettled`, so the post goes out with no description and the user isn't told. → Mark the attachment `altError`, give the toast a Retry action, and block or confirm posting while alt PUTs are failing. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useComposeMedia.ts:429` — When the clipboard has an image plus `text/plain` (copying from Docs, Notes or some browsers' "Copy image"), the image is silently dropped. The old fix moved the loss from text to image. → Attach the files *and* let the text paste (no `preventDefault`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/composeConstants.ts:9` — The upload timeout is capped at 120s (`uploadTimeoutForBytes`, 34). A 99MB video needs about 0.8MB/s sustained, so typical mobile uploads abort near the end with "timed out". → Remove the hard cap while bytes are flowing (XHR progress), or scale to a ~0.1MB/s floor. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/prepareComposeImage.ts:96` — Only `image/gif` is passed through. Animated WebP and APNG above the recompress threshold go through `createImageBitmap`, which keeps only the first frame, so the animation is silently lost. → Detect animation (`ANIM`/`acTL` chunk sniff, or `ImageDecoder` `frameCount > 1`) and pass those files through. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/utils/prepareComposeImage.ts:133` — A single-step `drawImage` with the default `imageSmoothingQuality` ('low') moiré-aliases 4032→1600 downsizes (text and fabric shimmer). → Set `ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'` (or step down by halves). · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/utils/prepareComposeImage.ts:106` — `pngHasAlpha` (canvas plus `getImageData`) runs before the "no work needed" early return at 109, wasting a readback on every small PNG. → Move the alpha probe after the passthrough checks. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/composeConstants.ts:6` — `DEFAULT_MAX_ATTACHMENTS`, `DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES` (7) and `COMPOSE_MAX_IMAGE_DIMENSION` (19) are unused, while `instances.ts:330-334` hard-codes the same 4 / 40MB. → Import the constants in `composeMediaLimits` and delete the dead export. · mech

### PreviewCard & peertube.ts
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] `app/components/PreviewCard.vue:74` — The PeerTube `<iframe>` has no `sandbox`. Its src can come from remote-controlled `card.html` whenever `providerName` contains "peertube" (`peertube.ts:79`), and CSP `frame-src https:` allows any origin. A hostile page gets full script plus popups and top navigation on user activation. → Add `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-presentation allow-popups"`, and accept only URLs normalized by `peerTubeEmbedUrl`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/peertube.ts:24` — Detection is path-only. Any site with `/w/<x>` (e.g. `wikipedia.org/w/index.php`) or `/videos/watch/<id>` (20) becomes a "video" card that iframes `/videos/embed/…`, a 404 page. → Require `card.type === 'video'` *and* (`providerName` ~ peertube or `html` iframe on the same origin) before treating a URL as PeerTube. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/PreviewCard.vue:104` — Card images always have `alt=""` and ignore Mastodon 4.3's `card.imageDescription`. There's no blurhash or `@error` fallback, so a failed OG image leaves a gray box. → Use `imageDescription` when present, and hide the media box on error. · mech

- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/NeoLightbox.vue:174` — `touch-action: pinch-zoom` on the root blocks one-finger panning for descendants, so long alt text in the caption (max 12dvh, `overflow-y: auto`, 239) can't be scrolled on phones. → Set `touch-action: pan-y` on `.neo-lightbox__alt`, and ignore drags that start inside it. · mech

Arrowing to rows 5–8 highlights options that stay scrolled out of view. → `scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` the active option when `activeIndex` changes. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeAutocomplete.vue:32` — With zero results the popup just vanishes: there's no "No accounts match @foo" state (and nothing is announced). → Render a non-option status row when the search finishes empty. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeAutocomplete.vue:60` — Suggestion names show raw `:shortcode:` text for custom-emoji names. → Render `sanitizeDisplayName(emojify(...))`, like PostHeader. · mech

### ComposeGroupPicker
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ComposeGroupPicker.vue:76` — `queryTag` allows only `[a-z0-9_]`, so "Use #…" never appears for #café, #日本語, #Ελλάδα, though Mastodon supports Unicode hashtags. The same ASCII-only rule is in `hashtag.ts:4`, which also rejects leading digits (#2024election). → Use `/^[\p{L}\p{N}_]*[\p{L}_][\p{L}\p{N}_]*$/u`. · mech

- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeGroupPicker.vue:258` — There's no "No matches" state. A query with zero results (or a non-ASCII query) leaves only the hint text, which reads as broken. → Add "No groups match "{q}"" with the Use-#tag row when valid. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeGroupPicker.vue:227` — Enter in the search field (`enterkeyhint="search"`) does nothing. → On Enter, pick the "Use #query" row or the first result. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ComposeGroupPicker.vue:104` — The button says "Use #{{ queryTag }}" (lower-cased at 74), but `useQueryAsTag` picks the raw query, so "Rust" posts `#Rust` while the UI promised `#rust`. → Pick `queryTag.value`, or display the raw form. · mech

- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/ComposeGroupPicker.vue:477` — About 60 lines of dead CSS (`.group-pick-sheet`, `__backdrop`, `__panel`, enter/leave transitions at 684-704) are left from before the NeoSheet migration. → Delete them. · mech

### PostActionSheet
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/PostActionSheet.vue:33` — The `<Transition>` never runs. RealPostCard mounts the sheet with `v-if="shareOpen"` (`RealPostCard.vue:1146`) while `open` is already true (no `appear`), then unmounts it on close, so there's no slide-in or fade-out. The card's own `<Teleport>` (1144) is also redundant. → Mount unconditionally with `appear`, or add `appear` and let the sheet drive the leave before the parent drops it. · judgment

### Text utils (stripHtml, sanitizeHtml, emojify, mastodonLength, hashtag, useComposedStatus)
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/stripHtml.ts:13` — `doc.body.textContent` joins `<br>` and `<p>` boundaries with no whitespace: "line one<br>line two" becomes "line oneline two". This garbles reply context, quote previews, share text and conversation previews. → Before reading `textContent`, replace `<br>` with "\n" and append "\n" after block elements (or walk nodes). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/mastodonLength.ts:6` — URL weight is hard-coded to 23. Servers that report a different `configuration.statuses.charactersReservedPerUrl`, or count URLs in full (Pleroma/Akkoma/GoToSocial), get a counter that under-counts, so posts shown as valid are rejected. → Take the per-URL weight from instance config (and treat "absent" as full length on non-Mastodon servers). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/mastodonLength.ts:10` — The URL token stops at `(`, so `https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foo_(bar)` counts 23 + 5 instead of 23 and the counter overstates. → Allow balanced parentheses in the URL pattern (as twitter-text does). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/utils/emojify.ts:57` — It always uses the animated `url` and ignores `staticUrl`, so animated custom emoji loop in names and content even under `prefers-reduced-motion`. → Use `staticUrl` when reduced motion is on (or by user setting) and animate on hover. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/emojify.ts:45` — Shortcodes are folded to lower case, so `:Blobcat:` and `:blobcat:` (distinct emoji on many servers) render the same image. → Key the map case-sensitively. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/sanitizeHtml.ts:8` — The allowlist drops `sub`, `sup`, `ruby`, `rt` and `rp`, which Mastodon allows. Japanese furigana collapse into run-on text ("漢字かんじ") and x² becomes "x2". `ol[start|reversed]` and `li[value]` (15) are also stripped, so lists renumber from 1. → Add those tags and attributes. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/sanitizeHtml.ts:116` — `NAME_ATTR.filter((a) => a !== 'class')` is dead, because `purifyWithSafeClasses` re-adds `'class'` (63). → Drop the filter (or `class` from `NAME_ATTR`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/hashtag.ts:25` — `tagHistorySummary` prints "1 posts · last 7d" and non-localized counts. "people" is a sum of daily unique accounts over 7 days, so it counts the same person repeatedly. → Pluralize, use `Intl.NumberFormat`, and label it "accounts (daily sum)" or use the last 2 days like Mastodon. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useComposedStatus.ts:13` — One throwing handler aborts the loop, so later columns never receive the freshly posted status. → Wrap each handler call in try/catch. · mech

## Edward (lean-back 3D discovery mode)

### Entry / exit / shell
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:395` — `watchDeckShown` includes `!recessed`, so going recessed unmounts the mobile deck (`:1066`), the desktop rail (`:1202`) and the peek (`:1442`). Those are the only components with a `composeSheet.open` watcher that clears `recessed` (`EdwardWatchActions.vue:230-235`, `EdwardPostModal.vue:265-270`). Cancelling the reply sheet therefore leaves Edward at opacity 0.35 with `pointer-events:none`. `onKey`/`onEscapeKey` bail out while recessed (`:630`, `:717`), and its focus trap keeps pulling focus into the dead layer. The user can't get out without a reload. → Move the `composeSheet.open → setRecessed(false)` watcher into `EdwardMode.vue`, which is always mounted, or into the store. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* The bug is real: being recessed unmounts the only components that watch `composeSheet.open`, and `onPosted` is the only other path that calls `setRecessed(false)`, so cancelling the sheet leaves Edward at 0.35 opacity with `pointer-events:none` and its shortcuts dead. But the user isn't hard-trapped: the header Exit button (`:898`) stays mounted and the focus trap still holds Tab, so Tab+Enter on Exit works. Mouse and touch clicks also pass through to the shell underneath. [Corrected: "Cancelling a reply or yap from Edward leaves it recessed and dim. Mouse and touch can't reach it, and its shortcuts are dead until the user tabs to Exit or reloads." Impact Medium-High. Fix unchanged: put the watcher in EdwardMode or the store.]
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/layouts/default.vue:26-28` — `defineAsyncComponent(() => import(EdwardMode.vue))` has no `errorComponent`/`onError`. If the chunk fails (offline, or a deploy changed chunk hashes), `edwardActive` stays true and nothing renders. The waffle then shows Edward as "current" and the first tap only turns it off. → Add an `onError` that calls `useEdwardStore().exit()` and shows a toast ("Couldn't load Edward — check your connection"). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/edward.ts:302-316` — `enter()` doesn't push a history entry, so Android back or the browser Back button navigates the page underneath while Edward stays on top. → Push a `?edward=1` state (or use `history.pushState`) on enter and close on `popstate`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/SuiteMenu.vue:28-32` — The only way in is the bottom-right waffle, which is hidden while compose is open (`layouts/default.vue:252`). There's no keyboard shortcut, command-palette entry or route, and the blurb "stream · balls" doesn't explain the feature. → Add a shortcut or palette entry and better blurb copy (e.g. "lean back · watch the fediverse float by"). · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1407-1412` — Gesture help only exists in the crumb hint, which is hidden on touch (`:2382-2384`), and in `__watch-hint` (`:1196`), which is never visible (see Deck). Touch users get no first-run explanation that edges scroll, the middle orbits, pinch zooms and tapping opens a post. → Show a dismissible first-run coach card, remembered in `neospace_edward_prefs_v1`. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/components/TimelineColumn.vue:845-849` — `canPoll()` doesn't check `edwardActive`, so every column under the fullscreen Edward overlay keeps polling. That spends the same ~300 req/5 min budget Edward's firehose depends on, and drains battery. → Add `!edwardActive.value` to `canPoll` (import from `~/utils/edwardShell`). · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/stores/edward.ts:314` — `document.body.classList.add('edward-active')` (also removed at `:341` and `EdwardMode.vue:792`): no stylesheet or script ever reads `edward-active`, so it's dead. → Remove it, or use it for something real (e.g. `body.edward-active #main-content { visibility:hidden }` to skip painting the shell). · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:752-753` — Sets `document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'` manually even though `useFocusTrap` (`:744`) already takes `useScrollLock`. That's two competing locks, and `prevOverflow` can restore a stale value if another sheet changed it meanwhile. → Drop the manual overflow juggling. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:766` — `setTimeout(nextQuip, 2200)` isn't tracked or cleared on unmount. Exiting within 2.2 s runs `nextQuip` after teardown and starts a fresh `typeTimer` interval that `onUnmounted` has already missed. → Keep the handle and clear it in `onUnmounted`. · mech

### Stream, polling & rate limits (`useEdwardStream.ts`)
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:474-507` — When the stage is empty, `poll()` re-seeds, and the `finally` block skips backoff (`if (!reseeding)`). `seed()` only bumps `failStreak` on a 429 with an empty stage (`:447-449`). So offline, DNS or 5xx failures re-seed every 12 s forever. Each seed hits every server's federated and local timelines, plus home, 3 tags, trends and the guest fallback. → Increment `failStreak` in `seed()`'s catch (and when it returns an empty stage), and let `nextDelay()` apply to re-seeds too. · mech
  - *Verifier:* Confirmed: offline, DNS or 5xx failures throw out of `fetchFromTargets` into `seed()`'s catch, which never bumps `failStreak`, and `poll`'s finally skips backoff while re-seeding. So re-seeds repeat every 12 s. It only runs while Edward is active and the tab is visible, and a 429 with an empty stage does back off, which limits the damage. [Impact Medium, not High. Fix unchanged.]
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:453-454` — When the firehose fails, the error banner shows the raw `errors[0]`, e.g. "mastodon.social: Failed to fetch" or "x.y requires login". → Map it through `friendlyError` (already imported for `httpStatusFrom`) to plain copy like "Couldn't reach your servers — retrying soon", with the server named. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:99-104` — The shedding loop says "shed the oldest" but `backpack[src].pop()` removes the newest batch (polls push newer posts at the end). Meanwhile `takeNext` `shift()`s the oldest first (`:116`). Once the backpack is full the stage replays stale posts and throws away the freshest. → Shed with `shift()`, or keep each source newest-first and release from the front. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:133-144` — `drip()` keeps merging into the store every 1.4–2.6 s while the tab is hidden. Each merge re-runs the explore pipeline and `syncBallsFromStore`, building new 512² textures, even though the canvas has paused its rAF. → Skip `mergeNewer` while `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'`, as `poll()` already does. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:425-427` — `seed()` fires `void loadAffinity()`, which awaits `groups.fetchFollowedTags()` (`:362-363`), and then immediately calls `groups.fetchFollowedTags()` again (`:427`). That's two identical paginated requests in parallel, because `groups.ts:749` doesn't dedupe. → Share one promise, e.g. an in-flight guard in `fetchFollowedTags`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:342-417` — `loadAffinity` only checks `generation` at the very end, so after `stop()` it still pages up to 4 `following` requests plus a 40-post home fetch. → Check `gen !== generation` after each await and bail. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:52` — The module-level `affinityCache` (your follow list and home authors) survives logout and account removal for 15 minutes in memory. → Clear it from `stop()`, or key it by account id and clear it on account removal. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/composables/useEdwardStream.ts:150-155` — `feedAccount()` falls back to `instances.primaryInstance`, but `primaryInstance` is literally `activeAccount` (`stores/instances.ts:277-279`), so the fallback branch is dead. → Fall back to the first instance with an `accessToken`, or delete the branch. · mech

### Store (`stores/edward.ts`)
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/stores/edward.ts:573-590` — `dropAuthor` only filters `statuses`. The backpack in `useEdwardStream.ts:70` (up to 360 posts) still holds the ghosted or shaped author's posts, and they drip back within seconds. Firehose posts fetched through other connected accounts or the guest host aren't filtered by your server-side mute either. → Expose a `dropAuthor` on the stream that also purges the backpack, and keep a session "ghosted" set that `pack()` and `mergeNewer` reject. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/stores/edward.ts:576-577` — `dropAuthor` compares the post's `acct` as its own server wrote it (`bob` on server A vs `bob@A` seen via server B). Ghosting a local `bob` from A also removes every other server's local `bob` and misses `bob@A` arriving through B. `authorKey` has the same problem (`edwardSemantics.ts:297`), which mis-threads author filaments. → Normalize to a full `user@host` handle using `lookupAcctFor`-style qualification (with `account.url` host) before comparing. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/edward.ts:551-566` — `mergeNewer` slices to `EDWARD_MAX_BALLS` without protecting `selectedIdentity`, `focusedIdentity` or a held or recessed post. After about 9 min at flow (or about 6 at rush) the open peek, or the post being replied to, gets evicted. `selectedStatus` turns null and the modal vanishes mid-read. → Pin selected, focused and held identities when trimming. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/stores/edward.ts:193-197` — `watchCanPrev` is true with a single history item. `watchPrev` then enters scrub mode on the same post (cursor 0 equals current), so "previous" just pauses the deck. → Require `watchHistory.length >= 2` when live, or when the only entry is the focused one. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·M] `app/stores/edward.ts:144-157` + `app/utils/edwardExplore.ts:523-527` — Every `statuses` change re-runs `filterAndSortBalls`, which spreads all ~280 descriptors into new objects even in `stream` sort. That defeats the WeakMap memo and gives the canvas new objects every drip. → Return the cached descriptors untouched for `stream`, and only stamp rank on the visible slice. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·L] `app/stores/edward.ts:269-293` — `serverDialects` runs `new URL()` twice per status (280) on every drip just to count hosts. → Use `ball.instanceHost`, already computed in the cached descriptor. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/stores/edward.ts:302-316` — `enter()` restores speed, tour and captions from prefs, but the lens shape (`focusMode`) is neither persisted nor reset on `exit()`. It survives within a page session and is forgotten on reload. → Add `focusMode` to `EdwardPrefs` (`edwardPace.ts:41`). · mech

### 3D canvas — GPU, perf & lifecycle (`EdwardCanvas.vue`)
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1496-1525` — `frame()` calls `renderer.render` at display rate unconditionally. With speed `still`, under reduced motion (`integrateBubblePhysics` early-returns at `:731`), behind the peek modal, or while recessed, it re-renders an identical transmission-glass scene 60–120 times a second, which drains laptops and phones. → Render on demand: skip `render` when there's no sim, camera, hover or drag change, or throttle to ~10 fps when idle, recessed, or while `selectedIdentity` is set. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* Rendering every frame unconditionally is real, but the scene isn't "identical". Even at `still` (mul 0), bubbles breathe on wall-clock `t` (`:1127`) and stars rotate unless reduced motion is on. The real waste is continuous transmission-glass rendering while recessed or behind the peek modal, and under reduced motion. [Corrected: "Canvas renders at display rate even while recessed, behind the peek, or under reduced motion. Throttle or render on demand in those states." Impact Medium.]
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:489-508` — Every ball creates its own `MeshPhysicalMaterial` with `transmission`, `iridescence` and `clearcoat`, plus its own 512² `CanvasTexture` with `anisotropy=4` (`:353-378`). With 72 desktop / 38 phone balls that's about 1.3 MB of GPU texture per ball, and every transmissive mesh forces the extra transmission pass. Low-end GPUs stutter. → Share one shell material per dialect accent (tint via instance color), pack faces into an atlas, and add an adaptive quality tier (drop transmission or iridescence when frame time is above 20 ms). · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1696-1699` — `powerPreference: 'high-performance'` forces the discrete GPU on dual-GPU MacBooks for a lean-back ambient mode, a big battery hit. → Use `'default'` (or `'low-power'` when `edwardIsCompact()`). · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1105-1106` — Each frame reads `hostEl.clientWidth/clientHeight` right after the previous frame wrote reactive `bubbles` (every frame, `:1207`) and `captions` (every 2nd frame, `:1100`). That's a forced synchronous layout per frame, plus Vue patching up to 16 absolutely-positioned nodes at 30–60 Hz. → Cache host size from the existing ResizeObserver, and update bubble and caption transforms imperatively via refs instead of reactive arrays. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1515-1518` — While hovering, `projectHover` runs every frame. It calls `getBoundingClientRect()` (`:1248`) and assigns a brand-new `hoverCard` object, re-rendering the whole hover card DOM at 60 fps. → Only replace `hoverCard` when the identity changes, and move it with a transform on a ref'd element. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:851-887` — `rebuildFilaments` allocates a new `BufferGeometry` (GPU buffer create plus dispose) every 30 frames and on every sync. Between rebuilds the lines stay frozen at old positions while the balls move, so filaments visibly detach. → Keep one preallocated `Float32BufferAttribute` (180×6), rewrite the pairs each frame, and set `needsUpdate`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:664-669` — When a newer post pushes the 73rd ball past `maxBalls()`, `disposeBall` removes it instantly wherever it is, often mid-screen or inside the lens. Bubbles visibly pop out of existence every drip. → Fade or shrink the outgoing ball over ~400 ms, or prefer evicting balls that are off-stage. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1676-1679` — `await import('three')` isn't wrapped. A chunk-load failure is an unhandled rejection, leaving a blank purple void with no message. → try/catch around the import, then `edward.setError('Couldn't load the 3D view — check your connection and reopen Edward.')`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1700-1703` — Without WebGL, Edward shows one error line but keeps polling every server. The lens never runs (`updateBalls` never fires), so the watch deck never fills. The user gets an error over an empty void plus a hidden list. → Without WebGL, fall back to a 2D mode: show the `__list` panel visibly and let tour drive the deck, or exit with a toast. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1582-1583` — The context-lost copy says it "resumes when the device allows", but there's no retry and it stays up indefinitely if Chrome refuses to restore. → Add a "Restart 3D" action that disposes and re-inits the scene, or use `WEBGL_lose_context.restoreContext()`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1329-1346` — `onPointerDown` ignores `e.button`. A right click (no movement) reaches `onPointerUp` and emits `pick` (`:1423-1424`), so the browser context menu opens on top of the peek. Middle-click does the same. → Return early unless `e.button === 0` (or `pointerType !== 'mouse'`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1459-1473` — `applyEdgeHoverScroll` scrubs the stream whenever the mouse rests in the outer 13%/11% bands. Nothing visible explains it (the edge gradients are 8–10% alpha), so a parked cursor makes the stream "run away". → Require a drag or wheel to scrub, or show a visible chevron cue while the edge is active. · decision
- [ ] [PERF·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1846-1861` — Three watchers call `syncBallsFromStore`. A filter change fires both the `visibleBalls` watcher and the `[exploreQuery, exploreSort]` watcher, and an affinity change fires both `affinity` and `visibleBalls`. Each sync rebuilds filaments. → Keep only the `visibleBalls` watcher. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1278` — `hitTest` allocates `balls.flatMap(...)` (about 150 elements) on every `pointermove`. → Keep a cached pick-mesh array, rebuilt in `syncBallsFromStore`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:956-957` — `measureLens` reaches into the parent with global `document.querySelector('.edward-mode__header' / '__explore')`, coupling the canvas to the parent's class names. → Pass the header and console rects (or their heights, already measured as `headerH`/`exploreH`) as props. · mech

- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardCanvas.vue:1923` — The hover header prints the internal mood id (`{{ hoverCard.mood }}` such as `yapping`, `linky`, `eggplant`, `booby`) uppercased next to the `why` label, which often repeats it ("REPLY · YAPPING"). → Show `why` only, or map moods to user-facing labels. · mech

### Watch deck & HUD (`EdwardMode.vue`)
- [ ] [CODE·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1115-1136` — The phone deck only renders below 1100px (`:1067`), but the `@media (max-width:1099px)` block hides every `__film-tile` and `__watch-hint` (`:2245-2249`). - [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:865-886` — The captions ("words") toggle only exists in the desktop pace dock (`:1252-1262`, ≥1100px). - [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:538-541` — `clearExplore()` always focuses the input. Tapping the clear "x" on a phone pops the soft keyboard over the stage, and Esc→clear also lands focus in the input. → Only refocus when the clear came from the keyboard (or the input already had focus). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:331-341` — `lastMovingSpeed` starts as `'flow'` instead of the restored `edward.speed`, and `-`/`=` (`:669`, `:674`) call `edward.stepSpeed` directly without updating it. - [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1266-1268` — The error banner sits at top:40% over the lens with no dismiss and no retry. - [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:836-843` — The only loading cue is a tiny "hacking…" stat. When the stage is empty and unfiltered (all servers returned nothing, or guest-gated), the user sees an empty starfield with no explanation. → Add a centered stage message for loading ("ed is tuning in…") and for empty unfiltered results ("nothing floating by yet — your servers are quiet"). · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1416-1439` — The keyboard and screen-reader twin list comes last in DOM order, after about 30 Tab stops (header, server chips, deck, rail, pace, input, 6 channels, 7 sorts, ~10 chips), and has no shortcut. → Move it before the console, or add a shortcut (e.g. `P`) and a skip link from the dialog start. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:3114-3119` — Sort pills are `min-height:22px` and channels are 24px (`:2870`), with no coarse-pointer bump. - [ ] [A11Y·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:3024` — The desktop pace dock rests at `opacity: 0.6` on top of `color-mix(#fff8d6 62%)` text on a 70%-alpha panel. - [ ] [A11Y·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1799` — Much of the UI is 9px or smaller: `0.5625rem` on the watch label, legend (`:1605`), crumb (`:2174`), deck label (`:2715`), pace options (`:3051`), rail head (`:3165`), and the inline action buttons (`EdwardWatchActions.vue:417`), plus `0.5rem` film names. - [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1061-1063` — The "ed remembers what floats through the lens…" empty copy shows whenever `deckRecent.length < 3`, so it sits under one or two real rows as if the list were empty. → Show it only when `deckRecent.length === 0`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1093` — The phone deck label says "held · live" / "watching · live" and has no touring state. The desktop says "held" / "touring · flow" / "watching · flow" (`:967-975`). → Share one label computed across both. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:836-843` — The header stat reads "{n} coins", which gives "1 coins", and "coins" isn't explained anywhere. - [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:869` — The pace button's `aria-label` says "Tap to change", but the narrow header also shows for mouse and keyboard users at 640–1099px. → Use "Stream pace: {label}. Activate to change". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:628-707` — Only Enter checks `selectedIdentity`. - [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:2214-2217` — `.edward-mode__stat:nth-child(2)` hides "the server stat" on phones. When `sourceCount` is 0 (v-if comment node), the backpack stat becomes child 2 instead. → Give the servers stat its own `--servers` class and hide that. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:1011` — Deck hero and row images use `alt=""` even when the attachment has a `description`, so the hero media is invisible to screen readers. → Carry `description` through `cardFromStatus` and use it as `alt`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:898-903` — The icon-only Exit button has no `title` tooltip. Mouse users get no hint and Esc is only mentioned in the crumb hint. → Add `title="Exit (Esc)"`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:87-93` — `ageLabel` is copy-pasted in `EdwardCanvas.vue:1230-1236` and hardcodes English "now/m/h/d". → Reuse the app's shared relative-time helper (`Intl.RelativeTimeFormat`). · mech

### Post peek (`EdwardPostModal.vue`)
- [ ] [UX·H·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:246` — `initialText: '@${handle} '` uses `account.acct` as the post's own server wrote it. For a post fetched from another server (firehose or tag), a local author is just `bob`, so the reply posted from your server mentions your server's `bob`. The same bug is in `EdwardWatchActions.vue:131`, and the toasts print the bare name too (`:174`, `:180`). → Build the handle with `lookupAcctFor(acct, account.url, _instanceUrl, activeUrl)` (already used for follow) in both places. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:227-238` — Peek reply calls `resolveReplyId` without `uri` (the deck version passes `uri: body.value.uri`, `EdwardWatchActions.vue:115`). The store notes `uri` "resolves more reliably". On failure the peek shows a dead-end toast, while the deck offers "Open on {host}" via `replyHandOff`. → Pass `uri` and reuse `replyHandOff` (move it to a shared util). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:374-382` — Media is a fixed 2-column grid of `<img src=previewUrl||url>`. An audio attachment (no preview) puts an mp3 URL into `<img>` (broken icon). A single image renders half-width. More than 4 attachments are silently dropped. Images can't be opened or zoomed (and `NeoLightbox` at z 1065 would sit under Edward's 1090). → Single-image full width, a "+N" tile, a type-aware placeholder for audio/video, and a click-to-lightbox with the lightbox z-index above Edward. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:363-372` — Polls aren't rendered. A poll post shows only its question (the face even says "poll"), with no options or results. → Render read-only poll options with percentages, or reuse the app's poll component. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:56` — `safeHtml` isn't run through `emojify` (`RealPostCard.vue:162` does it), so custom emoji show as raw `:blobcat:` shortcodes in the body even though the display name is emojified (`:52`). → `emojify(sanitizeStatusHtml(...), body.emojis, ...)` as in `RealPostCard`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:106` — `needsReveal` (and `edwardSemantics.ts:216`'s `cw` badge) treats `sensitive` without a spoiler as a content warning. Mastodon uses that for sensitive media only, so the text is safe, yet the peek hides the text behind "Sensitive content", and the deck, hover and list say "CW · sensitive" / "content warning". It also ignores the user's `reading:expand:media` / `reading:expand:spoilers` prefs (`stores/settings.ts:128`). → Fold text only when `spoilerText` is set, gate media on `sensitive`, and honour the account's expand prefs. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:164-189` — Follow and unfollow are a single tap with no confirm, and there's no self check. On your own post "follow" errors with "Couldn't update follow" (same for ghost and shape in `EdwardWatchActions.vue:180-228`). → Hide or disable follow, ghost and shape when the author is you (`affinityDetail.self` or `currentUser.acct`), and consider confirming unfollow. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:311` — The dialog's accessible name is just "Thought". → Use "Post by {displayName}". · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:619-620` — The close "x" is 32×32px and the footer buttons are about 28px tall (`:796`), with no coarse-pointer bump, on a bottom sheet that's mostly used on phones. → Use 44px minimum targets under `(pointer: coarse)`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:495` — `backdrop-filter: blur(6px)` over a full-screen WebGL canvas that's still rendering at 60 fps means the blur is recomputed every frame, which janks the peek on phones. → Use a solid dim on `(pointer: coarse)`, or pause the canvas while a post is selected (see the frame-loop item). · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardPostModal.vue:244-252` — The peek says Reply, Bookmark and "Sign in to do that", while the deck says Yap, Stash and "Sign in to do that!!" (`EdwardWatchActions.vue:58,129,342`). The peek also lacks beam, ghost and shape, and the deck lacks follow, profile and join. Two vocabularies and two action sets for the same post. → Pick one voice and a shared action set (or clearly primary/secondary). · decision

### Watch actions (`EdwardWatchActions.vue`)
- [ ] [UX·H·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardWatchActions.vue:127` — `edward.setRecessed(true)` unmounts this very component (`EdwardMode.vue:395,1067,1203`), so its `composeSheet.open` watcher (`:230-235`) never runs. - [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardWatchActions.vue:207` — Shape uses a native `window.confirm`, which breaks the Edward look and is blocked in some embedded contexts. - [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardWatchActions.vue:192-196` — The ghost and shape toasts have no Undo, though both are reversible and fire from a moving deck where mis-taps are likely. → Add `actionLabel: 'Undo'` that calls `unmute`/`unblock`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardWatchActions.vue:146-159` — "Beam" only copies to the clipboard. ### Semantics, affinity, faces & explore (utils)
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/utils/edwardAffinity.ts:108-137` — "You posted this", "you follow" and "mentions you" match on the part of the handle before `@` alone (`authorLocal === selfLocal`, `followingLocalParts(...).has(authorLocal)`, `localPart(mn) === selfLocal`). In a multi-server firehose, every `alice@anywhere` gets "★ you posted this" or "♥ you follow @alice", a +0.55/+0.38 size boost, and passes the "you" filter. → Match full `user@host` (qualify bare handles with the source host, as `lookupAcctFor` does), and keep local-part matching only for posts from your own server. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/utils/edwardAffinity.ts:150-153` — The "your handle appears in plain text" bonus is a substring test for any username longer than 2 characters. Users named `the`, `art` or `dev` get an affinity bump on most posts. → Require a word or `@` boundary (`\b@?name\b`) and at least 4 characters. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/utils/edwardFaces.ts:116-117` — The spicy lexicon flags ordinary content: `KITTY_RE` matches 🐱 😻 🐈 and "kitty" (every #caturday post), `PEACH_RE` matches "cake"/"peach" (`:115`), `\bcock\b` roosters, and `\btits?\b` birds (`:119`). Spicy also overrides the CW face and dimming (`edwardSemantics.ts:263`). Cat photos get hovered as "SPICY". → Drop the cat emoji, "kitty", "cake" and "peach" from the spicy sets (keep explicit terms), or require two cues. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/utils/edwardFaces.ts:87` — `LAUGH_RE` contains an unanchored `www+`, so any post whose text includes a "www." link becomes a laughing "giggle" face. `ASK_RE` (`:110`) matches any `?`, including URL query strings, so link posts read as "asking something". → Anchor `www` as `\bw{3,}\b` (JP-style laughter, without a following dot), and skip `?` inside URLs (strip URLs before cue matching). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/utils/edwardSemantics.ts:235` — `mediaUrl = previewUrl || url` falls back to the raw media URL. Audio and some video have no preview, so the canvas tries to load an mp3/mp4 as an `Image` (`EdwardCanvas.vue:381-396`), the rim says "· picture ·" (`:258`), tour odds and gem score get the media boost, and the deck renders a broken `<img>` (`EdwardMode.vue:171-174`). → Only use `previewUrl`, or `url` when `type === 'image'`, and add an audio/video badge. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/utils/stripHtml.ts:14-15` — `textContent` concatenates block elements without whitespace: `<p>a</p><p>b</p>` gives "ab", and `<br>` gives nothing. Edward previews, captions, bubbles, rim text and search all glue the last word of one paragraph to the first of the next (cross-cutting; used app-wide). → Replace `<br>` and `</p>` with `\n` (or insert spaces after block elements) before reading `textContent`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/utils/edwardExplore.ts:348` — `#tag` search strips everything outside `[a-z0-9_]`, so `#日本語` becomes empty (ignored) and `#café` becomes `#caf`. Non-Latin and accented hashtags can't be searched. → Use `/[^\p{L}\p{N}_]/gu`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·L] `app/utils/edwardExplore.ts:518` — A `sort:x` token in the query always wins over `exploreSort`. After typing `sort:loud` (or picking a placeholder example), clicking another sort pill (`EdwardMode.vue:1330`) changes `exploreSort`, but nothing moves and the pill doesn't light up (`sortActive` reads the query first, `:383-384`). → Have `setSort` strip any `sort:` token from the query. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/utils/edwardExplore.ts:129-136` — The sort labels "near · you" and "related" sound the same, and the hints ("related and big, pulled close" vs "closest to who and what you follow") don't separate them. → Rename `near` to something like "big & close", or merge the two modes. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/utils/edwardExplore.ts:396` — `-lang:xx` only excludes posts that declare a language, so the "far away" channel (`:194`) is full of untagged posts in your own language. → For the `far` channel, require `ball.language` (treat unknown as excluded). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/utils/edwardSemantics.ts:221` — Previews, labels and snippets cut with `.slice()`: `preview.slice(0,160)`, `label.slice(0,48)` (`:231`), `EdwardCanvas.vue:213,259`, `EdwardMode.vue:182`, `EdwardPostModal.vue:66`. That splits emoji surrogate pairs and ZWJ sequences into broken glyphs, even though `clipGraphemes` exists in `utils/stripHtml.ts:23`. → Use `clipGraphemes` everywhere. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·L] `app/utils/edwardSemantics.ts:414-416` — Reason chips print raw counts ("loud · 12345 reactions"). → Format with `Intl.NumberFormat` (compact). · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·L] `app/utils/edwardFaces.ts:963` — `manic` (viral) and `rage` (`:966`) share the glyph `✧ヮ✧`, so the legend, deck and peek can't tell "viral" from "heat". → Give rage its own glyph (e.g. `ಠ益ಠ`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/components/edward/EdwardMode.vue:909` — The legend shows `EDWARD_FACE_LEGEND.slice(0,10)`, which drops `cw` (an important one) along with cool, smug, huh and sleepy. The most common faces (media ♥‿♥, ask, bot ▣▣, link, poll) aren't in `EDWARD_FACE_LEGEND` (`edwardFaces.ts:937-953`) at all. → Curate the legend around frequency: media, reply, boost, ask, cw, bot, link, love, anger, laugh. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·L] `app/utils/edwardVoice.ts:40-69` — `edQuip` has no lines for loading, error or empty-unfiltered states. While the banner says the server is rate-limiting or the 3D view died, Ed still types "hack hack hack ✌". → Pass `loading`/`error`/`total` and return matching lines ("ed can't reach the net… retrying (´・ω・`)"). · mech

### Tests (gaps)
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·M] `tests/edward.test.ts:48` — The test "matches followed accounts by local part as before" encodes the cross-server false positive (bare `alice` matches `alice@far.example`). There are also no tests for `useEdwardStream` (re-seed backoff, backpack shed/release order, ghost purge), for the recessed restore after the compose sheet closes, or for `mergeNewer` keeping the selected post. → Flip that test to assert no match from a foreign server, and add store and composable specs for those three behaviours with fake timers. · judgment
