## Settings, theming & design system

Contrast ratios below were computed (WCAG 2.x relative luminance, `color-mix(in srgb)` and alpha composited over the real surface) from the literal token values in `app/assets/css/_themes.scss` / `_structural.scss`. "face" = `--neo-bg-secondary` (the Settings modal surface), "bg" = `--neo-bg-primary`, "tertiary" = `--neo-bg-tertiary`.

### Segmented control (`NeoRadioGroup.vue`)
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/components/NeoRadioGroup.vue:30-49` — `onKeydown` works out `next` and emits the new value, but never focuses the next button. Focus stays on the old button, which now has `tabindex=-1`, so the next ArrowRight works out the same `next` again and `select()` returns early. Arrow keys move exactly one step, then stop. → Keep button refs and call `refs[next].focus()` after `select()` (or in `nextTick`). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/NeoRadioGroup.vue:99-103` — The selected segment differs from the others only by fill (`--neo-bg-card` against `--neo-bg-tertiary` = 1.08–1.39:1 in all 12 themes, e.g. light 1.22, dark 1.19, contrast 1.39) plus a 1px hairline ring that is itself ~1.2:1. The selected state is barely visible (WCAG 1.4.11 needs 3:1). → Give the checked state a ≥3:1 indicator: an accent border/underline or `--neo-border-color-dark` ring, plus a weight change. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/NeoRadioGroup.vue:30-46` — The keydown handler hijacks Arrow keys even with Alt/Ctrl/Meta held, which breaks browser back/forward shortcuts, and it doesn't support Home/End. → Return early when `e.altKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey`, and add Home/End. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/NeoRadioGroup.vue:115-118` — At ≤480px every button gets `flex: 1 1 calc(33.333% - 2px)`, so the 5 font-size options wrap 3 + 2 and the last two stretch wider than the first three. → Use `grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(4.5rem,1fr))` or size from `options.length`. · judgment

### Settings modal: behaviour & sync (`SettingsModal.vue`, `stores/settings.ts`)
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/stores/settings.ts:166,361-367,845-862` — `localPreferences.defaultVisibility` / `defaultSensitive` / `postingDefaultsTouched` are stored once in `neospace_local_prefs` and the getter prefers them over `preferences['posting:default:visibility']` for every account. After one save, every other linked account's server default is ignored, both in Settings and in the composer. → Key the posting overrides by `activeAccountId` (or drop the local override and trust the server `source.privacy` once the PATCH succeeds). · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:870-879` — If `updateCredentials({source})` fails (offline, 422, GoToSocial/Akkoma variant), the failure only goes to `console.warn`, and then `saveSuccess = true` shows "Settings saved successfully". The user thinks the server has the new default, but the web UI and other clients disagree. → On failure, set `this.error` ("Saved on this device only — couldn't update your server") and skip `saveSuccess`. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:537-550` — "Block domain" fires immediately with no confirmation. On Mastodon a domain block removes every follower from that server and hides all its content. One mistyped Enter drops followers irreversibly. → Use `overlayStore.openConfirm` with a danger style and copy that says followers from that domain will be removed. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:903` + `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:94-117` + `app/stores/settings.ts:408-415` — `updateAppearance` sets `saveSuccess = true` without `clearSuccess()`. Sidebar cycles (theme/chrome/corners/density/lines) run it while Settings is closed, and `open()` never resets `saveSuccess`, so the next time Settings opens it shows a stale "Settings saved successfully" banner. → Reset `saveSuccess` in `open()`, and only set it from explicit Settings saves (or always pair it with `clearSuccess()`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1384,1433` (also `settings.ts:774,993,1014`) — The "Collapse reposts" and "Photo upload size" changes, block-domain, create-filter and delete-filter all set `saveSuccess` without starting the 3s clear timer. The green banner stays until the user changes category, and it duplicates the "Filter created" / "Blocked x" toasts. → Pick one feedback channel per action (toast *or* banner) and always call `clearSuccess()`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1087-1094,409-421` — Theme swatches are native radios sharing `name="settings-theme"`, so each Arrow key checks the next theme and fires `change` → `onThemeApply`. Arrowing across 13 swatches shows 13 "Theme updated" toasts, and "Revert" only goes back one step, not to the theme the user started from. → Apply on `change` but debounce the toast, and store the theme from when the panel opened (or when focus entered the grid) as the Revert target. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:286-300` + `app/stores/settings.ts:509-536` — The appearance watcher isn't deep and only fires when `localPreferences` is replaced. `startSuiteLookSync` changes theme/ui/font in place, so the open modal keeps showing the old theme. The next Font size/Corners click calls `saveAppearance()` with the stale `appearanceForm.theme/ui/font`, which undoes the change a sibling tool just made. → Make the watcher `{ deep: true }`, or have the suite sync replace the object (`this.localPreferences = {...}`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:719-751` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old #764): every `open()` calls `loadSettings()`, and the `FunLoader fill` block (min-height:100%) renders *in addition to* the panel, not instead of it. Each open pushes the current panel below the fold for a moment, including local-only Appearance. → Put the panel in `v-else`, or only show the loader when `!settingsStore.account`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:347` — Keyword matching also checks `query.includes(k)`, so short keywords match inside longer words: "email", "detail" and "maintain" all show *Humans first* (keyword `ai`), and anything containing "ui" (e.g. "build", "guide") shows Appearance. → Match whole words / prefixes only (`k.startsWith(query)` or tokenise the query); drop the reverse check. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:56,63,70,84` — Search keywords point to settings that don't exist (`avatar`, `header`, `fields`, `website`, `language`, `cw`, `push`), and `discoverable` sends users to Privacy although the toggle is in Profile. Real settings have no keywords: `photo`/`upload`/`quality` (Posting), `reposts`/`boost`/`collapse`, `import`/`export`/`reset`/`backup`, `size`/`corners`/`chrome`/`flip`, `password`/`2fa`/`email` (Account). "photo" currently finds only *Humans first*. → Rebuild the keyword lists from what each panel actually renders. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:658-714` — Search only filters the nav list. On desktop the content pane keeps showing the previous category (e.g. Profile while the nav shows only "Appearance"), Enter does nothing, and the matching control isn't highlighted or scrolled to. → On a single match (or Enter), switch to the first result and scroll/flash the matching fieldset. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1049,1552` — The instance links are built as `/settings/notifications` and `/settings/filters`. Mastodon serves these at `/settings/preferences/notifications` and `/filters`, so both buttons open a 404 on stock Mastodon. → Fix the Mastodon paths (and branch by `instance.software` for GtS/Akkoma, see skipped old #771). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:611-615` + `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1520-1522` — If `v2.filters.list()` fails (GoToSocial before v2 filters, older Akkoma), the error only goes to the console and the panel says "No filters configured", even though the server may have v1 filters or not support filters at all. → Keep a `filtersError` / `filtersUnsupported` flag and show "Filters aren't available on this server" instead of the empty state. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:750-761` + `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:999-1001` — Blocked domains have no loading state, swallow load errors, and make one unpaginated `domainBlocks.list()` call. The panel says "No blocked domains" while loading or after a failure, and long lists are silently cut at the server page size. → Add `isLoadingDomains`, an error message, and Link-header paging like mutes/blocks. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:764` — `blockDomain` only trims and strips a leading `@`. Pasting `https://mastodon.social/`, `mastodon.social/@bob` or `@bob@host` sends that string to the server and gets a raw 422 message back. → Parse with `new URL()` when the input contains `://`, take the part after the last `@`, strip paths, and validate the hostname before sending. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:518-523` + `app/stores/settings.ts:986-989` — New filters always use the contexts home/notifications/public/thread (never `account`, i.e. profiles) and `wholeWord: true`, with no expiry option. The list (`SettingsModal.vue:1529-1536`) shows neither context nor `expiresAt`, so expired filters look active. → Add context checkboxes, a whole-word toggle and an expiry select, and show "Expired" / context chips on each row. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1504-1508` — "Create filter" stays enabled with empty fields and then shows an error toast, while "Block domain" (`:994`) disables itself when empty. Two forms on adjacent panels validate in different ways. → Disable the button until title and ≥1 keyword exist, and show inline field errors. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:84-85,131-132,777,799` — The 30/500 limits are hard-coded, and `.length` / `maxlength` count UTF-16 code units: emoji count as 2 here but as 1 on the server. GoToSocial/Akkoma allow longer names and bios, so a name already longer than 30 characters arrives `aria-invalid` and Save stays disabled until the user shortens it. → Count with `[...str].length` (or `Intl.Segmenter`), read limits from instance config where available, and drop `maxlength` so the counter and error do the limiting. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1375-1386,1424-1435,1454-1462` — The Posting panel mixes two save models: "Collapse duplicate reposts" and "Photo upload size" apply instantly, while visibility and sensitive media wait for "Save Posting Defaults" further down. "Collapse duplicate reposts" is a Home-timeline reading setting, not a posting default. → Auto-save all four (and remove the button), or move "Collapse reposts" to a Reading/Timeline group. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:629` — `@click.self="tryClose"` on the overlay fires when a text selection drag (e.g. in the Bio textarea) starts inside the modal and ends on the backdrop, because the click target becomes the overlay. A clean form closes unexpectedly. → Close only if `pointerdown` also started on the overlay (track it in a `pointerdown.self` flag). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:192-195` — `initialFocus: '.settings-search__input, …'` focuses the search field on every open. On phones (list view) the soft keyboard opens right away and covers half the category list. → On `isNarrowSettings`, focus the title or close button instead and leave search for the user to tap. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:452-455` + `app/stores/settings.ts:936-938` — "Reset appearance" runs at once with no confirm or undo (theme changes get a Revert toast, reset doesn't). It also turns off Reduce motion and custom profile CSS, so an accessibility preference silently comes back on. → Confirm or offer an Undo toast, and keep `reduceMotion` out of the reset (or say what it covers). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:959-963` — `importAppearanceJson` accepts any JSON object or array (e.g. `package.json`, `[]`): `parseAppearance` fills every missing key with defaults and the user sees "Appearance imported", having just reset everything. → Require at least one known appearance key (or a `v` field), and reject anything else with "Not a NeoSpace appearance file". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1596-1621` — The "Linked accounts" rows are plain text: you can't switch to an account or sign out of one from here, only "Sign out all". → Make each row a button that switches account, and add a per-row "Sign out". · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/settings.ts:128-130` — `reading:expand:media`, `reading:expand:spoilers` and `reading:autoplay:gifs` are fetched and typed but nothing in `app/` reads them, and there's no in-app equivalent. Users who set "always show sensitive media", "always expand CWs" or "don't autoplay GIFs" on their server get the opposite here. → Honour the server reading prefs in the post card (or add local overrides under Appearance). · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:129,135-143` — `confirmDiscard` only tracks profile, privacy and posting. A half-typed filter (title/keywords) or a domain in the block field is dropped silently on close or category change. → Include the `newFilter*` and `newBlockDomain` drafts in `isDirty`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:383-390,1455-1461` — `savePostingDefaults` doesn't await the store promise, resets the baseline before the PATCH resolves, and the button has no pending state. A failure leaves the form looking clean. → `await` it, show "Saving…", and only re-baseline on success. · mech

### Settings modal: accessibility
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1912-1916,2237-2241` — `.settings-search__input:focus` / `.settings-input:focus` set `outline:none`. The scoped selector (0,3,0) beats both `input:focus-visible` (`main.scss:745-753`) and the forced-colors rule (`main.scss:767-780`). The only remaining indicator is a 3px `--neo-accent-soft` glow (12% alpha, ≈1.1:1) plus a 1px border colour change, and in forced colors both are overridden, so there is no focus indicator. → Delete the `outline:none` lines and let the global two-tone ring apply (or use `:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--neo-focus, var(--neo-accent)) }`). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2232` — `.settings-input` / textarea / filter `<select>` borders use `--neo-border-color` (hair), which is 1.08–1.32:1 against the modal face in 11 of 12 themes (light 1.22, dark 1.19, loom 1.08, y 1.13; only contrast is 3.66). The form fields have no visible edge. `--neo-input-border` (3.2–6.3:1) exists for exactly this. → Use `var(--neo-border-width) solid var(--neo-input-border)`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1343-1451` — The `<fieldset>` whose legend is "Default Visibility" also wraps "Collapse duplicate reposts", "Mark media as sensitive" and the whole "Photo upload size" group, so screen readers announce "Default Visibility, group" for unrelated controls. → Close the fieldset after the visibility list, and give each other block its own fieldset/legend. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2532-2535,2631-2634,1097,2431-2441` — In forced-colors mode, Chrome and Typography cards show "selected" only through `border-color: accent` + `accent-soft` fill, which are both overridden, so nothing shows the selection. Theme chips are painted with `background` colour, which is also overridden, so all 12 solid swatches render as identical Canvas squares. → Add `@media (forced-colors:active)` rules: `.active { outline: 3px solid Highlight }`, and `forced-color-adjust: none` on `.settings-theme-swatch__chip`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1172-1179,1184-1195,1225-1247` — Each `NeoRadioGroup` sits in a fieldset whose legend already says "Font Size" / "Corners" / "Density" / "Lines", and also gets `ariaLabel` with the same text, so screen readers hear "Font Size group, Font size radio group". → Make `ariaLabel` optional and pass `aria-labelledby` the legend id, or drop the fieldset wrapper. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1791` — The static "Not built yet…" paragraph has `role="status"`, so it is announced as a live update every time the Humans panel opens. → Remove the role; it's ordinary text. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1053,1556,1701,1717` — External links end in a literal "→", which screen readers read as "right arrow", and nothing says they open in a new tab. → Wrap the arrow in `aria-hidden` and add sr-only "(opens in new tab)" or an external-link icon. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:819-833,1253-1264` — `<label class="settings-toggle">` wraps a `<div>`, which isn't valid inside a label, and the checkbox's accessible name runs on into the long description (plus "robot face" from the 🤖 emoji at `:829`). → Use `<span>`s, point `aria-describedby` at the description, and drop the emoji from the label. · mech

### Settings modal: visual polish & copy
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2061,2076,2176,2210,2233,2315,2903,2966,3006,3060,3113` — Hard-coded radii (6/8/10/12px) on the success/error banners, empty states, inputs, option rows, account items/cards, linked rows and filter rows. Choosing Corners "Sharp" or the Brutal/NES/Bauhaus/NYT chromes squares the modal shell while its contents stay rounded. → Use `var(--neo-radius-md)` / `var(--neo-input-radius)` / `var(--neo-card-radius)`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2060,2075,2232,2861,2902` — Hard-coded `1px`/`2px` borders ignore `--neo-border-width`. Brutal (3px), Contrast (2px) and Ink/Crayon lines change every card in the app except these. → Use `var(--neo-border-width) var(--neo-border-style)`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1623-1629,1672-1685,1695-1718` — The Account panel's buttons and link-buttons aren't wrapped in `.settings-actions`. On desktop "Sign out all accounts" and "Clear data on this device" sit only a whitespace apart. At ≤480px (`.settings-btn{width:100%}`, `:3405-3409`) they stack flush with no gap, and "Add account" touches the linked-accounts list. → Wrap each button set in `.settings-actions` (gap 0.75rem), or add `margin-top` to them. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2425-2429,1081-1085,425-436` — The hover/focus preview outline (`is-previewing`) looks exactly like the selected outline (`active`), so while the pointer is over a swatch you can't tell which theme is actually set. Merely moving the mouse across the grid also re-themes the whole app, including the modal you're reading. → Style the preview differently (dashed or thinner) and add a "Current" badge. Consider previewing only inside the chip's mini card, with a short delay before a full-app preview. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1134,1160-1163,2576-2607` — Chrome and Typography samples ask for web fonts (Source Sans 3, Josefin, Plex, Oswald, Cormorant, Newsreader…) that `ensureFontsLoaded` only fetches for the *active* chrome+font. Every other card renders in fallback system fonts, so the previews don't show what you'll get. → Load a subset (`&text=Aa>_`) for every sample family when the Appearance panel opens. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2042-2051,2088-2090,2301-2343,2657-2853,2856-2887,3332,3416-3419` — About 550 lines of dead CSS: `.settings-loading__spinner` + `@keyframes spin`, `.settings-option-grid/card`, `.settings-radius-*`, `.settings-density-*`, `.settings-line-*`, `.settings-segmented*`, and `.settings-section{animation:none}`. None of these classes are in the template. → Delete them. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:623,778,780` — The Military uppercase rule and the chrome UI-font rule target `.settings-nav__item`, but the real class is `.settings-nav-item` (`SettingsModal.vue:683`). `.settings-segmented__btn` no longer exists. In Military chrome the Settings nav is the only nav that isn't uppercase and tracked. → Rename the selector to `.settings-nav-item` and drop the dead one. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:771,865,888,934,979,1173,1344,1517,1578,1689,1706` vs `:1595,1633,1201,1270` — Headings mix Title Case ("Display Name", "Post Privacy", "Muted Accounts", "Font Size", "Default Visibility", "Active Filters", "Connected Account", "Instance Settings", "Data Export") with sentence case ("Linked accounts", "Install app", "Flip text", "Custom profile CSS"). Buttons do the same: "Save Changes" / "Save Posting Defaults" vs "Create filter" / "Block domain". → Use sentence case everywhere. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:662,797,843,880` — ASCII "..." ("Search settings...", "Tell the world about yourself...", "Saving...") next to "…" everywhere else ("Loading…", "Unmuting…", "Creating…"). → Use "…" throughout. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:732,738,829,1535` — The banners, bot label and filter actions use emoji (✓, ⚠️, 🤖, 🚫), which render in full colour and ignore the theme, next to the `NeoIcon` line icons used everywhere else. → Use `NeoIcon` (`check`, `alert`, `ban`) inheriting `currentColor`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:903-906,949-952,1581-1587,1602-1613` — Muted/blocked/linked rows print `displayName` raw, so custom-emoji names show `:blobcat:` shortcodes (`utils/emojify.ts` exists and is used by post cards). The avatars have no `width/height/loading="lazy"`, so up to 40 rows fetch eagerly and shift layout as they load. → Render names through `emojify`, and add `width`/`height`/`loading="lazy"`/`decoding="async"`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:150-155,755-763` — On phones the header `<h1>` changes to the category name ("Appearance"), and the panel's own `<h2>` repeats it right below in larger type. → Hide the `h2` (keep only the description) when `isNarrowSettings && mobilePanelOpen`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1076,1116,1643` — The Theme hint says "use the sidebar cycle button", but phones have no sidebar. "Chrome" names the interface style while the Install section a few panels later means Google Chrome. → Reword the hint per breakpoint, and rename "Chrome" to "Interface style" (or "Shell"). · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1658` — The Android install steps hard-code "Open neospace.ibm.io", which is wrong on preview deploys and self-hosted forks. → Use `location.host`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:1812` — `z-index: 1000` bypasses the token scale. It ties `--neo-z-dropdown` and sits below `--neo-z-sticky` (1020), `--neo-z-fixed` (1030), `--neo-z-modal-backdrop` (1040, e.g. `FeedbackNotes.vue:458`) and `--neo-z-popover` (1060). → `z-index: var(--neo-z-modal)`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:3134` — Filter keywords hard-code `'JetBrains Mono', monospace`, which isn't loaded anywhere and ignores the chrome's mono stack. → `font-family: var(--neo-font-family-mono)`. · mech

### Theme tokens & computed contrast (`_themes.scss`, `_structural.scss`)
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:389` — `--neo-focus` is only defined for `[data-theme='beta']`. `AlgorithmSheet.vue:749,899,944` and `ChatComposer.vue:829` declare `outline: 2px solid var(--neo-focus)` with no fallback. The declaration is invalid at computed-value time, so in the other 11 themes the outline falls back to `none` and those controls have no focus ring. → Define `--neo-focus: #{$accent}` in `neo-from-braun` (themes override as needed). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:411` — Y theme: `--neo-text-on-accent: #fff` on `#1d9bf0` is **3.00:1** (hover `#1a8cd8` 3.63:1). This covers every primary button, the active Settings nav item, the skip link and badges. Danger buttons (`#fff` on `#f4212e`) are **4.11:1**, and danger text on the `#16181c` face is **4.32:1**. → Use `#0f1419` on accent (6.17:1) or darken the accent, and lift the danger colour to ≥4.5 on the face. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/assets/css/_structural.scss:9-12` — `--neo-success: #2f7d4a` is shared across all themes. The "Settings saved successfully" banner (`SettingsModal.vue:2059-2062`, success text on `--neo-success-soft` over the face) is **4.15** light, **3.01** dark, **3.85** contrast, **3.79** paper, **4.19** glass, **3.22** frost, **4.31** brutal, **3.33** loom, **2.44** tank, **4.08** nes; it fails AA in 10 of 12 themes (only beta/y override it). → Give each theme its own success colour in the mixin (e.g. a lighter green for dark schemes). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:76-77` — Danger text on `--neo-danger-soft` over the face (Settings error banner, `SettingsModal.vue:2074-2077`, 15px) is **4.47** light, **4.42** loom, **4.28** tank, **4.31** nes, **4.28** beta, **3.90** y. → Use the full `--neo-text-primary` for banner text with a danger icon and border, or darken/lighten the danger colour per theme. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:381,52` — Muted/secondary text on the selected-card fill (`--neo-accent-soft`; used by Settings option rows, chrome/font cards and the theme picker) is **3.67** in beta and **3.89** in y; beta muted on `--neo-bg-tertiary`/hover is **4.16**. The descriptions of the *selected* option become the hardest text to read. → Lift the beta/y mute a step (beta `#9a9a9a`, y `#8b9196`), or use `--neo-text-primary` for descriptions inside `.active`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:280,346` — Brutal's `$mark` (danger) is `#000000`, the same as ink (1.00:1). Errors, "Delete" and "Clear data" buttons look like ordinary text or black buttons. NES danger equals its accent `#d00000`, so destructive actions look primary (the bug old #135 fixed for Contrast). → Give Brutal a red danger (`#d00000` is already its accent; pick e.g. `#b00020` and differentiate by shape/icon) and NES a distinct danger. · decision
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:40` — `--neo-bg-overlay: color-mix(ink 45%, transparent)`. In the 7 dark themes ink is near-white, so the scrims behind `NeoSheet.vue:95`, `MobileDrawer.vue:382`, `PostActionSheet.vue:85` and `ComposeGroupPicker.vue:496` are a 45% *white* veil that washes the page out to grey, while SettingsModal uses a black 48% scrim (`SettingsModal.vue:1821`). → Base the overlay on black (e.g. `rgb(0 0 0 / .5)` dark, `/.4` light) and use the token in SettingsModal too. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:1025-1035` vs `:143,283,330,349` — `html[data-line=dashed|crayon|ink]` (specificity 0,1,1) overrides the theme's `--neo-border-width` (0,1,0). Contrast's 2px and Brutal/NES's 3px borders drop to 1.5px with Dashed, and Tank's 2px becomes 2.5px with Crayon. The "hard edge" themes lose their defining border. → Have line styles scale a multiplier (`--neo-line-scale`) or apply `max()` against the theme width. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/utils/appearance.ts:57,63,65,66` + `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:593` — Swatch/ink values have drifted from the tokens. Light ink `#c45c26` vs accent `#a84c1e`, Brutal ink `#ff0000` vs `#d00000`, Tank ink `#57a253` vs `#63b25f`, NES swatch `#209cee` / ink `#e4002b` vs page `#f0f0f0` / accent `#d00000`, and the Auto chip uses the old `#c45c26`. The swatch is also written to `<meta name=theme-color>` (`appearance.ts:330-334`), so NES paints the phone status bar bright sky blue above a light grey page. → Derive swatch/ink (and theme-color) from the same values as `_themes.scss`, e.g. one JSON imported by both, or a build step. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:185-186,214-217,233-234,262-265` — Glass/Frost make the face 78%/88% translucent but turn `backdrop-filter` off below 1024px. On phones the full-screen Settings sheet becomes a muddy `#e2e2e3` grey (78% white over the 48% black scrim) with the timeline faintly showing through. → On mobile, also make `--neo-bg-secondary`/`--neo-bg-card` opaque (e.g. `#f4f7fc` / `#10182a`). · mech

### Cascade & token hygiene
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:430-441,443-571` vs `:839-875,926-953` — Every `html[data-ui=…]` block (specificity 0,1,1) includes `neo-chrome-base`, which sets `--neo-leading-body: 1.5` and `--neo-measure: 62ch` (some chromes set their own). This always beats `[data-font=…]` and `[data-font-size=…]` (0,1,0), and `data-ui` is always set by prepaint. Book's 52ch/1.65 and Dyslexic's 48ch/1.7 measure and leading tokens, and the per-size leading, never take effect. Dyslexic readers get 62ch lines on bios, compose and post text (only `.status-content` gets the explicit overrides at `:877-888`). → Raise the font and size selectors to `html[data-font=…]` and put them after the chrome blocks, or let chromes set `--neo-chrome-leading` and resolve with `max()`. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:430-441` vs `:839-875,926-953` — The specificity analysis is correct: every UI id has an `html[data-ui]` block including the base mixin, `normalizeUi` defaults to braun, and the JS only inlines font-family. So Book and Dyslexic `--neo-measure` and all per-size leading never apply. But Dyslexic and Book leading are re-applied explicitly on `body` and `.status-content` (877-888), so the loss is mostly measure (48ch → 62ch) plus leading on compose, bio and the other reading surfaces. [Impact Medium.]
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:40,423,893-896` + `app/assets/css/_themes.scss:926-953` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old #112): there are two font-size mechanisms again. `html{font-size:var(--neo-font-size-base)}` (0,0,1) loses to `[data-font-size]{font-size:…}` (0,1,0), so the "Dense trims type" rule at `main.scss:893-896` does nothing to the root. `.neo-input{font-size:var(--neo-font-size-base)}` applies a rem value on top of an already-scaled root: XXL inputs render at 1.3125rem × 21px ≈ 27.6px instead of 21px, and Medium inputs at 14.06px. → Have `[data-font-size]` set only `--neo-font-size-base` in px/% and give `html` `font-size: var(--neo-font-size-base)` with a higher specificity; make `.neo-input` use `1rem`. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:155-156` — `.neo-card` declares `box-shadow` twice; the second (`--neo-chrome-card-shadow`) discards `--neo-card-extra-shadow`. This rule comes after `_themes.scss:796-803` (which combines both), so Jagged corners never get their stamp-cut edge shadow on `.neo-card`. → Remove both lines here (the combined rule already lives in `_themes.scss`), or write the combined value once. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/components/ComposeSheet.vue:392`, `app/pages/index.vue:2629`, `app/components/ProfileHumanBadges.vue:208`, `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:1937`, `app/components/GroupCard.vue:336` — These use undefined tokens `--neo-link`, `--neo-on-accent`, `--neo-focus-ring`, `--neo-radius-xs` and `--neo-radius-pill`. `--neo-link` has no fallback, so the colour silently inherits. `--neo-on-accent` falls back to `#fff`, which is **2.98:1** on Dark's `#e07a45`, **2.21** on Loom gold, **2.60** on Tank green and **1.07** on Contrast yellow. → Rename to `--neo-text-link` / `--neo-text-on-accent` / `--neo-focus`, and add `--neo-radius-xs`/`--neo-radius-pill` to `_structural.scss` or use `-sm`/`-full`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/assets/css/_structural.scss:173-176` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old #119): `:root` sets `--neo-sidebar-width: 64px` from the stale 56/64/72 tokens, while the real widths are 248/200/68 (`default.vue:316,352,357`). Every `var(--neo-sidebar-width, 248px)` fallback is dead, and anything teleported outside `.neo-layout` gets 64px. → Emit `$sidebar-width-expanded`/`-rail` as the tokens, set `--neo-sidebar-width` on `:root` instead of `.neo-layout`, and delete the `-sm/-md/-lg` set. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/assets/css/_variables.scss:8-28,175-201` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old #116/#119): the light/dark palette (`$bg`…`$dark-mark`, with `$mute: #757575` and `$accent: #c45c26` disagreeing with the live `#666666`/`#a84c1e`), `$sidebar-width-rail/expanded`, `$bp-*`, `@mixin desk` and `@mixin mobile` aren't used anywhere. The comment "used by pre-paint + layout" (`:175`) is false. Only `$accent` leaks out, via `$chaos-stop-1`. → Delete the palette, point `$chaos-stop-1` at a theme token, and either adopt `@include vars.desk` or remove it. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·M] `app/assets/css/_structural.scss:14-25,91-129,185-206` (+ `_themes.scss:66,69,357`) — About 80 custom properties are defined and never read: the whole `--neo-opacity-*` scale except 50, `--neo-letter-spacing-*`, `--neo-transition-timing-*`, `--neo-transition-slow(er)`, `--neo-container-*` except xl, `--neo-content-width*`, `--neo-z-base/-fixed/-sticky/-sheet`, `--neo-warning-*`, `--neo-success-light`, `--neo-danger-light/-dark`, `--neo-info-light/-dark`, `--neo-accent-light`, `--neo-accent-soft-hover`, `--neo-bg-active`, `--neo-border-color-light`, `--neo-body-tracking`, `--neo-chrome-outline-style`, `--neo-nes-sky`, `--neo-mobile-chrome-top`. → Prune them (or document them as public API for profile CSS). · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:265-281,399-403` — `.neo-btn--chaos` and `@keyframes gradient-shift` aren't used anywhere in `app/`. → Delete them, along with the `--neo-chaos-*` tokens (`_structural.scss:214-217`). · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/_structural.scss:158-165` + `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:165-174` — The `--neo-cat-*` group colours are fixed light-theme hexes (two are the old `#c45c26` accent) and aren't themed. The sidebar/GroupCard tints are therefore the same in Contrast, Beta and Brutal. The helpers also sit in a "shell appearance" composable they have nothing to do with. → Move them to `utils/groupCategory.ts`, and define the cat tokens per scheme (or derive them from `--neo-accent`). · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:194,341,447` + `SettingsModal.vue:3193` + `main.scss:224` — Disabled opacity is .45, .5, .6 or .7 depending on the component, so disabled controls look different across adjacent surfaces. → Add a single `--neo-disabled-opacity` token. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:739-743` — The global `:focus-visible` sets `box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px var(--neo-bg-primary)`, which replaces any component shadow while focused. Brutal/NES 4px offset card shadows and Ink-line press shadows vanish on focus, and cards jump. → Implement the halo with `outline` + `outline-offset` only (or a CSS layer the components can opt out of). · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss` (whole file) — There is no `::selection` style, so selection uses the OS highlight, which ignores the theme (blue on Loom gold or Tank green). There are no `@media print` rules, so printing a thread prints the sidebar, mobile tab bar and dark page background. → Add `::selection { background: var(--neo-accent-soft-hover); color: var(--neo-text-primary) }` and a minimal print sheet that hides the shell and forces light colours. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/assets/css/main.scss:65` — Only `body` cross-fades `background-color`/`color` (220ms) on theme change; cards, sidebar and modal snap immediately, so a switch shows a half-second two-tone flash. → Drop the body transition, or apply a short view-transition for theme changes only. · judgment

### Runtime appearance (`appearance.ts`, `loadFonts.ts`, `suiteLook.ts`, `useShellAppearance.ts`, `stores/theme.ts`)
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/utils/appearance.ts:273-280` — `resolveTheme('auto')` reads `matchMedia` once per `applyAppearance` call, and nothing in `app/` listens for `prefers-color-scheme` or `prefers-contrast` changes. When macOS/iOS switch to dark at sunset, or the user turns on Increase Contrast, an "Auto" NeoSpace tab stays on the old theme until reload. The sidebar label "Auto · Light" (`useShellAppearance.ts:64-72`) isn't reactive either. → Add `change` listeners for both queries that re-run `applyLocalAppearance()` when `theme==='auto'`, and expose the resolved scheme as a ref. · mech
  - *Verifier:* Wrong that nothing listens: `app/layouts/default.vue:128-133` re-runs `applyTheme()` on `prefers-color-scheme` change when the theme is auto. What's actually missing: no `prefers-contrast` listener, no listener on `layout:false` pages (login), and `currentThemeLabel` (`useShellAppearance.ts:64-72`) isn't reactive to the media query. [Corrected claim as above. Impact Low-Medium.]
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/appearance.ts:282-286` + `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:94-97` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old #182): the sidebar theme button still cycles `THEME_CYCLE`, which excludes `auto`. One click from "Auto" locks the user into a fixed theme, and the only way back is Settings → Appearance. Reaching the previous theme takes 11 clicks. → Add `auto` to the cycle (first slot), or replace the cycle with the listbox popover described in #182. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·M] `app/utils/loadFonts.ts:13-27` — Google Fonts are requested at weights 400;500 only (Space Mono 400;700). The UI uses 600/700 throughout: Noyes headings (`_themes.scss:495` weight 600), Settings title, h2 and labels (`SettingsModal.vue:1878,2098,2222,2564`), and `.neo-text--bold`. Monocle/Bauhaus/Noyes/Military/NYT therefore show browser-synthesised faux bold (smeared stems, wrong widths). → Add `;600;700` to the families used for UI, or cap UI weights per chrome via `--neo-chrome-heading-weight`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/utils/loadFonts.ts:75-119` + `public/boot/prepaint.js:19-24` — Web fonts are requested only after the app bundle runs `applyLocalAppearance`, and the pre-JS CSS fallback (`_themes.scss:839-875`) keys only on `data-font` with Braun stacks. A Monocle/Bauhaus/Noyes/Military/NYT user sees Helvetica, then the real face swaps in, on every cold load, and the UI-font swap moves the whole layout. (Self-hosting was skipped in old #214; this is the cheaper fix.) → Have prepaint inject the matching `fonts.googleapis.com` `<link rel=stylesheet>` (and `preconnect`) for the stored chrome+font, and set `--neo-font-family`/`-ui` inline from a generated map. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/suiteLook.ts:58-62` — `normalizeTheme()` maps any id it doesn't know to `'auto'`, so the `theme === 'auto' || THEME_IDS.includes` guard always passes. If a sibling tool (bruh/wordcount) stores a theme NeoSpace lacks, every load (`settings.ts:472-473`) and every storage event overwrites the user's chosen NeoSpace theme with Auto. → Check `THEME_IDS.includes(LEGACY[blob.theme] ?? blob.theme) || blob.theme === 'auto'` *before* normalising, and ignore anything else. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/stores/settings.ts:508-536` + `app/utils/suiteLook.ts:94-101` — There is no `storage` listener for `neospace_local_prefs`, only for the suite blob. When tab A changes the theme, tab B's handler calls `saveLocalPreferences()` and writes its *stale* fontSize/density/radius/line/flip values over tab A's. Tab A's other changes are lost on the next reload. → Also listen for `LOCAL_PREFS_KEY` (re-parse and apply), and in the suite handler merge into the stored blob instead of rewriting the whole object. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/stores/settings.ts:483-505` + `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:438-441` — `applyLocalAppearance()` always calls `writeSuiteLook()` (localStorage read, JSON.parse, write) and `ensureFontsLoaded()`. It also runs on every theme-preview `mouseleave`/`focusout`, so sweeping the swatch grid does two sync storage round-trips per swatch. → Split out an `applyOnly()` for previews and loads; write the suite blob only from `updateAppearance`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/appearance.ts:136-205` + `app/components/SettingsModal.vue:2576-2607` — The type stacks exist in three places: `TYPE_FACES` (the dyslexic stack repeated 8 times), `UI_FONTS` (duplicating 5 sans stacks) and the hand-copied chrome-card sample fonts in SettingsModal. Monocle's sample already lacks `"Gill Sans MT"`. → Bind the card samples to `UI_FONTS[option.id]` via `:style`, and build `TYPE_FACES` from shared constants. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:119-139` vs `app/utils/appearance.ts:110-114` — There are two unrelated "density" settings: desk column density (Focused / Roomy / Packed) and Appearance density (Roomy / Cozy / Dense). Both have a "Roomy" option that means different things. → Rename one ("Columns: Focused/Wide/Packed" vs "Spacing: Roomy/Cozy/Dense"). · decision
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:60-62` — `lookAnnounce` is only rendered by `DesktopSidebar.vue:516`. The mobile drawer's theme button (`MobileDrawer.vue:326-334`) cycles with no live announcement, and focus stays on a button whose label changed silently. → Render the shared `lookAnnounce` region once in the layout instead of per sidebar. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ThemeSwatch.vue:8-12` — The swatch is `role="img"` with `aria-label="Theme: …"`, but both callers (`DesktopSidebar.vue:525`, `MobileDrawer.vue:333`) put it inside a button that already has the same `aria-label`. Some screen readers read it twice, and the default "Theme" label is meaningless anywhere else. → Default to `aria-hidden="true"` and only expose `role=img` when a `label` is passed for standalone use. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/theme.ts:30,39-43,97-107,120-123` — `safeCustomCSS` (identity getter), `toggleMode`, `setProfileCssDisabled` and `loadUserTheme` have no callers. `setProfileCssDisabled` claims to be the "escape hatch from Settings" but no Settings UI exists, so `?safe=1` is the only way out of a broken skin. → Delete the dead actions, or wire `setProfileCssDisabled` to a "Disable profile CSS on this device" toggle in Appearance. · judgment

### Pre-paint (`public/boot/prepaint.js`, `scripts/build-prepaint.mjs`)
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `public/boot/prepaint.js:4-37` vs `app/stores/settings.ts:472-475` — Prepaint reads only `neospace_local_prefs`, while runtime then applies the `ibm.tools.shared` suite look on top. Users who changed their theme or chrome in bruh/loom/wordcount get one frame of the old NeoSpace theme, then a switch, on every visit: exactly the flash prepaint exists to prevent. → Have the generated script read the suite blob after local prefs, with the same validation as `readSuiteLook`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `public/boot/prepaint.js:43-49` — If `localStorage` throws (Safari with storage blocked, some private modes), the catch forces `data-theme="light"` without checking `prefers-color-scheme`/`prefers-contrast`, and never sets `data-font-size`. Runtime (`readStoredLocalPrefs` → `auto`) then switches to dark, so dark-mode and high-contrast users get a full white flash. → Move the `matchMedia` resolution out of the `try`, so the fallback still honours OS scheme and contrast, and set `data-font-size="medium"`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `nuxt.config.ts:125-126` + `public/boot/prepaint.js` — The static `theme-color` metas are `#faf9f7` / `#1a1a18`, which match no theme (light bg `#f2f2f0`, dark `#161616`). Prepaint never updates them, so the mobile status bar shows a third colour until JS runs, and users on Loom/Tank/Y with a light OS get a light bar over a black page at boot. → Have prepaint set `meta[name=theme-color]` from the same generated swatch map, and make the static values match light/dark `--neo-bg-primary`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `public/boot/prepaint.js:39-42` + `app/composables/useShellAppearance.ts:28-31` — Prepaint writes an inline `--neo-sidebar-width: 68px` on `<html>`. `syncRailAttr` only toggles the `data-rail` attribute and never removes that inline property, and `.neo-layout` redefines the variable anyway (`default.vue:316,357`). The property does nothing inside the layout and stays stale on `<html>` after the rail is turned off. → Drop the inline property from the generator (the `html[data-rail]` selector already covers it). · mech
- [ ] [TOOLING·L·S] `package.json:9-12` + `scripts/build-prepaint.mjs` — Only `generate` regenerates `public/boot/prepaint.js`; `dev` and `build` use whatever is committed, and no test checks it against `appearance.ts`. A newly added theme, chrome or size id is treated as invalid by prepaint (falls back to auto/braun/medium) and flashes on every load until someone remembers `npm run prepaint`. → Run the script from a Nuxt `build:before` hook (or `predev`/`prebuild`), and add a vitest that regenerates into a string and diffs it against the committed file. · mech

---

## App shell, navigation & shared UI primitives

### Layout (`app/layouts/default.vue`)
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/layouts/default.vue:247`: `<FeedbackNotes v-if="!isMobileSubview">` depends on the route only, not the breakpoint. On desktop, the sidebar's "Leave a note" (`DesktopSidebar.vue:583-591`) does nothing on /notifications, /messages, /status/*, /profile* and /groups/*. `useFeedbackNotes.ts:48-53` still pushes a phantom history entry, so Back seems to do nothing once. The dialog then pops up unprompted when the user navigates Home (`open` is still true). → Mount it when `isDesk || !isMobileSubview`. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/composables/useAppShellHeight.ts:18-25` + `app/layouts/default.vue:240,251-261`: with the iOS overlay keyboard, `--neo-app-height` shrinks to `visualViewport.height`. The in-flow tab bar and the bottom dock (waffle + install banner) then ride up above the keyboard on any page with an input (Explore search, group search). `html[data-keyboard-open]` is only used by `pages/status/[id].vue:1418`. → Hide `.mobile-nav` and `.neo-bottom-dock` while `html[data-keyboard-open]`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/layouts/default.vue:268-281` + `app/components/SubviewChrome.vue:59-62`: on desktop the auth notice is `position:sticky; top:0; z-index:90`, and SubviewChrome is also sticky at `top:0`. When the notice is showing, the Back bar slides underneath it as you scroll. → Make the notice non-sticky at ≥1024px, or offset `.subview-chrome` by the notice height. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/layouts/default.vue:219-222,240`: the comment says only the active breakpoint's nav is rendered, but `MobileHeader` and `MobileTabBar` have no `!isDesk` guard. They mount on desktop and are only hidden with CSS. The tab bar's `useAutoHideNav` adds a capture-phase scroll listener (with a `matchMedia` call on every scroll event), a second `AccountSwitcher` instance and badge computeds. → Add `!isDesk &&` to both v-ifs. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/layouts/default.vue:85` + `MobileTabBar.vue:88-151`: the Messages and Activity tabs route to `/messages` and `/notifications`. Both are `isMobileSubview`, so `showMobileNav` turns false and tapping a tab removes the tab bar. Users can't go Messages → Activity directly and must press Back first. → Decide whether tab destinations keep the tab bar (as Profile already does at line 85). · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/layouts/default.vue:227-234`: the shell has no offline indicator. No `online`/`offline` listener exists anywhere in `app/`, so losing the network only shows up as scattered per-request failure toasts. → Show an "Offline: showing cached posts" notice in the always-mounted status region. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/layouts/default.vue:301-313`: the auth-notice "Dismiss" button is `min-height:24px` at 0.75rem, well under a 44px touch target on phones. → `min-height:44px` at ≤1023px (or padding plus a larger hit area). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/layouts/default.vue:424-427,472-480`: on desktop, `main` keeps `min-height:100dvh` while the auth notice takes its own flex line above it. The page always scrolls by the notice's height, even when the content is short. → Use `min-height: auto` on desktop (the layout already has `min-height:100dvh`). · mech

### App root, error page & plugins
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/plugins/pwa-rescue.client.ts:23-38` + `nuxt.config.ts:43`: every refocus (throttled to 60s) calls `reg.update()`. With `registerType:'autoUpdate'`, a new service worker activates and the page reloads straight away, so a half-written compose sheet, column scroll positions and open threads disappear mid-use. → Hold the reload until nothing is open (no compose sheet or dialog) or the tab is hidden, or show an "Update ready" toast with a Reload button. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/error.vue:35-45`: the crash page shows no status or message and has no way to report the problem. Yet `plugins/error-report.client.ts:3` keeps a log ring "for feedback" (`utils/feedback.ts:73`). → Add small "Error 500 · <message>" text and a "Copy details / Send report" action that includes `formatLogRingForFeedback()`. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/error.vue:36`: `role="alert"` sits on the whole card, buttons included, so screen readers read the eyebrow, title, description and both button labels as one assertive burst. → Put `role="alert"` on the title and description only (or focus the `<main>`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/error.vue:29-31`: the 404 card offers only "Back home". Someone who followed a stale in-app link loses their place. → Add a "Go back" button when `router.options.history.state.back` exists. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/error.vue:67`: `border-radius:16px` is hard-coded, so the user's Corners setting doesn't reach the error card. → `var(--neo-radius-lg)`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/error.vue:15,28` vs `app/components/NeoMark.vue:17,50` / `shell/MobileHeader.vue:38` / `DesktopSidebar.vue:213`: the brand is "NeoSpace" in titles, the error page and the install banner, but "neospace" in the wordmark and in aria-labels ("neospace home"). → Choose one spelling for spoken and visible names. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/app.vue:44-50`: scroll restore stops only on wheel, touchstart or keydown. Dragging the scrollbar, or a trackpad/mouse pointer on iPads (768–1023px, where `main` scrolls), fights the saved offset for up to 60 frames. → Also cancel on `pointerdown`. · mech

### DesktopSidebar (`app/components/shell/DesktopSidebar.vue`)
- [ ] [UX·M·M] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `DesktopSidebar.vue:519-527` + `MobileDrawer.vue:326-335` + `app/utils/appearance.ts:282-286`: old item 182 ("listbox popover that includes Auto") is marked fixed, but both shells still call one-way `cycleTheme()`. `nextTheme` never returns `'auto'`, so after one click you can't get back to Auto from the shell, and there's no way to step backwards through the themes. → A small listbox or menu of themes, including Auto. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:126-132,224-227`: on /notifications, both the Activity heart and Inbox show the active state and `aria-current="page"`, because `inboxActive` includes `/notifications` and the notifications column. That makes two "current page" items. → Limit `inboxActive` to messages plus `isMentions`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:133,305,313`: the Inbox badge falls back to the notification count (`messagesBadge || notifBadge`) and is announced as "Inbox, N unread". It duplicates the Activity badge, although Inbox only holds DMs and Mentions. When DMs are unread, the mentions count is hidden. → Badge the DM count (plus a mentions count if one is available), never the total notification count. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:664-668`: rail mode hides `.sidebar__section-action` and `.sidebar__more`. Algorithms after the first 5, groups after the first 5, "New algorithm" and "All groups" can't be reached in rail mode. → Keep icon versions ("+", "⋯ more") in rail, or a single overflow menu. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `DesktopSidebar.vue:485,522,532,542,552,562`: aria-labels still end in "Click to cycle." (old item 165), which assumes a mouse and is repeated on every row. Board density changes (`toggleDeskDensity`, line 487) are also never sent to the `lookAnnounce` live region (line 516). → Use stable names ("Board density"), keep the value in the text, and announce changes. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:241-243`: the rail toggle changes its aria-label ("Expand/Collapse sidebar") and also sets `aria-pressed`, so it reads as "Collapse sidebar, toggle button, pressed", which contradicts itself. → Use a stable label ("Compact sidebar") with `aria-pressed`, or `aria-expanded`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:255`: in rail mode the Home link is removed (`v-if="!sidebarRail"`). The "Main menu" nav landmark then has no Home item; only the logo (outside the nav, with no aria-current) goes home. → Render an icon-only Home link in rail. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:372-375,415` + `MobileDrawer.vue:235-241`: the "New" and "All" controls have accessible names "New" and "All". `title` isn't used as the name, so they're ambiguous out of context. → aria-label "New algorithm" and "All groups". · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:365,411,472,499`: four `<section aria-label>` elements create region landmarks (Algorithms, Groups, Board, Look) next to the nav, which is landmark noise. → Use `aria-labelledby` pointing at the h2 ids, or drop the labels. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:576-611` + `app/assets/css/main.scss:350-353`: the foot buttons have both `title` and `aria-label`, and main.scss draws a CSS tooltip from the aria-label. Hovering shows two tooltips (the CSS tip, then the native one after a delay). ChromeButton has the same problem (`ChromeButton.vue:26,36`, e.g. SubviewChrome's "Back"). → Drop `title` where `.neo-tip` or the foot-btn tooltip applies. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:384,424,447`: every row has a `title` that repeats its visible label, so expanded mode shows a native tooltip on every hover. → Set `title` only in rail mode or when the label is truncated. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:101,230,97,600,331,590`: icons mean different things in the same sidebar. The heart is both Activity and the "Liked" feed, `servers` is both Federated and "Accounts & servers", and `message` is both "Leave a note" and "Direct messages". → Give each its own glyph. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:472-495` vs `513-515,548-557`: "Board density" (Packed/Roomy/Focused) sits right above Look's "Density" (text spacing), so two different "density" controls are next to each other. The Look toggle also pairs "Try" with "Hide" (line 510). → Rename Look's control to "Spacing" and use "Show/Hide". · decision
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:286-295,319,350-359`: these use raw `route.path`, although the normalized `path` (line 31) exists, so trailing-slash URLs lose the active state. The Inbox icon's `filled` (line 319) checks only `route.path==='/messages'`, while its stroke uses `inboxActive`, so the fill and stroke disagree when the messages board column is active. → Use `path` and `inboxActive` throughout. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:652,684,765-791,930-971,1252-1262,1359-1365`: dead CSS. `.sidebar__brand`; `.sidebar__mark` (NeoMark renders `.neo-mark__glyph`, so the rail "logo on" ring at line 684 never applies); `__rail-btn`; `__micro*` (the old Inbox popover); `__theme-swatch` (now `<ThemeSwatch>`); `.nav-badge--inline`. → Delete them, and move the rail ring to `.neo-mark__glyph`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:96,384,390-393` vs `MobileDrawer.vue:76`: the label is `` `Local` ``, then the template special-cases `item.key==='local'` to print "Local" again (a dead branch). The drawer labels the same feed "Local (host)". → One label source shared by both shells. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:174-189` and `MobileTabBar.vue:57-72`: `openCompose` (login redirect plus group-tag inference) is copied verbatim in both. → Move it into `useComposeSheetStore`/`useBoardNav`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `DesktopSidebar.vue:24` + `MobileDrawer.vue:23`: `algorithmsStore.hydrate()` runs as a side effect in two components' setup. → Hydrate once at store init or in the layout. · mech

Both the logo and the Home `NuxtLink to="/"` get `aria-current="page"` (old item 169 was fixed only on desktop). → Use `<NeoMark>`, aria-label "NeoSpace home", and no exact-active state on the logo. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:175`: the drawer's Home is a plain NuxtLink. Unlike the tab bar and the header logo (`goHome`), when you're already on '/' it doesn't clear column focus or jump to the Home column. → Call `goHome()`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:168,453-455`: the close button is 36×36, below the 44px target on a touch-only surface. → 44×44. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:218-228,367-372`: guests see two "Sign in" calls to action in the same drawer (the guest card and the footer). → Keep one. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:121,130`: the sign-out confirm says "You can keep browsing public posts.", then immediately runs `router.push('/login')`. → Stay on the current page (or change the copy). · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:326-335`: cycling the theme in the drawer isn't announced. The `lookAnnounce` live region exists only in `DesktopSidebar.vue:516`. → Add an sr-only `aria-live="polite"` element bound to `lookAnnounce`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:243-257`: algorithm rows have no active state or `aria-current` (desktop has `sidebar__row--on`). - [ ] [UX·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:296-321`: Suggested groups always show in the drawer. Desktop shows them only when fewer than 4 groups are joined (`DesktopSidebar.vue:69-71`). → Same rule on both. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:357-360,799-804`: "Posting as · host" has a stray middot. The display name has no nowrap or ellipsis, so long names wrap onto several lines in the footer. → "Posting on host"; truncate the name. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·M] `MobileDrawer.vue:149-162`: the 280px drawer has no swipe-to-close (or edge-swipe-to-open) gesture; only tapping the overlay or the X closes it. → Track horizontal drag on the panel and close past a threshold (respecting reduced motion). · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `MobileDrawer.vue:840-892`, `MobileTabBar.vue:247-300,303-355`, `DesktopSidebar.vue:1340-1366`: `.nav-badge` is copied three times and `.chrome-hint` with its `@keyframes` twice. - [ ] [UX·L·M] `MobileTabBar.vue:76-184`: the tabs are icons only, with no visible labels. The heart means "Activity" here but "Liked" in the drawer and sidebar algorithm lists. → Decide on labels, or on distinct icons. · decision

### NeoMenu (`app/components/NeoMenu.vue`)
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `NeoMenu.vue:128-135` vs `98-109`: pressing ArrowUp on the trigger schedules `nextTick(() => last.focus())`. The `flush:'post'` watcher's `onOpen` then awaits its own `nextTick` (queued later) and focuses `filter || list[0]`, so focus ends on the first item; the APG pattern wants the last. → Pass a `focusLast` intent into `onOpen` instead of racing it. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `NeoMenu.vue:334-349`: the non-sheet panel uses `v-show`, so every menu's `#items` slot is fully rendered while closed. That includes the ⋯ menu on every post card, so a feed holds thousands of hidden buttons. → Render items with `v-if="open"` inside an always-present panel element (keeps `aria-controls` valid). · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:301,325`: sheet mode declares `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, but the popup is `role="menu"` with no `aria-modal`. → Make them match (`menu`). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:155-158`: `if (!list.length) return` runs before the Escape branch, so an empty or still-loading menu can't be closed with Escape. → Handle Escape and Tab first. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:65-96`: positioning clamps only right-edge overflow and flips upward. There's no max-height or scrolling when the menu fits neither below nor above (long menus on short landscape screens run off-screen), and no left-edge or RTL clamp. → Add `max-height: calc(100vh - 16px); overflow:auto` and a left clamp. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:308-333,414,455-462`: the sheet enters with an opacity fade only and has no exit animation (`v-if` removes it instantly). It feels abrupt next to NeoSheet's slide. → Wrap it in a `<Transition>` with a translateY slide. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:438-439`, `NeoSheet.vue:107,120,139`: sheet radii (16px/12px) and the rgba shadow are hard-coded, so the Corners setting doesn't reach sheets. → `var(--neo-radius-lg)` and shadow tokens. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `NeoMenu.vue:36`, `NeoTabs.vue:34`: ids come from `Math.random()`. → Use Vue's `useId()`. · mech

### NeoTabs (`app/components/NeoTabs.vue`)
- [ ] [CODE·L·M] `NeoTabs.vue:129-133`: the tab list has no overflow handling (flex with no wrap or scroll), so six consumers each re-implement it with `:deep(.neo-tabs__list)` (explore.vue:1154, profile.vue:1864, notifications.vue:840, FollowersModal.vue:428, AlgorithmSheet.vue:681, index.vue:2759). → Build in `overflow-x:auto; scrollbar-width:none` and keep the active tab scrolled into view. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `NeoTabs.vue:139,154-156`: hover switches the colour to `--neo-text-secondary`, which is lighter than the resting `--neo-text-tertiary` (tertiary is mute mixed 12% toward ink, `_themes.scss:48`). Hovering a tab lowers its contrast. → Use `--neo-text-primary` on hover. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `NeoTabs.vue:59-65`: ArrowRight always moves to the next tab, so the order is reversed under `dir="rtl"`. NeoMenu's `align` and the Back `chevron-left` (`SubviewChrome.vue:43`) don't account for RTL either. → Swap arrow handling based on the computed `direction`. · judgment

- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `NeoSheet.vue:84-88,135-147`: the centred-card layout requires `(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)`. Touch iPads at ≥1024px (a landscape iPad Pro is 1366px wide) get a bottom sheet stretched across the full width. → Cap `max-width` (e.g. 640px, centred) at ≥768px even on touch. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·M] `NeoSheet.vue:47-70`: the shared sheet primitive provides no `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, labelling, Escape handling or focus trap. All four consumers wire these up again themselves (e.g. `ComposeSheet.vue:128-133`, plus its own `useFocusTrap`). → Accept `titleId`/`onEscape` props and own the trap inside the primitive. · judgment

- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `NeoSheetHeader.vue:28-34`: the close button has `aria-label="Cancel"`, `title="Cancel"` and, on desktop, visible "Cancel" text, so the title adds a redundant native tooltip. → Drop `title`; drop the aria-label when the visible label shows. · mech

### NeoConfirm / overlay store
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/overlay.ts:128-155` + `MobileDrawer.vue:149-162` + `NeoMenu.vue:308`: Android and browser Back don't close the confirm dialog, the drawer, a NeoMenu sheet or the lightbox. Only FeedbackNotes pushes a history entry (`useFeedbackNotes.ts:48-53`). Back navigates the page underneath, and a confirm dialog stays open over the new page. → Add a shared "overlay history" helper (push on open, close on popstate). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/overlay.ts:134-137`: a second `openConfirm` silently resolves the pending one as `false`, so the first action is cancelled without the user choosing (e.g. two destructive confirms fired from different columns). → Queue confirms instead of replacing. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `NeoConfirm.vue:17-21`: there's no enter/leave transition, so the dialog pops in and out abruptly while every other overlay fades or slides. → `<Transition>` with opacity and scale (covered by the global reduced-motion rule). · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `NeoConfirm.vue:99-106` + `NeoReport.vue:222`: `.neo-btn--danger` isn't a global variant (main.scss only has `--primary/--secondary/--tertiary/--ghost`). Two local copies exist; hover is a barely visible `brightness(1.05)` and there are no `:disabled` or `:active` styles. → Add `&--danger` to `.neo-btn` in main.scss and delete the copies. · mech

### Toasts (`NeoToastHost.vue`, `stores/toast.ts`)
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/toast.ts:7-12,32-43`: toasts have no severity. Errors and confirmations look identical (no icon or colour) and are both announced politely. There's no cap or dedupe, so repeated failures (offline, rate limits) pile up across the screen. → Add `kind: 'info'|'success'|'error'`, cap the queue at 3, and merge identical messages. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:93-103`: the mobile bottom offset ignores `--neo-keyboard-inset` and the thread reply dock. A toast such as "Reply failed" renders under the iOS keyboard, or on top of the Send bar on /status (where `--neo-bottom-chrome-h` is 0). → Add `var(--neo-keyboard-inset, 0px)` and a reply-dock offset. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:31-42`: activating Undo or Dismiss removes the focused button, so focus drops to `<body>` and keyboard users lose their place. → Remember `document.activeElement` from before focus entered the toast and restore it on dismiss. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:83-91` vs `app/layouts/default.vue:406-409`: on desktop the toast stack sits in the same bottom-right corner as the bottom dock (SuiteMenu waffle + install banner) and covers it (z 1100 vs 90). → Offset it by the dock height or move toasts to bottom centre. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:98-101` vs `app/assets/css/_structural.scss:224-229`: the dock and notes FAB slide down with the hidden tab bar, but the toast host doesn't, so toasts float 56px above an empty strip. → Add a matching `html.neo-nav-hidden .neo-toast-host` translate. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/toast.ts:55-61`: timers keep running while the tab is hidden, so a 10s "Undo" expires unseen while the user is in another app. → Pause all toasts on `visibilitychange` and resume them on return. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:68-75`: the dismiss button contains a literal "×" inside the live region, and some screen readers say "times" with every toast. It also doesn't match the icon set. → `<NeoIcon name="x">` (aria-hidden). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `NeoToastHost.vue:120-123`: `.neo-toast__msg` has `min-width:0` but no `overflow-wrap`, so long handles or URLs overflow the toast. → `overflow-wrap:anywhere`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/toast.ts:70-76`: `clear()` is never called. Toasts, including Undo closures created under the previous account, survive an account switch or logout. → Clear toasts on active-account change and on logout. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/composables/useFocusTrap.ts:94-107`: the focusin guard pulls focus out of the toast host, so keyboard users can't reach a toast's Undo while any dialog is open (e.g. after "Remove follower" in FollowersModal). → Exempt `.neo-toast-host`, as teleported menus are exempted. · mech

### Focus trap, scroll lock, keyboard & viewport composables
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useScrollLock.ts:8-11` + `app/assets/css/main.scss:818-827`: the lock puts `overflow:hidden` on html/body with no scrollbar compensation. On desktops with classic scrollbars (Windows/Linux), the board and page shift sideways whenever a dialog or sheet opens or closes. → `html { scrollbar-gutter: stable }` at ≥1024px. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/composables/useFocusTrap.ts:25-30,74-78`: `isVisible` uses `checkVisibility({checkOpacity:true})`. During an enter transition (`NeoSheet.vue:159-162`, the drawer fade) every control counts as invisible, so Tab focuses the container, and the "first focusable" fallback at line 167 only works because every consumer passes `initialFocus`. → Drop `checkOpacity` (keep `visibility`/`display`). · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useKeyboardViewport.ts:30,33-38,261`: `lastOpen` is written but never read. `publishKeyboardOpen` sets the ref and toggles the html attribute on every visualViewport scroll, even when nothing changed. → Return early when the value is unchanged. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useKeyboardViewport.ts:251-256`: the 80ms `onInsetFocusOut` timer isn't cleared in `detachKeyboardInset`, so it re-publishes the inset after the last subscriber unmounts. → Track and clear the timer. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `useKeyboardViewport.ts:104-106`, `utils/keyboardDetect.ts:59-61`, `useAutoHideNav.ts:34-39,163`: a new `MediaQueryList` (one or two `matchMedia` calls) is created on every scroll or visualViewport event. → Reuse `useMobileViewport()` or cached MQLs. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/useAppShellHeight.ts:11-27`: `--neo-app-height` is rewritten on every visualViewport scroll frame, even when unchanged, invalidating style on `<html>` during iOS scrolling. → Cache the last value and skip identical writes. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useAutoHideNav.ts:174-185`: the tab bar only comes back on scroll-up or near the top. Reaching the end of a list leaves it hidden (and line 180 ignores the bounce there). → Show the bar when within about 24px of `scrollHeight`. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/usePrefersReducedMotion.ts:28-33`: the MutationObserver on `<html>` `class` fires on every shell class flip (`neo-nav-hidden` on each scroll-direction change, `neo-dialog-open`, `neo-route-board`) just to watch `reduce-motion`. → Have the settings store push the flag directly instead of observing. · judgment

### Install banner / standalone
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `InstallAppBanner.vue:56-58` + `app/composables/useInstallApp.ts:73-90`: "Not now" dismisses the banner permanently (localStorage, forever), and `clearDismiss()` is never called anywhere, so the install steps can't be brought back. → Rename it "Don't show again", or expire the dismissal after N days, and add an "Install app" entry in Settings. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useInstallApp.ts:48-51`: iPadOS 13+ reports a Mac user agent, so `isIos` is false and iPads get no promo or steps. → Also match `/Macintosh/` with `navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/useInstallApp.ts:29,53-55` vs `useStandaloneShell.ts:6-18`: the promo checks only `(display-mode: standalone)`, not minimal-ui, fullscreen or `navigator.standalone`. The manifest's `display_override` includes minimal-ui, so the banner can show inside the installed app. → Reuse `STANDALONE_MQ` and the `navigator.standalone` check. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `InstallAppBanner.vue:29`: "Chrome may hide Install in the menu — tap below for steps." On wider screens the button sits beside the text, not below it, and the sentence reads like an error. → "Add it from Chrome's ⋮ menu — see how." · mech

### SuiteMenu
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/layouts/default.vue:394-404` + `SuiteMenu.vue:187-194`: on phones, the 44px waffle floats permanently over the right edge of the content above the tab bar on every page except threads. It covers right-aligned controls (post ⋯ menus, counts) as you scroll. → Move the suite menu into the drawer at ≤1023px. · decision
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `SuiteMenu.vue:197,210,244,296,328-331`: the 2px radii and 0.625rem (10px) tile blurbs are still there (old item 238 asked for an 11px minimum and radius tokens). → `max(0.6875rem, 11px)` and `var(--neo-radius-sm)`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `SuiteMenu.vue:142-155`: the "current" neospace tile is still a link to https://neospace.ibm.io/, so clicking it reloads the whole app and loses open columns and drafts. → Render the current tile as non-interactive (or as close-only). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `SuiteMenu.vue:24-34`: the extra "play" group shows "edward" with the blurb "stream · balls", which tells a new user nothing about what clicking it does (it takes over the screen). → Clearer label and blurb. · decision

### FunLoader / sharedRaf / icons / time
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `FunLoader.vue:490,499-504`: every instance is `role="status"` with "Loading", so a board with five loading columns queues five identical announcements. → Only primary loaders get `role=status`; columns use aria-hidden plus `aria-busy` on the feed. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `FunLoader.vue:382-389,446-450`: with reduced motion on, the canvas redraws only at mount and when the setting toggles. ResizeObserver resizes (`applySize`) and theme changes don't redraw, leaving a stretched or wrongly coloured still frame. → Call `drawStill()` after `applySize` when motion is reduced. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `FunLoader.vue:25-26,37-41` + `TimelineColumn.vue:1747`, `FollowersModal.vue:259`, `ProfileInsights.vue:250`: three callers still use the deprecated `fill` prop. → Migrate them to `variant="region"` and delete the prop. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/sharedRaf.ts:8-16`: if one subscriber throws, the loop dies before it re-requests a frame, and every loader freezes. → Wrap each callback in try/catch. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/neoIcons.ts:49-59` + `NeoIcon.vue:56`: an unknown name (dynamic strings bypass the type) makes `resolveIcon` return undefined, and NeoIcon throws during render, blanking the parent. → Fall back to `() => []` with a dev warning. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/relativeTime.ts:60-63,97`: `Math.round` at unit boundaries gives "60 seconds ago", "24 hours ago", "7 days ago" and the compact forms "60m" and "24h" instead of moving to the next unit. → Use `Math.floor` (or compare against the rounded value). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/relativeTime.ts:89-100`: the compact form clamps future dates to zero elapsed, so a scheduled post (or one with clock skew over 60s) shows "now". → Show a short date for future times, or "in 3d". · mech

### SubviewChrome / ChromeButton / overlay host
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `ChromeButton.vue:21-30`: `disabled` is ignored on the `to` (NuxtLink) variant, so a disabled link button stays clickable and looks active. → Render a `<button disabled>` (or `aria-disabled` + `pointer-events:none`) when `disabled`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `NeoOverlayHost.vue:8-11`: `NeoReport` and `NeoLightbox` are imported and mounted eagerly on every boot, although they only render when opened (`NeoLightbox.vue:108`). → Use `LazyNeoReport`/`LazyNeoLightbox` behind a sticky v-if, as the layout does for its sheets. · mech
