## Profile, Explore & Search

### app/pages/profile.vue — identity, header & relationship
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/pages/profile.vue:620` — The profile ⋯ menu only has Share, Copy handle, Open on instance and the presence links. There's no Mute, Block, Report, domain block, Unmute or Unblock. The chips at `:242-244` say "Muted" or "Blocked", but the page gives no way to undo either. → Add relationship actions to the `NeoMenu`, using the Relationship the server returns to update the state and `openConfirm` for Block and Report. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* Confirmed: the profile menu has only Share, Copy handle, Open on instance and links, with no Mute, Block or Report. But "no way to undo" is overstated: Settings moderation has unmute and unblock (`settings.ts:1026,1044`). [Corrected: "The profile page can show Muted/Blocked chips but offers no mute, block, report, unmute or unblock. Undo is only available in Settings." Impact Medium-High.]
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:691` — `viewedProfile.moved` is never read. A migrated account shows as a normal profile with an active Follow button, and nothing says "This account has moved to @new". → Add a banner with a link to `moved.acct`, and demote the Follow button to secondary. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:691` — `limited` (hidden by moderators) and `suspended` accounts aren't handled. A suspended remote account shows "No posts yet." and a working Follow button. → Show a "Suspended" or "Hidden by your server's moderators" state with a click-through. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:237` — The relationship chips ignore `blockedBy`, and the Follow button (`:933`) still says "Follow" while you're blocking or blocked. Clicking it fails with a generic toast. → Add a "Blocked you" chip, and swap Follow for Unblock (or disable it) when `blocking` or `blockedBy` is set. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:38` — `INTERNAL_FIELD_NAMES` keeps underscores (`custom_css`, `chaos_css`, `neospace_columns`), but `:96` compares them against `normalizeFieldKey()`, which strips every non-alphanumeric character. Those fields are never filtered out, so raw CSS or column JSON shows up as a profile chip. → Store the normalized keys (`customcss`, `chaoscss`, `neospacecolumns`) or normalize the set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:38` — Any field labelled "Theme" or "Style" (for example "Style: Jazz") is hidden from viewers and handed to the CSS sanitizer (`stores/profile.ts:120`). → Only treat a field as CSS when its value looks like CSS (contains `{`), or reserve the namespaced `neospace_css` key. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:97` — Every presence-like field is hidden from the field tokens, but `buildPresenceLinks` (`utils/profileSources.ts:248`) keeps only one link per kind. A second "Homepage" or "Bluesky" field disappears for viewers, and so does a "Website"/"URL"/"Site" field whose value isn't a link (for example "coming soon"). → Show any presence field that didn't become a chip as a normal token. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:813` — The verified ✓ only renders on non-presence tokens. A Website field that Mastodon verified via `rel=me` (the most common verified field) loses its checkmark when it moves to the presence chips (`:853`). → Carry `verifiedAt` into `PresenceLink` and render the ✓ on the chip. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:210` — `softwareLabel` guesses the software from the profile URL's hostname (`/gotosocial|gts\./`). Almost every profile is labelled "MASTODON", including Akkoma, Misskey and Pixelfed hosts. → Read nodeinfo or `instanceInfo`, or drop the badge. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:217` — For local accounts, `fullHandle` uses the hostname of `p.url`, which is the web domain. On servers with `WEB_DOMAIN` ≠ `LOCAL_DOMAIN` it copies a handle that doesn't resolve. → Use the active account's handle domain or the instance `uri`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:103` — `joinDate` hard-codes `'en-US'` and formats a UTC-midnight `createdAt` in the local time zone, so remote accounts created on the 1st show as "Joined February" anywhere west of UTC. → Use `timeZone: 'UTC'` and the user's locale. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:495` — When following a locked account, the optimistic update sets `following: true`. The button flashes "Following" and then flips to "Requested". → Set `requested: viewedProfile.locked, following: !locked` optimistically. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:430` — `unfollowUser` lowers `followersCount` even when it only withdrew a pending request, so cancelling "Requested" drops the follower count by one. → Only decrement when the previous relationship was `following`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:92` — `isOwnProfile` compares the active account's id with the viewed profile's id. With `?account=B` (`profile.vue:59`, used by notification links), your own B profile shows Follow and Message instead of Edit, and ids from different servers are compared directly. → Compare against the read-override account's user, by acct and host. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:64` — When `route.query.account` changes, the read override updates but the profile isn't reloaded. The relationship and statuses stay from the previous account. (`AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:59` also calls `fetchProfile()` directly, skipping relationship, tab and CSS sync.) → Watch `[user, account]` and always go through `loadProfileFromRoute`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:183` — When `accounts.lookup` fails, the store takes the first fuzzy `v2.search` hit with no exact-match check. `/profile?user=bob` can open "bobby@elsewhere". → Accept the hit only if `acct` equals the request (case-insensitive, with or without the local domain); otherwise show "User not found". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:588` — The store keeps only `e.message`, and the page wraps it in `new Error(...)`, so the HTTP status is lost. A 404 ("User not found" or "Record not found") shows "Something went wrong" with a Retry button. → Store the original error (or its status) and map 404 to "Account not found" with no Retry. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:373` — If the user cancels the native share sheet (AbortError), the code falls through to the clipboard and shows "Profile link copied". It also re-imports a toast store that's already imported at `:35`. → Return on `AbortError`, and use `toastStore` directly. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:380` — The share text is `@${p.acct}`, which for local accounts has no domain. → Use `fullHandle.value`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:396` — `messageUser` has no try/catch, so a failed direct-conversation lookup or compose open is an unhandled rejection with no feedback. → Catch the error and toast `mapErrorToMessage`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:196` — The header and avatar (`:207`) always use the animated `header` and `avatar`, ignoring both the app's `reduceMotion` setting and `prefers-reduced-motion`. → Use `headerStatic` and `avatarStatic` when reduced motion is on. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:794` — Bio links go through `sanitizeStatusHtml` with `target=_blank`, so @mentions and #hashtags in a bio open the remote instance in a new tab instead of `/profile` or `/groups`. The same happens for mention links in fields (`:104`). → Intercept `.mention` and `.hashtag` clicks the way `RealPostCard.vue:708` does. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:257` — `chromeTitle`, the `useHead` title (`:604`) and the unfollow confirm (`:497`) use the raw `displayName`, so custom emoji appear as `:blobcat:` shortcodes. → Strip shortcodes for plain-text uses and emojify where HTML is allowed. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1499` — `.profile-chip` sets `text-transform: lowercase`, which also applies to the relationship chips, so they render "follows you" and "domain blocked" while the same chips elsewhere are Title Case. The bot chip is lowercase "bot" (`:744`), "BOT" in `FollowersModal.vue:304` and "Bot" in `SearchPersonRow.vue:59`. → Use one sentence-case style everywhere. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:748` — Locked accounts are labelled `title="Private"` / "Private account". `SearchPersonRow.vue:53` calls the same state "Approves followers", and the edit toggle says "Require follow approval". → Use one phrase ("Approves followers") everywhere. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:664` — The "Linked. Tap your name to switch." banner has no dismiss and stays as long as `?linked=1` is in the URL. → Strip the query after showing it once, or add a close button. · mech

### app/pages/profile.vue — tabs, posts & editing
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/pages/profile.vue:1060` — On your own profile there are six `flex:1` tabs with about 24px padding each (Posts…Insights). At 375px they're wider than the column, and because `main` is `overflow-x:hidden` on mobile, "Human" and "Insights" are clipped off-screen and can't be tapped. → Make the tablist horizontally scrollable, as Explore does at `explore.vue:1247`, or move Human and Insights into the ⋯ menu. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/pages/profile.vue:323` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: Posts and Reposts still filter one 20-item page on the client (`stores/profile.ts:246`) and don't auto-page. A heavy booster's Posts tab says "No posts yet." with a Load more button underneath. → Auto-page until N matches or the end is reached, or request `exclude_reblogs:true` for Posts. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:248` — Status fetch errors are only logged, and `hasMoreStatuses` stays `true`. A failed load shows "No posts yet." plus "Load more" instead of an error with Retry. → Add a `statusesError` state and render it in `profile.vue:1142`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1142` — The empty state can't tell why there are no posts. A locked account you don't follow, or a remote account whose older posts never federated, shows "No posts yet." → Use "Only approved followers can see posts" when `locked && !following`, and add an "Older posts may be on {host}" link for remote accounts. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1121` — Pinned posts render at the top with no "Pinned" heading or badge (`RealPostCard` has no pinned indicator), so an old pin looks like the newest post. → Add a "Pinned" section label or a per-card pin badge. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:312` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `tabFetchOpts` and `optsForTab` (`:351`) duplicate the tab→options mapping, and Load more (`:1162`) and compose `onPosted` (`:307`) use the other copy. → Delete `tabFetchOpts` and use `optsForTab(profileTab.value)`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:413` — Navigating A→B resets the tab to Posts but keeps the "Search posts…" text, so B's posts are filtered by A's query. → Clear `postsQuery` in `loadProfileFromRoute`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:713` — While editing, the display-name input and the old `<h1>` name both render, so the name appears twice and the heading doesn't update as you type. → Hide the `<h1>` while `isEditing`, or bind it to the draft. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:905` — The save button says "Done", Cancel (`:907`) stays enabled while saving, there's no success toast, and navigating away silently discards unsaved edits. → Rename it "Save", disable Cancel while `isUpdating`, toast "Profile saved", and add an `onBeforeRouteLeave` guard when the form is dirty. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1032` — The remove-field button is a bare "✕" text glyph, while every other close button uses `NeoIcon name="x"`. → Swap in `NeoIcon`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:926` — "View insights" switches the tab but doesn't scroll, so on phones nothing visible changes above the fold. → Scroll `#profile-tab-panel` into view after the switch. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1080` — The "Search posts…" placeholder implies a server search, but it only filters loaded posts. The empty message (`:1146`) admits this only after a miss. → Change the placeholder to "Find in loaded posts…". · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:1199` — `.profile-handle__acct` is defined twice (also `:1488`) with conflicting colors. `.profile-loading__spinner` and `@keyframes spin` (`:1345-1352`), `.profile-chrome-more` (`:1671`) and `.profile-error span { font-size: 3rem }` (`:1312`) are dead. → Delete them. · mech

### app/stores/profile.ts & app/utils/profileSources.ts
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:294` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `initEditForm` builds the bio and fields with `stripHtml`, which collapses whitespace (`stripHtml.ts:1`) and joins `<p>a</p><p>b</p>` into "ab". Saving any multi-paragraph bio flattens it, and `@user@remote` mentions are saved as `@user`. → Fill the form from `verifyCredentials().source.note` and `source.fields`, which hold the raw plain text. · mech ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/profile.ts:124` — `profileCustomCSS` returns the server-rendered HTML field value. Mastodon entity-encodes `>`, `"` and `'` and linkifies URLs, and `sanitizeProfileCss` strips tags but not entities, so child combinators and quoted strings in profile skins become `&gt;` / `&quot;` and the rules are dropped. → Decode with `stripHtml` (DOMParser `textContent`) before sanitizing. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/utils/profileSources.ts:86` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: any `http(s)` value returns early, so the seenu.io host check at `:103-110` can never run. A field named "SeenU" (or "Bluesky") pointing at `https://phish.example` renders a "SeenU"/"Bluesky" chip with that network's icon (`profile.vue:887`). → Check the host for the bluesky and seenu kinds before the generic URL branch, and fall back to `website` otherwise. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/profileSources.ts:97` — Bluesky handles aren't validated or encoded, so "my bsky is cool" becomes `https://bsky.app/profile/my bsky is cool`. → Validate against a domain-handle pattern and `encodeURIComponent`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/profileSources.ts:120` — The javascript/data/vbscript check is a dead duplicate of `:85`. → Remove it. · mech

### app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts — profile skin sanitizer
- [ ] [SEC·H·S] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:78` — The position rewrite only fires when `position` follows `^`, `;`, `{` or whitespace. The blocklist replacements on `:49-55` insert `/* blocked … */` markers that end in `/`, so `a{javascript:position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483647}` becomes `/* blocked javascript: */position:fixed`, which isn't rewritten. The browser drops the comment and applies `position:fixed`, and a full-screen spoof overlay escapes the scope. → Run the position rewrite before inserting markers, and also match after `*/` (`(^|[;{\s]|\*\/)`). Better: rewrite each declaration in `scopeCss` with a declaration-level parser. Add a regression test. · mech ✓
- [ ] [SEC·H·M] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:30` — `position:absolute` is allowed, and `#main-content` is `position:static` with no `contain` (`layouts/default.vue:424`). An absolutely positioned descendant with `top:0;left:0;width:100vw;height:100vh;z-index:99999` therefore covers the sidebar, tab bar and dialogs. `scopeSelectors` (`:148`) also turns an empty selector part (`, x{}`) into a rule on `#main-content` itself. → Inject `.chaos-active #main-content{position:relative;contain:paint;isolation:isolate;z-index:0}` with the skin, drop empty parts, and clamp `z-index`. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:143` — Splitting selectors on raw commas breaks `:is(a, b)` and `:where(…)` into `.scope :is(a, .scope b)`, silently changing what legitimate skins match. → Split only at top-level commas (track paren depth). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·M] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:186` — `@keyframes`, `@container` and `@font-face` are dropped, so any animated chaos skin silently stops animating. → Keep `@keyframes` with a per-profile name prefix (and rewrite matching `animation-name` values). · judgment
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/utils/sanitizeCss.ts:61` — `element()` / `-moz-element(#id)` aren't blocked. In Firefox a skin can paint live app elements (another column, the compose box) as a background to fake UI. → Strip `-?moz-element(` and `element(`. · mech

### app/components/ProfileInsights.vue, app/stores/insights.ts, app/utils/insights.ts, app/utils/loomExport.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/insights.ts:201` — Followers-only posts are excluded from your own charts as well as from export (also `utils/insights.ts:191`). For people who mostly post followers-only, every KPI reads near zero. → Include `private` in the on-screen report and filter only the CSV/Loom payload, or add a toggle. · decision
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/insights.ts:198` — The "Post types" waffle counts boosts (`:303`), but the "Posts" KPI, the heatmap, the daily posts and the attribute percentages use `countable` (no boosts). The numbers on one screen disagree, for example 10 posts in the KPI against a waffle of 12. → Either exclude boosts from the waffle or label the KPI "Posts (excl. boosts)" and show a boosts count. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/insights.ts:118` — `stripTags` decodes only five named entities. Mastodon encodes apostrophes as `&#39;`, so Top-post previews and the CSV `preview` column show "it&#39;s". → Use `stripHtml` (DOMParser) or decode numeric entities. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/insights.ts:152` — The preview is the post body even when it's behind a content warning, so Top posts reveal CW'd text and media-only posts read "(no text)" (`ProfileInsights.vue:451`). → Prefer `spoilerText` when set, and fall back to "Photo / video post" or "Poll". · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/stores/insights.ts:168` — Every load fetches 90 days (up to 12 × 40 statuses) even for the default 30d or 7d view, so the first paint waits on the largest window. → Fetch the requested window first, render, then backfill to 90d in the background for the cache. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/insights.ts:173` — The `AbortController` signal is never passed to `statuses.list(...)` (and the params are cast `as any`), so `abortInFlight()` doesn't cancel the network requests. → Pass `{ requestInit: { signal } }` as `useFediverseSearch.ts:375` does, and drop the cast. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/insights.ts:192` — The 5-minute cache isn't invalidated when you post or delete, and there's no refresh control, so a just-published post is missing from Insights. → Add a Refresh button that clears `statusCache`, or invalidate the cache from compose `onPosted`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ProfileInsights.vue:163` — Plurals are wrong: "· 1 posts" in the heat summary, `title="… — 1 posts"` (`:366`), "N statuses" (`:494`, which also says "statuses" where the rest of the app says "posts") and "followers" for 1. The hour also shows as "9:00" in titles but "09:00" in the summary. → Pluralize, say "posts", and use one hour format. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ProfileInsights.vue:465` — The tag meta reads "3 · 1.2K eng", where "eng" is unexplained jargon and the bare "3" has no unit. → Use "3 posts · 1.2K engagement". · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/ProfileInsights.vue:189` — Each kind's waffle cells are rounded separately, so the cells can total 99 (one grey "empty" cell at 100% coverage), and the legend percentages (`:416`) can sum to 99 or 101. → Use largest-remainder rounding. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/loomExport.ts:88` — "Download CSV" builds one file that stacks three tables with different headers and `# name` comment lines. It isn't valid CSV, so Excel and Sheets show mixed columns. → Zip the three files, or offer three separate downloads. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/loomExport.ts:165` — After 8 seconds the message listener is removed and the CSVs auto-download. If Loom loads slowly, the user ends up with an empty Loom tab plus an unexpected download, and a late "ready" is ignored. → Keep the listener (and the payload) alive for the tab's lifetime, and show "Loom is slow — download instead?" rather than auto-downloading. · judgment

### app/components/FollowersModal.vue
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:313` — There's no self check. When your own account appears in someone's Followers or Following list, it gets a "Follow"/"Follow back" button and a Mute/Block menu for yourself. The same applies to `SearchPersonRow.vue:66`. → Hide the actions when `account.id === currentUser.id`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:80` — Relationships are fetched fire-and-forget after the rows render. Every row first shows "Follow back"/"Follow" and then flips to "Following", and a quick tap re-follows. → Render a neutral, disabled button until `relationships[id]` arrives. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:136` — Unfollowing from the list happens on one tap with no confirmation, while the profile page confirms (`profile.vue:498`). → Reuse `openConfirm` for unfollow. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:88` — `loadError` shows the raw `err.message` (for example "Record not found" or a fetch TypeError), even though `mapErrorToMessage` is already imported in this file. → Map the error to friendly copy. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:270` — For remote accounts the server only knows local followers, so "12.4K followers" opens a list of three with no explanation. → When the account is remote, add "Only followers known to {your server} are shown" and an "Open on {host}" link. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:367` — When `lastStatusAt` is missing (GoToSocial and Akkoma often omit it), rows say "No recent posts", which is a claim the app can't support. → Show nothing when the field is absent. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:303` — Display names render raw, so custom emoji show as `:shortcode:`. The same happens in `ProfilePeekCard.vue:61` and `SearchPersonRow.vue:25`. → Emojify via `sanitizeDisplayName(emojify(...))` as `profile.vue:79` does. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:183` — Block and Remove follower (`:204`) drop the row but don't update the profile's follower/following counts, and there's no success toast. → Decrement `profileStore.viewedProfile` counts when it's your own list, and toast "Blocked @x". · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:380` — "Loading..." uses three dots while the rest of the app uses "Loading…", and the overlay color is hard-coded `rgba(0,0,0,0.75)` (`:394`). → Use the ellipsis and the scrim token. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/FollowersModal.vue:66` — `void signal` drops the pager's AbortSignal, so closing or switching tabs never cancels the request. `useLinkedProfileFeed.ts:78` does the same. → Pass `{ requestInit: { signal } }` through `$raw`/`list`. · mech

### app/components/ProfilePeekCard.vue & ProfileHumanBadges.vue
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ProfilePeekCard.vue:71` — Stats always use plurals ("1 followers", "1 posts"). → Pluralize. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ProfilePeekCard.vue:49` — The avatar's `alt` repeats the `<h3>` name right next to it, so screen readers announce the name twice. → Use `alt=""`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/ProfilePeekCard.vue:57` — `.slice(0, 1)` splits surrogate pairs, so a name starting with an emoji renders a broken glyph in the placeholder. → Use `Array.from(name)[0]` or `clipGraphemes`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ProfileHumanBadges.vue:39` — On someone else's profile the tab publicly says "This account is marked as a bot (or looks like one)" based on a name heuristic (`edwardSemantics.ts:194` matches surnames like "Mia Bot"). → Only show this when `account.bot` is true. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/profile.vue:277` — The "Human" tab appears on every profile with the same eight "soon" placeholder cards and nothing specific to the person. → Hide it on other people's profiles until badges exist, or move it to Settings. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ProfileHumanBadges.vue:71` — The microcopy "Settings → Humans first — why this exists." is awkward, and screen readers read the arrow as "right arrow". The `opacity:0.92` on the cards (`:135`) also lowers the contrast of the already-muted 11px text. → Use "Why this exists" as the link text and drop the opacity. · mech

### app/pages/explore.vue
- [ ] [CODE·H·M] `app/pages/explore.vue:528` — Discovery only resets when `isSignedIn` changes, and the search watcher (`useFediverseSearch.ts:538`) doesn't watch `activeAccountId`. After switching accounts with Explore open, results, suggestions and relationships still come from account A, and Follow calls `activeClient()` (B) with A's account ids, which fails or targets the wrong account. → Watch `instancesStore.activeAccountId`: `discovery.reset()`, `clearResults()`, clear `relationships`/`followedTags`, and re-run. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useFediverseSearch.ts:407` — Every new search sets `relationships = {}`, but suggestions only get relationships when `discovery.suggestedPeople` changes (`explore.vue:193`). After a search and Clear, the People empty state shows "Follow" on people you already follow. → Call `ensureRelationships(discovery.suggestedPeople)` when the query clears, or don't wipe relationships for ids that are still shown. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useFediverseSearch.ts:485` — `loadMore` pages `opts.query.value` (the text in the box) rather than `resultsFor`. Typing while scrolled to the sentinel appends page 2 of the new query, at the old offset, onto the old results. → Page `resultsFor.value`, and skip while the debounce timer is pending. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useFediverseSearch.ts:473` — A failed load-more just sets `searchHasMore=false`, so results end silently with no error or Retry. → Add a `loadMoreError` with an inline "Couldn't load more · Retry" in place of the sentinel at `explore.vue:1072`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:108` — `hasQuery` requires 2+ characters (also `useFediverseSearch.ts:307` and `useRecentSearches.ts:48`). Single-character CJK searches like "猫" or "東" never run. → Allow 1 character when the query contains a CJK, Hangul or Kana code point. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/searchQuery.ts:19` — `HOST_RE` treats "node.js", "vue.js" or "asp.net" as a hostname. On All this shows a "Couldn't reach node.js" server card (`explore.vue:961`), and Enter (`explore.vue:398`) runs a server lookup instead of a search. → Only claim host intent on Enter when the TLD is in a known list or the instance probe succeeds; otherwise search. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/searchQuery.ts:15` — `URL_RE` requires a scheme, so pasting "mastodon.social/@user/1234" (common from mobile share sheets) is treated as text and never resolved. → Accept a `host/path` shape, prefix `https://` and resolve. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/pages/explore.vue:671` — The filter chips (Mine, Media, Polls, Links, No replies, date) and the operator tips (`:831`) are offered on every server. On GoToSocial, Akkoma or Mastodon without Elasticsearch, `from:me has:media` is sent as literal words and returns nothing. → Gate the chips on the server's full-text capability (from `instanceInfo` or a first probe) and explain when they're unavailable. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/searchQuery.ts:79` — `isoDay` uses UTC, and Mastodon's `after:` is exclusive by date, so "Past day" returns only posts from today (UTC), not the last 24 hours. Late in the evening west of UTC it can return nothing. → Use `after:` with the local date minus N+1 days. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/searchQuery.ts:106` — The "Past week" tip's `after:` date is computed once at module load, so a long-lived SPA tab inserts a stale date. "In English" is hard-coded to `language:en` (`:105`). → Compute tips in a computed/getter, and use the user's UI language. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/composables/useFediverseSearch.ts:187` — The module-level cache key uses the server URL, not the account. Two linked accounts on the same server share cached results for `from:me` or "People I follow", so account B sees A's posts and followings. → Include `activeAccountId` in `cacheKey`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:277` — The count reads "16 people+ for “x”". → Put the plus on the number ("16+ people"). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:768` — "Trending topics" falls back to the hard-coded `STARTER_TOPICS` (`:48`) when trends are disabled or empty, so made-up topics are labelled "Trending". → Title the section "Topics to explore" when the chips aren't from trends. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:813` — The People and Tags (`:869`) empty states have no "nothing here" copy when suggestions, directory or trends are disabled, leaving a heading with nothing under it. If `sourceHost` is empty, the heading also reads "Active on " (`:810`). → Add an empty line ("Your server doesn't share trends") and a fallback host label. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:953` — `registrations:false` is described as "Invite or approval only", but closed servers aren't approval-based, and `approvalRequired` is ignored. The Join fallback `/auth/sign_up` (`:150`) is Mastodon-only. → Use three states (Open, Approval required, Closed), and hide Join without a `registrationsUrl` on non-Mastodon servers. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:780` — Trending news links, Join (`:957`) and the hosting links (`:1101`) use `rel="noopener"` without `noreferrer`, so they leak the Explore URL (including `?q=`) to third parties, unlike the rest of the app. → Add `noreferrer`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:1084` — The "Get started" help block renders under every result list too, and its "Watch — Public timelines, no account." card has no action. → Show it only in the empty state, and link Watch to the server preview. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:1072` — The infinite-scroll sentinel is a `role=status` live region that announces "Scroll for more" each time it mounts, and keyboard-only users get no button. → Make it a "Load more" button that the observer also triggers, and announce only "Loaded N more". · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:1374` — The server-grid `TransitionGroup` slide and the `#explore-results` opacity transition (`:1687`) ignore reduced motion; only the search sweep is guarded (`:1575`). → Wrap them in `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:1630` — Filter chips are 32px tall and the clear button is 2rem (`:1588`), below the 44px touch target used elsewhere, such as `FollowersModal.vue:561`. → Raise them to 44px under `(pointer: coarse)`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:882` — The category filter uses a `<nav>` landmark for a toggle-button group, which adds a misleading "Categories navigation" landmark. → Use `role="group"`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/explore.vue:1314` — `.explore-discover` is dead CSS, and the local `.sr-only` (`:1536`) re-declares the global one without `white-space:nowrap`. → Delete both. · mech

### app/components/search/*.vue
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/search/SearchPersonRow.vue:57` — The chip says "Mutuals" (plural) for a single person. → Use "Mutual". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/search/SearchPersonRow.vue:38` — Locked accounts show "Request" here but "Follow" on the profile page (`profile.vue:492`) and in FollowersModal (`:332`). → Pick one label for locked accounts across all three. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/search/SearchPersonRow.vue:33` — `lastStatusAt` is a calendar date, but `formatCompactRelativeTime` parses it as UTC midnight, so someone active today shows "active 14h". → Show "active today", "yesterday" or "N days ago" from a `formatDateOnly`-style date. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/search/SearchTagRow.vue:24` — Tags with no `history` (GoToSocial, Akkoma, freshly resolved tags) say "No posts this week", which is a false claim. → Show nothing when `history` is absent. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/search/SearchTagRow.vue:42` — The button combines `aria-pressed="true"` with `aria-label="Unfollow #x"`, so screen readers hear "Unfollow #x, pressed". → Keep a static label ("Follow #x") with `aria-pressed`, or drop `aria-pressed`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/utils/searchQuery.ts:180` — Explore's `formatCount` always uses compact notation in the user's locale, while the profile and insights `formatCompact` (`utils/insights.ts:401`) uses `'en'` and compacts only at 10,000 and above. The same account shows "1.2K followers" in search and "1,234 followers" on its profile. → Use one formatter app-wide. · mech

### Composables: discovery, recents, account search, pagers
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/composables/useAccountSearch.ts:48` — The Following list is paged with `maxId = last account id`. Follow lists paginate by follow-row id from the Link header (`utils/linkHeader.ts:4`), so after the first 80, people are skipped or duplicated in the mention and DM picker. → Use `cursorPage(...list.$raw(...))` like `FollowersModal.vue:74`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/composables/useAccountSearch.ts:30` — `loadFollowing` returns early once anything is cached, without checking which account it belongs to, so after an account switch the picker suggests A's followings while composing as B. → Key the cache by `activeAccountId` and reload on change. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/useAccountSearch.ts:85` — `resolve` is set whenever the query contains "@", so typing "alice@mast" fires a remote WebFinger lookup for each partial domain. → Resolve only when the host part matches a complete-domain pattern. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/composables/usePager.ts:92` — For plain-array pages, `hasMore` stays true after a short final page, so `useLinkedProfileFeed` shows "Load more" past the end and an extra empty request runs. → Pass the page size and set `hasMore = items.length >= limit`, as `useAccountPager.ts:49` does. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useSearchDiscovery.ts:69` — `fetchSlice` has no generation guard. After `reset()` on a sign-in change, an in-flight request from the previous source still writes its suggestions and trends. Failed slices aren't memoized either (`:72`), so a server with trends disabled is re-hit on every tab flip. → Add a generation token, and memoize failures briefly. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/composables/useRecentSearches.ts:36` — Recent searches are kept per account in localStorage but aren't pruned when that account is logged out (only `clearLocalDeviceData` wipes them), and there's no setting to turn history off. Every tapped topic is also recorded (`explore.vue:426`). → Delete the bucket in `logoutInstance`, and add a "Remember searches" toggle. · decision
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useRecentSearches.ts:37` — The store is read once per mount and written whole, so two open tabs overwrite each other's history. `remove` (`:53`) is case-sensitive while `record` dedupes case-insensitively. → Re-read before writing (or listen for `storage` events), and compare case-insensitively. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/composables/useLinkedProfileFeed.ts:84` — The params are cast `as any`, and the errors at `:119` and `:132` surface the raw `e.message`. → Type the params, and map errors with `mapErrorToMessage`. · mech

## Threads, notifications & direct messages

### app/pages/status/[id].vue — thread & DM chat view
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:930` — The find bar is rendered with `v-if="… && !keyboardOpen"`, and `keyboardOpen` is the shared flag that flips for *any* focused text field. On a phone, tapping "Search conversation…" opens the keyboard, which unmounts the input mid-focus. The keyboard then closes and the bar comes back, so search can't be used on touch. → Only hide the bar when the focused element is inside a `[data-keyboard-fixed]` dock (e.g. `!document.activeElement?.closest('.thread-search')`), or switch to `v-show` and keep it while it has focus. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:78-81` — The chat is `ancestors + focus + descendants` of the post that was opened. The inbox opens `lastStatus` (`messages.vue:93`, `dmHelpers.ts:39`), and Mastodon's context only returns that post's parent chain. In a group DM where two people answered the same message, the other branch is never shown. → After the first fetch, if `ancestors[0]` exists, fetch the root's context, then de-duplicate and show the whole tree. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:78-81,416` — `chatMessages` uses the server's depth-first descendant order, not chronological order. Branched DMs therefore show messages out of time order, and `replyTarget = chatMessages.at(-1)` may not be the newest message. → Sort `chatMessages` with `compareId` (or `createdAt`) before rendering and picking the reply target. · mech
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1145-1146` — The mobile composer is `v-if="dmPane === 'chat'"`. Flipping to Profile unmounts `ChatComposer`, which throws away the typed text, attachments, alt text and any pending or failed bubble. Desktop (1121) and mobile (1145) are also separate instances, so rotating an iPad across 1024px wipes the draft too. → Keep a single instance alive (`v-show` for the pane), or persist the draft (see ChatComposer). · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:864` — The DM back button always calls `router.push('/messages')`, which overrides SubviewChrome's history-back. If you came from Activity or a profile, Back goes to the inbox, and browser Back then returns to the DM (a loop). The same hard-coded push is in `notifications.vue:363` and `messages.vue:240`. → Drop `backAction` and let SubviewChrome use history, falling back to the current target. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:422-426` — The public reply dock always prepends the focus author, including when that author is you, so replying to your own post sends "@you …". It also drops the other people mentioned in the post (Mastodon keeps them). → Leave out the viewer's acct, and add `focusStatus.mentions` except yourself. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:643-644` — `statusStore.resolveThreadId` (`app/stores/status.ts:393-398`) swallows every error. Offline, 401 and 5xx all end up as "Couldn't find that post on your server." → Only treat a 404 as not-found, and send other errors through `mapErrorToMessage` so Retry makes sense. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:913-927,785` — Public threads have no refresh button (only the DM header has one, at 895) and no polling (`startThreadPoll` returns early when the thread isn't a DM). New replies only show after a full page reload. → Add the same refresh action to the public header, and optionally a quiet poll with a "N new replies" pill. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1174-1182` — Nothing marks a missing or deleted parent. If `focusStatus.inReplyToId` is set but `ancestors` is empty, or `ancestors[0].inReplyToId` points at a post that wasn't returned, the thread starts as if it were the root. → Render an "Earlier post unavailable · Open original" stub above the first ancestor. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:662` — `fetchThread` only asks the viewer's server. Replies your server never received are missing, and the only way to see the full remote thread is the globe link out of the app. → Decide whether to merge the origin server's public `/api/v1/statuses/:id/context` (matched by `uri`) for public posts. · decision
- [ ] [PERF·M·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:786-789` — The DM poll re-downloads the focus status and the whole context every 40s (`stores/status.ts:369-372`), on top of the conversations poll every 45s. Long DMs refetch and re-render hundreds of statuses per tick. → Drive it from the conversations store: only refetch context when this conversation's `lastStatus.id` changes. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:787` — The poll skips hidden tabs but nothing catches up on `visibilitychange`. Returning to a DM shows stale messages for up to 40s. → Run `loadThread({ quiet: true })` on visibility → visible, throttled. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:570-576` — `scrollChatToEnd` runs on `nextTick`, but bubble images are `loading="lazy"` with no width/height (`MessageBubble.vue:274-279`). As they load they push the newest message under the composer. → Give images intrinsic size from `meta.small`, or re-scroll on image `load` while the reader is still at the bottom. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:697-699` — A DM opened from a notification about an older message always scrolls to the bottom. The referenced message isn't scrolled into view or highlighted. → When `paramId` isn't the last message, scroll to that bubble and briefly highlight it. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:169-181` — Participants who have only been mentioned (for example, you sent the first DM and they haven't replied yet) get `displayName = username` and `avatar: null`. A new 1:1 header shows a lowercase handle and a letter placeholder. → Hydrate them via `accounts.$select(id).fetch()`, as the peek pane already does. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:997-1001` — "Older messages may be truncated by your server's context limit" appears on any DM with 40 or more messages. Mastodon's context isn't capped at 40 for signed-in users, so the warning is usually false. → Show it only when `ancestors.length === 40` and `ancestors[0].inReplyToId` is set. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:636,662` — Each fresh open fetches the focus status twice: `resolveThreadId` → `fetchStatus` (`status.ts:394`), then `fetchThread` → `fetch` (`status.ts:370`). → Have `resolveThreadId` return the status and reuse it. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:969-979` — `role="tabpanel"` is set even when the tablist isn't rendered (desktop, or no participants). The result is an unnamed tabpanel with no tabs. → Bind `role`/`aria-labelledby` only when `showDmContextChrome`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1036-1050` — `role="radiogroup"`/`role="radio"` chips have no arrow-key roving and every chip is a tab stop. Chips without `p.id` do nothing when clicked. → Add roving tabindex plus arrows, or use plain buttons with `aria-pressed`, and disable chips that have no id. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1026-1034` — The profile pane's `aria-label` sits on a role-less `div`, so it is ignored. → Add `role="region"`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·M] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:985-989` — Clearing the find query switches `aria-live` back to `polite` in the same render that re-inserts every filtered-out bubble. Screen readers can then read out the whole conversation. → Turn `aria-live` back on one tick after the list has settled. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:952-953` — The loading label branches on `isDirectThread`, which is always false while loading because `focusStatus` is null. DMs show "Loading conversation", and the "Loading messages" branch never runs. → Use one neutral label, or decide from the route. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1219-1221` — "No replies yet — be the first." only appears when there are no ancestors either, so a leaf reply shows nothing. It is also shown to signed-out viewers, who can't reply. → Base it on `descendants` only, and use different copy when `!canReply`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:723-728` — `onReplyPosted` adds 1 to the focus post's `repliesCount` for any `@replied` from an ancestor or nested card (1181, 1209), even when the reply went to a different post. → Only increment when `status.inReplyToId === focusStatus.id`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:499` — Depth is capped at 6 with no "continue thread" affordance. Deeper replies all sit at the same indent, so you can't tell parent from child. → At the cap, render "Continue thread →" linking to `/status/{id}`. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:872-886` — If the first participant has no avatar and the second does, the first `<img>` is hidden by `v-show`. The second renders as the small `--stack` badge next to an empty main circle. → Render a placeholder per slot instead of hiding the first. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:885,1063` — `.slice(0, 1)` on a display name splits emoji surrogate pairs, so the initial shows as �. → Take the first grapheme (`clipGraphemes`/`Intl.Segmenter`). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:264,304,310` — The peek pane shows raw `e.message` (e.g. "Request failed with status code 404"). → Use `mapErrorToMessage`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:1247` — `focusStatus.visibility === 'direct' ? 'direct' : focusStatus.visibility` returns the same value either way, and this branch is never a DM. → Pass `focusStatus.visibility`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:394-406` — The thread title joins names with "&" and "+N" in English and duplicates `participantLabel` (`dmHelpers.ts:54-62`). → Share one helper built on `Intl.ListFormat`. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:832-838` — DM participants' names go into `document.title`, so they show up in browser history, tab switchers and screen shares. → Decide whether DM titles should be a generic "Message | NeoSpace". · decision

### app/components/MessageBubble.vue
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:259-263,235` — Custom emoji are never rendered. The bubble HTML and sender name skip `emojify` (RealPostCard uses it), so DMs show raw `:blobcat:`. The same applies to names in the chat header, inbox rows (`messages.vue:420`), the recipient picker (`RecipientPicker.vue:233`) and notification actors (`notifications.vue:569`). → Run `emojify(html, status.emojis)` and `account.emojis` for names. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:329-341` — The ⋯ menu also renders on optimistic and failed bubbles (`ChatComposer.vue:321`). "Delete" calls `deleteStatus('pending-…')` and shows an error toast; "Open as post" goes to `/status/pending-…`, which errors. → Hide the menu (or limit it to Copy) when `delivery` is set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:183-186` — "Open as post" goes to `/status/{id}`. For a direct status that page renders the same chat view, so the action appears to do nothing. → Open `status.url` on the origin server, or remove the item. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:268-279,120-128` — GIFV is shown as a static `previewUrl` that never animates. Tapping it hands the lightbox `item.url`, an `.mp4`, as an image source, so the lightbox is broken. → Render gifv as `<video autoplay loop muted playsinline>` and skip the image lightbox for it. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:210-226` — A public or unlisted post in a DM thread (an ancestor the DM replied to, or someone's non-direct reply) looks exactly like a private bubble. Users can't tell which messages are actually public. → Show a "Public"/"Unlisted" chip when `status.visibility !== 'direct'`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:259-310` — `status.poll`, `status.card` and quotes are ignored, so a DM containing a poll shows only its text and there's no way to vote. → Reuse the poll and link-card components from RealPostCard inside the bubble. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:313-318` — Every bubble has its own timestamp, so consecutive messages repeat "3:41 PM" under each line, and bubble corners aren't grouped. The chat feels noisy compared with Threads/IG. → Show the time only on the last bubble of a run, or after a gap of more than 5 minutes; tighten corners inside a run. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/status/[id].vue:437-442` — The sender's name and avatar only reappear when the speaker changes. After a day separator or a gap of several hours, the same person's next message has no header. → Also show meta when `dayLabelFor(...)` is set or the gap is over 1h. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:60-66,240-251` — `sensitive` with no spoiler text hides the message text as well. In Mastodon, `sensitive` only marks the media. → Use the full gate only when `spoilerText` is set; otherwise blur just the media. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:241,250` — The CW button switches to "Hide" when revealed, but it is `v-if="showSpoilerGate"`, so it disappears and a revealed CW can't be hidden again. → Keep a toggle above the content once revealed. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:313-318` — Edited DMs aren't marked; `status.editedAt` is ignored. → Append "· Edited" with the edit time in the title. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:120-129` — The lightbox only gets the tapped image, so a DM with 4 photos can't be swiped through. → Pass every image/gifv in `knownMedia` as `items` along with the start index. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:220,227` — The speaker is announced twice: the article's `aria-label` starts with `speakerLabel`, and the sr-only span repeats it. → Keep one of them. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:188-195` — Long-press doesn't suppress the native `contextmenu`/selection on Android, so selection handles and our menu both appear. → `preventDefault` the `contextmenu` that follows a long-press that opened the menu. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:433-440` — On touch, every bubble has a permanently visible 36px ⋯ button at 0.6 opacity even though long-press opens the same menu, which doubles the chat clutter. → Decide whether to hide it on coarse pointers and show it only on focus. · decision
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/MessageBubble.vue:348-358` — There is a scoped copy of `.sr-only` even though the global utility exists. → Delete it. · mech

### app/components/ChatComposer.vue
- [ ] [UX·H·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:43` — The DM composer has no draft persistence; `useDraft` is imported only by `RealComposeBox.vue:13`. Navigating away, reloading, or any remount (pane flip, breakpoint change, `chatThreadKey` change) loses the message. → `useDraft(\`dm:${threadRootId}\`)`, cleared on a successful send. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:258,264-271` — Attachments are cleared before the request. If the send fails, "Edit" restores only the text; the photos and alt text are lost because their ids only live in `pendingSend`. → Keep the tray until the send succeeds, or restore attachments in `editFailed`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:252,76,109` — For a media-only send, the optimistic bubble shows the mentions-only body ("@alice") and no image, because `mediaAttachments: []`. → Use the typed text only, and add local `previewUrl` attachments to the optimistic status. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:280-286,465` — Enter always sends, including on touch keyboards that have no Shift key, so mobile users can't type a line break in a DM. → Decide: on coarse pointers, make Enter insert a newline and leave sending to the button. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:192` — `canSend` requires `!pendingSend`. While a failed message is in the dock, nothing new can be sent until the user picks Retry or Edit. → Allow new sends and keep the failed bubble queued separately. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:267` — `editFailed` only restores the text if the field is empty. If the user has started typing again, the failed message is silently dropped. → Prepend it to the current text, or ask. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:177-185` — Within 40 characters of the limit, `counterAnnounce` changes on every keystroke, so screen readers hear "38 characters left", "37…" continuously. → Announce only when crossing thresholds (40/20/10/0) or going over. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:765-769,857-865` — The "Sending to …" hint and the counter are 11px `--neo-text-quaternary`, below 4.5:1 (elsewhere the previous audit moved this to tertiary). → Use `--neo-text-tertiary`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:470-479` — The remaining-character count ("487") is always visible in a chat input. → Show it only when `nearLimit || overLimit`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:431,489-494` — The attach button says "Add photo" although video is accepted, and the loader's label stays "Sending" during uploads. → Use "Add photo or video" and label the loader by state. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·M] `app/components/ChatComposer.vue:320-328` — The optimistic bubble renders inside the fixed composer dock rather than at the end of the chat log. The dock grows, then jumps when the real message appears in the stream. → Emit the pending status to the parent and render it after the last bubble. · judgment

### app/components/DmContextFooter.vue
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/components/DmContextFooter.vue:93-104,117-120` — The Chat/Profile tabs are 22×22px with 12px icons at 0.55 opacity, and this UI is mobile-only. That is below the 24px minimum target (44px recommended on touch) and roughly 3:1 non-text contrast; the focus ring is 1px. → Use a 44px hit area (padding or `::before`), full opacity, and a 2px focus outline. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/DmContextFooter.vue:71-81` — The tabs are icon-only and rely on `title` tooltips, which don't exist on touch. The selected Profile icon isn't filled (only Chat fills), so the current state is hard to read. → Fill both selected states, or add tiny text labels. · mech

### app/pages/messages.vue, app/stores/conversations.ts, app/utils/dmHelpers.ts
- [ ] [CODE·H·S] `app/composables/useAccountSearch.ts:48` (feeds `RecipientPicker.vue:109` and `messages.vue:24,71`) — The following list is paginated with `maxId = last account id`. Mastodon paginates `/following` by follow-record id from the Link header, so page 2 onward returns the wrong, duplicate or missing people. That causes duplicate `:key` rows in `RecipientPicker.vue:221` and people missing from the picker. → Use masto's paginator, or parse `Link: rel=next`. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:129-132` — Every inbox open and pull-to-refresh pages through up to 400 followed accounts (5 sequential requests) just to fill ≤8 suggestion chips. Those chips only render when the inbox is empty or has fewer than 5 chats. → Call `loadFollowing()` lazily, only when `!conversations.length || isSparseInbox`, and don't force it on refresh. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:106-110` — A failed "Remove chat" is silent. `conversations.ts:224` sets `store.error`, which only renders when the list is empty (326-327). → Remove the row optimistically, restore it and toast on failure. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/conversations.ts:235,240-243` — Media-only DMs have content like `<p>@alice</p>`, which strips to "". The row shows a blank preview or a dangling "You: ". → Fall back to "Photo" / "Video" / "Attachment" from `lastStatus.mediaAttachments`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/conversations.ts:240-243` — The inbox preview shows the text of content-warned DMs because `spoilerText` is ignored. → Use "CW: {spoilerText}" when it is set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/utils/dmHelpers.ts:38` — `await conversations.markRead(existing.id)` runs before `router.push`, so "Message Alice" waits on a network round-trip. The same happens in `ComposeSheet.vue:103`. → Use `void conversations.markRead(...)` after `markReadLocal`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:383-384` — The 64px pull indicator opens whenever `isRefreshing` is true. That also happens for the header Refresh button, account switches (236) and `afterMessagePosted` (82), so ordinary refreshes push the list down and show a second spinner. → Open the indicator only for refreshes started by a pull. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:464-484` — After a load-more failure, both the "Load more" button and an inline "Retry" link are shown, and they do the same thing. → Keep one control. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:338-347` — The empty inbox says only "No messages" with no explanation, and the secondary link is labelled just "People". → Add one line of guidance and use "Find people to message". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:406-420` — A DM to yourself (`accounts: []`) shows no avatar (an empty 48px gap) and the label "Direct message". → Show your own avatar and "Note to self". · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/messages.vue:331`, `app/pages/notifications.vue:516,659` — Straight apostrophes ("Couldn't", "don't", "That's") sit next to curly ones used elsewhere on the same screens. → Use ’ consistently. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/dmHelpers.ts:54-62` — `participantLabel` builds "A & B" and "A +2" with English concatenation; the same logic is duplicated in `status/[id].vue:394-406`. → Use one helper with `Intl.ListFormat`. · mech

### app/pages/notifications.vue, app/stores/notifications.ts, app/utils/notifHelpers.ts, app/utils/notifGroup.ts
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/stores/notifications.ts:122-130` — Filters run client-side over the 30 rows loaded per account, so "Mentions", "Polls" and "Edits" often show "Nothing here" even though older matches exist. The empty state (`notifications.vue:507-520`) also unmounts `loadTrigger` (651), so no further pages are ever fetched. → Send `types[]` to the API for the active filter and refetch when the filter changes. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [PERF·H·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/layouts/default.vue:135-139,161-162` — `refreshUnreadBadge` runs on mount and on `visibilitychange` only. It never polls while the tab is visible, and it isn't refreshed when an account is added (the watcher at 178-189 only refreshes DMs). A tab left open shows a stale Activity badge indefinitely. → Poll with backoff while visible, as `conversations.ts:252-288` does, or use the notification stream. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/notifHelpers.ts:39-41` — `severed_relationships`, `moderation_warning` and `annual_report` notifications carry the recipient's *own* account. Rows read "YourName severed a relationship", "YourName received a moderation warning" and "YourName shared an annual report", with your own avatar, and clicking opens your own profile (`notifications.vue:290-293`). → Treat these as having no actor. Use copy like "You lost connections with {domain}", "Your account received a moderation warning", and link to `{instanceUrl}/severed_relationships`, `/disputes/strikes/{id}`, and the annual report. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* Real: Mastodon sets `from_account` to the recipient for these types, so rows show "You severed a relationship" with your own avatar and link to your own profile (`notifications.vue:292-293`). But these types are rare. [Impact Medium. Fix unchanged.]
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:269-294` — Clicking an `admin.report` row opens the reporter's profile instead of the report. → Open `{instanceUrl}/admin/reports/{notif.report.id}`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/notifHelpers.ts:31` — The poll label is "'s poll has ended". With the name span's `margin-right: .35rem` (`notifications.vue:1197`) plus the template whitespace, it renders "Alice ’s poll has ended". For your own poll it reads "YourName's poll has ended". → Build the whole sentence: "{name}'s poll has ended" / "Your poll has ended" when `account.id` is yours. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:145-170` — "Oldest first" sorts rows within each bucket, but `groupOrder` (`notifications.vue:85,113`) stays Today → Older. Oldest-first still puts today's activity at the top. → Reverse `groupOrder` when `sortOrder === 'oldest'`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:308-317` — The IntersectionObserver only fires when intersection changes. When a filtered or searched view adds few rows and the trigger stays on screen, loading stops for good. → After `loadMore`, if the trigger is still visible and `hasMore`, load again (or re-observe). · mech
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:596-605` — Media thumbnails ignore `status.sensitive`, so NSFW previews appear unblurred in Activity. → Blur them, or show a "Sensitive media" chip, when `notif.status.sensitive`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:296-302,592` — The preview shows the full text of content-warned posts, because `plainTextOf` reads only `content`. → Show "CW: {spoilerText}" instead of the body when `spoilerText` is set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:600` — `media.previewUrl ?? media.url` puts a video or audio file URL into an `<img>` (audio has no preview), which shows broken-image icons. → Use only `previewUrl`; otherwise render a typed placeholder icon. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:210-217,263,270` — Opening another account's notification silently switches the global active account (with a toast). `PanelColumn.vue:370` and the status and profile pages already support a non-switching `?account=` read override. → Push with `query: { account: notif._instanceId }` instead of calling `setActiveAccount`. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:356` — `fetchNotifications` replaces the server `unread_count` (up to 1000, lines 253-262) with a count over the ≤30 rows loaded per account. The title and heading drop from "(150)" to "(30)" when the page opens. → Keep the server count for the badge, and use the local unread state only for row styling. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:249-262` — If every `unread_count` call fails, the badge is set to 0. That covers offline, and GoToSocial/Akkoma, which lack `/api/v1/notifications/unread_count`, so those accounts never get a badge. → Keep the previous value on total failure, and fall back to marker plus list count when the endpoint is missing. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:562-569` — `clearAll` counts any single account's success as success. It wipes every account's rows locally and shows "Notifications cleared" even though failed accounts still have theirs. → Only drop rows for accounts that succeeded, and report a partial result. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/notifications.ts:330-355,440` — Accounts are merged by time but each fetches only its newest 30. A quiet account's month-old rows sit between a busy account's last hour, then load-more inserts the busy account's older rows *above* them, so rows jump under the reader. → Show only rows newer than the highest per-account oldest timestamp; hold the rest until the next page. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:352-360` — Leaving the page marks every loaded notification read, even if the user only looked at the Likes filter or a search result, so unseen mentions lose their unread state. → Decide: mark read only when leaving the unfiltered, unsearched view, or only rows that were actually seen. · decision
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:616-631` — The follow-request buttons are named only "Accept" and "Reject", so in a list of requests screen-reader users can't tell whose request they're acting on. → Use `aria-label="Accept follow request from @{acct}"` (and the same for Reject). · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:1086-1089,1362-1380` — The type accent strips use the same `border-left-color` as unread, are declared later with equal specificity, and always apply. Unread rows lose the accent stripe, and every mention looks the same whether read or not. → Keep the left border for unread only, and move type color to the type badge. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:1076,1423-1425` — The grid `auto auto minmax(0,1fr)` assumes a badge and an avatar. At ≤560px the badge is `display:none`, and rows without an account skip the avatar, so the body lands in an `auto` track and preview boxes take content width (ragged right edges). → Use grid-template-areas or a flex row. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/notifGroup.ts:33-38` — Follows are never collapsed because they have no status, so 5 new followers make 5 rows. Mastodon groups them. → Also collapse consecutive `follow` rows from the same instance. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:540,566` — Unread state is checked only on a collapsed row's head. In oldest-first mode the head is the *oldest* member, so a group with newer unread members looks read. → Mark the row unread if any member is unread. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:170-173` — "Mark all as read" gives no feedback and swallows per-account failures. → Show a toast, and an error toast when any marker write fails. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:523-527` — A search miss only searched loaded activity and offers no way to search further back. → Add a "Search older activity" button that calls `loadMore()`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:301-302` — With no server marker and no local value, the first load saves `items[0]` as read. On a new device, or a server without markers, nothing ever shows as unread. → Leave it unset (treat as unread) and rely on the server marker. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/notifications.ts:147-150` — Today/Yesterday buckets use `new Date()` captured when the getter runs, so a page left open past midnight never re-buckets. → Depend on a shared minute or day tick. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/notifHelpers.ts:5-43` — Mastodon 4.5's `quoted_update` type isn't handled and falls back to "notification" with a bell icon. → Add "edited a post you quoted". · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·M] `app/stores/notifications.ts:289,525-527` — This uses v1 notifications, so a viral post fills 30-row pages with likes, and dismissing a grouped row sends N requests. Mastodon 4.3+ offers `/api/v2/notifications` (grouped) and group dismiss. → Decide whether to adopt v2 with a v1 fallback. · decision
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:1189-1270,1404-1414` — Dead CSS: `__who`, `__action-btn`, `__open--bare`, button and hover styles for `__name` (now a span), and the `.dropdown-*` transitions. → Delete them. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/notifications.vue:503` — The "Try again" button is missing `type="button"`. · mech

- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/NeoReport.vue:95-105` — Submit closes the dialog immediately.
