## Accounts, auth & server picking

### A. OAuth flow & PKCE (instances.ts startAuth/completeAuth, oauthPkce.ts, auth/callback.vue)
- [ ] [SEC·M·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:764` — `readCachedOAuthApp(host)` is reused with no expiry. It is only invalidated on `invalid_client` at the token step (`instances.ts:866-881`). When a server is reset or the app was deleted, or the cached app lacks a scope, Mastodon shows its own error page at `/oauth/authorize` and never redirects back. The cache is never cleared, and sign-in to that host stays broken until "Clear data". → Key the cache by `host+scopes+redirectUri` and store `createdAt`. Validate it with `POST /oauth/token grant_type=client_credentials` (or re-register) before use, and add a "Trouble signing in? Re-register" action on the login connecting state. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* The cache really is keyed by host only, has no TTL, and is cleared only on token-step `invalid_client`, so an app deleted on the server makes `/oauth/authorize` fail for that host until data is cleared. But the scope and redirect variants can't happen: `SCOPES` has never changed and the redirect URI is per-origin, like localStorage. This is a rare reliability edge, not a security issue. Corrected: "A cached OAuth app that the server has deleted wedges sign-in for that host until Clear data. Add a re-register fallback." Impact: Medium.
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:769` — During sign-in the client secret is only stashed in *sessionStorage* (`stashClientSecret`, also `:781`). `completeAuth` reads it at `:843`. When the callback opens in another browsing context (iOS home-screen app → Safari, 2FA in a new tab — exactly the case the localStorage pending record in `oauthPkce.ts:40-63` exists for), it throws "Missing OAuth credentials". The user then sees "Something interrupted sign-in". → In `completeAuth`, fall back to `readCachedOAuthApp(host)?.clientSecret` (already in localStorage). · mech
  - *Verifier:* For a fresh slot, the secret exists only in sessionStorage until after login, so a callback opened in a new tab or window of the same browser finds the localStorage pending record but no secret, and fails with "Missing OAuth credentials". The iOS home-screen→Safari example is wrong: that storage is partitioned, so the pending record is missing too and it fails earlier on state. The fix (fall back to `readCachedOAuthApp(host).clientSecret`) is valid. Impact: Medium/High.
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:799` — The authorize URL never sets `force_login=true`. Adding a second account on a host where you're signed in (`:742-743` opens a new slot) re-authorises whoever the browser is logged in as, so you get the same account twice. → Add `force_login: 'true'` when `opts.addMode` is set or when the host already has a signed-in slot. · mech ✓
- [ ] [CODE·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:922` — After `verifyCredentials`, nothing checks whether another slot already holds the same `url + user.id`. Re-authing an account adds a duplicate to the switcher. On reload, `loadFromStorage` (`:384-403`) keeps the *older* record and drops the newer token without revoking it. → After verify, find a slot with the same `url` and `user.id`, move the new token into it, revoke the old token, and remove the temporary slot. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:882` — `completeAuth` throws `error_description || error`. Mastodon's descriptions for `invalid_grant` and `invalid_client` ("The provided authorization grant is invalid…", "Client authentication failed…") never contain the code string that `friendlyAuthError` matches with `includes` (`app/utils/authErrors.ts:27-29`). Every token error shows the generic "We couldn't finish signing you in." → Throw an Error whose message is the `error` code, and keep the description on a property. · mech
  - *Verifier:* The mechanism is right: Doorkeeper descriptions don't contain the code strings, so `friendlyAuthError` (`authErrors.ts:27-29`) falls through to the generic "We couldn't finish signing you in. Please try again." That copy is still accurate and actionable, though, so this is a copy-quality issue. Impact: Low/Medium.
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:922` — On approval-required or email-confirm servers, `verifyCredentials` returns 403 ("Your login is currently pending approval" / "missing a confirmed e-mail address"). The user sees generic copy. The catch at `:945` also keeps the new token (`!instance.accessToken` is false), which leaves a token-only slot that appears in neither the signed-in nor the watching list until reload. → Map 403 bodies to "Your account is waiting for approval" / "Confirm your email first". On verify failure, revoke and clear the token, or keep the slot with an explicit pending state. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/stores/instances.ts:707` — Abandoned sign-ins still leave stray "Watching" servers. `addInstance` persists the server *before* OAuth (`:707-708`). Cancel (`app/pages/login.vue:143`), browser Back and `access_denied` (`app/pages/auth/callback.vue:35-41`) never remove it. → Keep the new slot unsaved and flagged `pendingAuth` until `completeAuth`, or remove it on cancel, deny or callback error when it had no token before. · judgment
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/stores/instances.ts:97` — `SCOPES = 'read write follow push'`. Nothing in `app/` uses push (no `PushManager` or `v1.push`), and `follow` is deprecated. → `'read write'`. Bump the app cache key at the same time, since the cache isn't scope-aware (see A1). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/auth/callback.vue:94` — `handleRetry` reads the legacy `sessionStorage 'neospace_auth_add'` (still written at `login.vue:89-92`) instead of `pending.addMode`, and it drops `returnTo`. In another context, or after the pending record is cleared, "Back to sign in" loses add mode. → Capture `pending` in a ref before `clearPendingAuth()`, and route to `/login?add=1&returnTo=…` from it. Delete the sessionStorage flag. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/auth/callback.vue:35` — `access_denied` (the user pressed "Deny") renders a red `role="alert"` "Couldn't sign in" page, treating a deliberate choice as a failure. → For `access_denied`, `router.replace` back to `/login` (keeping add/returnTo) with a neutral toast "Sign-in cancelled". · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/oauthPkce.ts:150` — Pending auth is a single localStorage slot. If two tabs start sign-in, tab A's callback falls back to its sessionStorage pair with `pending: null` (`:153-157`), so `addMode` and `returnTo` are lost. An add-mode link then lands on `/` saying "Now posting as …" (`callback.vue:69-84`). → Store pending records in a map keyed by `state` (with TTL pruning) instead of one slot. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:787` — `sessionStorage.setItem` is unguarded here and in `oauthPkce.ts:108-111` and `:169`. With storage blocked (Firefox with cookies disabled, some embedded webviews) it throws a SecurityError, and `friendlyServerError` passes the raw text through ("The operation is insecure."). → Wrap in try/catch, and show "Your browser is blocking storage NeoSpace needs to sign in". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/auth/callback.vue:104` — The page title stays "Signing in… | NeoSpace" on the error state. → Make it computed: "Sign-in failed | NeoSpace" when `error` is set. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/auth/callback.vue:17` — The initial "Signing you in…" is replaced by "Finishing up…" (`:54`) synchronously, so it never shows. "Just a moment." (`:131`) then sits under "Now posting as … — opening NeoSpace…". → Use one status string and drop the redundant sub-line on success. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/pages/auth/callback.vue:55` — The callback loads storage and verifies the new token. Right after `router.replace`, the layout's `initialize()` (`instances.ts:1141`) re-verifies every token, including the one just verified. → Skip verify for ids verified in the last minute, or let the callback run `initialize()` itself. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:786` — The legacy `neospace_auth_instance_id` sessionStorage key is still written and read (`:829-830`, `:935`) and duplicates `pending.instanceId`. → Remove it once A's map-keyed pending record lands. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:428` — The comment "Never persist clientSecret — XSS would steal…" is false. The secret is persisted in localStorage twice: per instance (`oauthPkce.ts:177`) and per host (`instances.ts:124`). → Keep only the per-host cache, delete `persistClientSecret` and the per-id copies, and fix the comment. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:776` — The app `website` is hard-coded to `https://neospace.ibm.io` even when it registers from `neospace-dc4.pages.dev` or localhost. → Use `window.location.origin`. · mech

### B. Token lifecycle, account switching & logout (instances.ts, useMasto.ts)
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/composables/useMasto.ts:139` — `publicClient` resolves the token with `getInstanceByUrl`, which returns the first slot for the host (`instances.ts:351-354`). With two accounts on one server, reads go out as account #1 while #2 is active, so relationship and following state are wrong. If #1 is expired (token null) on an auth-gated host, it falls through to the fosstodon guest client although #2 has a valid token. → Prefer `activeAccount` when its host matches, otherwise any slot on that host with a token. · mech
  - *Verifier:* `getInstanceByUrl` does return the first slot, but signed-in search uses `activeClient` (`useFediverseSearch.ts:171`). `publicClient` serves only group/tag timelines (`TimelineColumn.vue:59`, `groups.ts:575`), Edward trends, the public fallback and guest discovery. Corrected: "With two accounts on one host, publicClient reads (group/tag feeds, trends) carry account #1's viewer flags (favourited/boosted), and an expired #1 forces the guest remap although #2 has a token." Impact: Medium. Relationship/following state is not affected.
- [ ] [UX·H·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:1069` — A session is only marked expired during startup `verifyAllInstances`. When a token is revoked mid-session (from server settings or by an admin), every column shows "Sign in required" (`friendlyError.ts:53-58`) while the switcher still shows the account as active, with no banner and no "Sign in again" state. → Add `markSessionExpired(instanceId)` and call it from a 401 hook in `cachedClient` (`useMasto.ts:58`). Set `authNotice` the same way verify does. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [PERF·H·S] `app/composables/useMasto.ts:58` — Cached clients are created without `timeout`, and masto only adds `AbortSignal.timeout` when `props.timeout` is set. `verifyAllInstances` (`instances.ts:1062-1063`) uses these clients and `initialize()` awaits it before setting `isInitialized` (`:1141`, `:1168`). One hung server keeps `TimelineColumn` waiting on `isInitialized` (`TimelineColumn.vue:1197-1201`), and every column stays blank. → Give `cachedClient` a default `timeout` (~15s). · mech ✓
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1133` — A first-run guest waits on `addInstance(DEFAULT_PUBLIC_INSTANCE)`, a network probe with an 8s timeout, before verify, and again at `:1159-1165` if it failed. Offline that's up to ~16s before `isInitialized`. → Push the default instance synchronously with no probe, and refresh its info in the background. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1070` — 403 is treated like 401: it nulls the token and says "Session expired — sign in again". Mastodon returns 403 for suspended, disabled, pending-approval and unconfirmed accounts, so re-signing in loops forever. → Expire only on 401. For 403, keep the token and show the server's message ("Account pending approval", "Login disabled"). · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1060` — The verify loop writes to the captured `instance` object. If a cross-tab `mergeFromStorage` (`:509`) replaces `this.instances` mid-flight, the verified user and the expired flag are lost (the same bug was fixed for `refreshInstanceInfo` at `:1034`). → Re-find by id after the await. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:719` — Removing or signing out of the active account (also `logoutInstance` `:979-981`) silently switches the posting identity to `authenticatedInstances[0]`. There's no `authNotice` (unlike the verify path at `:1095-1096`), and `AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:97` shows no toast. → Set `authNotice = "Now posting as @x"`, or toast at the call sites. · mech
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `app/stores/instances.ts:957` — In `logoutInstance`, the `/oauth/revoke` response is never checked, failures are swallowed, and when the per-id secret is missing (older accounts) revoke is skipped silently. The previous fix promised "show manual-revoke instructions if it fails". → Fall back to `readCachedOAuthApp(host)`, check `response.ok`, and on failure toast a link to `${url}/oauth/authorized_applications`. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1004` — `logoutAll` awaits each revoke in turn, each with a 15s timeout. "Sign out all" or "Clear data" (`SettingsModal.vue:63,77`) can hang for 15s × N with one dead server and no progress UI. → Clear tokens locally first, then `Promise.allSettled` the revokes. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:1015` — `clearLocalDeviceData` only removes localStorage keys. In-memory Pinia state (watching rows, columns, settings) survives, and `SettingsModal.vue:78-81` only does `router.push('/login')`, so the next `saveToStorage` rewrites the "cleared" data. IndexedDB and Cache Storage are untouched. → After clearing, `location.replace('/login')`, and also clear Cache Storage and IndexedDB. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:553` — The storage listener ignores `newValue == null`. When another tab clears data or removes the key, this tab keeps its tokens in memory and writes them back on the next save, undoing "Clear data" or "Sign out all" done elsewhere. → On `null`, reset the store, or reload when the key was removed. · mech
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:660` — `addInstance` accepts auth-gated hosts for watching and reports "Watching mastodon.social". `initialize()` then silently deletes gated watch-only rows on the next load (`:1123-1131`). The Watch placeholder suggests exactly `mastodon.social` (`InstanceManager.vue:292`), and the card tooltip promises "You can still watch" (`InstanceCard.vue:158`). → Either refuse with an explanation in `addInstance` or stop pruning and show the gated state. · decision ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:973` — Signing out turns the slot into a watching row. On auth-gated hosts that row is pruned on the next start (`:1123`), which makes the confirm copy "You can keep watching public posts" false (`AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:92`, `InstanceManager.vue:132`). → Make the copy conditional on `isAuthGatedPublicHost`, or remove the slot outright. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:973` — `logoutInstance` doesn't reset `sessionExpired` or `error`. With two accounts on one server, signing out both leaves two identical "Watching" rows for the same host: the dedupe key at `:382` falls back to `inst.id` when `user` is null. → When another row for the host exists, remove the slot instead of downgrading it, and reset the flags. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:740` — `loginWithInstance` takes `getInstanceByUrl`'s first match. If that's signed-in account A while expired account B exists on the same host, re-authing B creates a third slot, and B stays expired forever (until a reload dedupes). → Prefer an expired or tokenless slot for the host before opening a new one. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:184` — `mapV2InstanceInfo` drops `registrations.approval_required`, so the picker, cards and preview show approval-only servers as plain "Open". → Add `approvalRequired` and show "Open · approval required". · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1357` — Cache keys are inconsistent. `fetchInstanceInfo` lowercases the key (`:1357`, `:1379`), but `openPreview` stores the raw `domain` (`:1401`) and the `getInstance` getter reads it raw (`:356-357`), so mixed-case domains (pasted links) miss the cache and the preview shows no info. → Lowercase in all three places. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1389` — The `openPreview` stale guard uses the domain string as the ticket, so open A → B → A resolves with A's first response. `closePreview` (`:1423-1427`) also leaves `previewLoading` stuck true. → Use a generation counter and reset `previewLoading` in `closePreview`. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:1373` — `fetchInstanceInfo` has no in-flight dedupe. The ServerPicker peek, InstanceCard and openPreview for the same host fire parallel identical `/api/v2/instance` requests. → Cache the pending promise per key. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/instances.ts:691` — The v2 → `instanceInfo` mapping is duplicated in `refreshInstanceInfo` (`:1037-1050`) and will drift. → Extract `mapInstanceLimits(info)`. · mech

### C. Server compatibility & input normalisation (utils/instances.ts, ServerPicker.vue)
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/instances.ts:688` — `addInstance`, `fetchInstanceInfo` (`:1376`), `openPreview` (`:1399`) and `refreshInstanceInfo` (`:1033`) only call `client.v2.instance.fetch()`. `loginWithInstance` requires `addInstance` to succeed, so servers without `/api/v2/instance` (Mastodon <4.0, older Pleroma/Akkoma, Friendica, Misskey-family Mastodon shims) can't sign in at all. The peek also says "Couldn't reach X" for a server that is up. → Fall back to `client.v1.instance.fetch()` and map `max_toot_chars`, `registrations` etc. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* All four probes call only `v2.instance.fetch()`, so a server without `/api/v2/instance` can't be added or signed into. In 2026 that is mostly legacy servers (Mastodon <4.0, old Pleroma/Akkoma/Friendica builds). Corrected: "Legacy servers lacking v2 instance can't sign in; add a v1 fallback." Impact: Medium.
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/instances.ts:62` — `normalizeServer`'s ASCII-only regex rejects IDN hosts (e.g. `bücher.social`, Cyrillic domains) with "Enter a server name like mastodon.social". → Convert to punycode first with `new URL('https://' + value).hostname`, then validate. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/instances.ts:59` — Valid inputs are rejected: a trailing-dot FQDN (`mastodon.social.`) and an explicit port (`example.social:8443`) both fail the regex. → Strip one trailing dot, and decide whether to allow ports. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/instances.ts:91` — `friendlyServerError` echoes any message under 120 characters. masto's HTML-response error ("The server returned data with an unknown encoding. The server may be down.", e.g. a typo domain with a parking page) and "Request timed out" reach users verbatim. The `timeout` branch (`:88`) never matches masto's "timed out" wording. → Branch on `err.name` (`MastoTimeoutError`, `MastoUnexpectedError`) and `/timed? ?out/`, and map "unknown encoding" to "That doesn't look like a Mastodon-compatible server." · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·M] `app/utils/instances.ts:12` — `AUTH_GATED_PUBLIC_HOSTS` is a hand-kept list of 3 hosts. Since Mastodon 4.2 any server can gate public timelines. → Learn it at runtime: on a 422 or 401 from `timelines/public`, remember the host (persisted with a TTL). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:121` — Each keystroke schedules a peek (`:73-76`, 420ms). Partial hosts like `mastodon.s` and `mastodon.so` are fetched, and a red `role="alert"` "Couldn't reach mastodon.s" (`:215`) interrupts screen readers mid-typing. The failure is cached for 2 min (`instances.ts:167`). → Only peek after ~1s idle or on blur, use `role="status"` for failures, and don't cache failures for hosts the user is still typing. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:205` — The summary line repeats the peek box stats (`:220-223`) in different words ("open registration" / "invite-only" vs "Registrations open" / "Registrations closed"). It isn't gated on `peekLoading`, so the previous server's stats show under a newly typed host. → Delete the summary line. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:208` — `registrations === false` is labelled "invite-only", and the same goes for `InstanceCard.vue:125` "Invite only". Closed sign-ups aren't invite-only (Mastodon invites are separate). → "Sign-ups closed", plus "Approval required" from `approval_required`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:190` — `inputmode="url"` gives an iOS keyboard without "@" on the primary layer, while the label and placeholder ask for `@you@server`. → `inputmode="email"`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:51` — `catalogHits` searches the raw query, so `@alice@hachyderm.io` or `https://hachyderm.io/` shows no catalog match. → Search on the `normalizeServer` host when it parses. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:232` — After you tap a hit, the query equals its domain, so the hit list stays open showing the one item you just chose. → Hide hits when the query exactly equals a hit's domain. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:255` — Featured rows jump straight into OAuth, while typeahead hits only fill the input (`:147-151`), which is inconsistent. Featured rows also show no live stats or registration state. → Make both fill and peek, or both start sign-in. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:235` — The hit button's emoji isn't `aria-hidden` (featured rows are, `:257`), so screen readers read "penguin fosstodon.org". → Wrap the emoji in `<span aria-hidden="true">`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:314` — Featured rows set `outline: none` on `:focus-visible`, overriding the global focus ring (`main.scss:739`). The only cue is a border and tint change. → Remove `outline: none`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:514` — The submit text uses `var(--neo-text-inverse, #fafaf8)`, while every other accent button uses `--neo-text-on-accent`. → Switch the token. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:35` — `showCustom` is always true. `openCustom` (`:57-62`), its exposed API and the `.server-picker__toggle` styles (`:365-383`) are dead. → Remove them. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/ServerPicker.vue:115` — "1,234 monthly active" has no noun or pluralisation (also `:206`, `:221`, `InstanceCard.vue:122`), and InstancePreview calls the same number "Active Users" (`InstancePreview.vue:162`). → Use one string, "1,234 active this month", built with `Intl.PluralRules`. · mech

### D. Login page
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/login.vue:123` — Add-account vocabulary drifts across screens: "Link a server" / "Link this server" (`:151`), tab title "Add account" (`:107`), sheet "Add / server" and "Add another server" (`AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:197-198`, `:254`), "Manage servers" (`:257`), and "+ Link server" (`InstanceManager.vue:185`). Adding a second account on the *same* server is still called "Link a server". → Standardise on "Add account" and "Accounts & servers". · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/login.vue:136` — In add mode the back link is hard-coded "← Back to profile", although add mode is reached from the switcher sheet and the Accounts manager on any page. → Go to `returnTo`, or `router.back()` when history exists. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/login.vue:155` — A signed-out visitor gets no "Continue without signing in" path, though guest browsing exists (`DEFAULT_PUBLIC_INSTANCE`). The only exits are Explore and an external sign-up link. → Add a "Just browse" link to `/`. · decision
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/login.vue:53` — On the signed-in fast path the picker renders first (and autofocuses on desktop, `ServerPicker.vue:164`), then redirects, so it flashes. → Gate the template on a `checked` ref set after the sync token check. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/login.vue:156` — The footer says "New to Mastodon?" while the picker hint says "fediverse" and the app supports GoToSocial and others. → "New to the fediverse?" · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/login.vue:158` — Two links to the same Explore page sit on the page: "Browse servers by interest" here, and "Explore servers" in `ServerPicker.vue:266-269`. → Keep one. · mech

### E. Account switcher (AccountSwitcher.vue, AccountSwitcherSheet.vue)
- [ ] [A11Y·H·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcher.vue:165` — In nav placement the button is announced as "Name, switch accounts" with `aria-expanded` and `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, but activating it goes to Profile (`:83-86`). The keyboard path (`:128`) only works with 2+ accounts, so single-account keyboard and screen-reader users can't reach the sheet's Add or Manage actions. → Make Enter/Space open the sheet in nav placement, or add a separate labelled "Switch account" control, and fix the ARIA. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcher.vue:160` — The tooltip "hold to switch accounts · double-tap to cycle" also shows in sidebar placement, where a tap toggles the sheet and double-tap does nothing. → Show the gesture hints only when `isNav`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcher.vue:70` — A double-tap first navigates to Profile on tap 1 (`:79`), then switches the posting account on tap 2. The page jumps *and* the identity changes, and the gesture is only discoverable from a tooltip that never shows on touch. → Drop double-tap cycling, or delay navigation until the double-tap window passes. · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:22` — The sheet has its own bespoke toast pill (`:42-48`, `:115-119`). `AccountSwitcher.vue:52-53` uses the global toast store, and `InstanceManager.vue:142-144` "Use for posting" shows no feedback at all. That's three behaviours for one action. → Route every account switch through `useToastStore` from a single `setActiveAccount` wrapper. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:143` — The subtitle "Feeds still mix across signed-in accounts" is false, and so is `InstanceManager.vue:170-171` "For You merges their home feeds". `fetchMergedHomeTimeline` now uses only the active account (`instances.ts:1304-1308`). → Rewrite: "Switch which account you post and read as." · mech

- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:316` — The backdrop and shadows (`:331`, `:336`) are `color-mix` of `--neo-text-primary`, so in dark themes they become a light wash instead of a dim. `InstanceManager.vue:365` uses bg-primary and `InstancePreview.vue:310` uses `rgba(0,0,0,.85)`, so there are three scrim recipes. → Add a `--neo-scrim` token and use it in all three. · mech

- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:240` — "Make this main" and InstanceManager's "Make main" (`:245-252`) have no functional effect. `primaryAccount` is only read for badges and the login lede (`login.vue:28`), and nothing explains what "Main" means. → Give "main" a meaning (default posting account on launch, the account for add-mode linking) or remove the concept. · decision
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:159` — There's no avatar fallback, so an account without an avatar shows an empty 72px ring (grid) or nothing at all (list `:212-217`), and the row text shifts left. → Add an initials or placeholder avatar like `AccountSwitcher.vue:192`. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:153` — Grid buttons carry both `aria-pressed` and `aria-current` (screen readers announce "toggle button, pressed, current"). List rows only have `aria-current`. → Use `aria-current` only. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcherSheet.vue:101` — The `acct-sheet-open` html class and its CSS (`:646-648`) duplicate the ref-counted `useScrollLock` that `useFocusTrap` already applies. → Remove them. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcher.vue:137` — `userDisplayName` returns "Guest" when there's no user, so the `|| username || 'Profile'` fallback is dead and the label can read "Guest". → Use `activeAccount?.user` directly. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/AccountSwitcher.vue:199` — The accent-filled account-count badge looks like an unread-notification badge on the avatar. → Use a neutral style (bg-tertiary / text-secondary) or the stacked peeks alone. · judgment

### I. Error mapping utils (friendlyError.ts, federation.ts, authErrors.ts, mastoGuard.ts)
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/friendlyError.ts:37` — Any `TypeError` matching "fetch/network/failed" is classified as offline, so CORS blocks, DNS failures and TLS errors on a dead remote show "You appear to be offline" to an online user. `federation.ts:33` does the same. → Treat only `navigator.onLine === false` as offline; otherwise say "Couldn't reach {server}". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/friendlyError.ts:96` — The fallback echoes `err.message`: masto's "The server returned data with an unknown encoding…", "Request timed out", and `mastoGuard.ts:24` "Unsafe API path segment" all reach users. → Map `MastoTimeoutError` to "took too long" and `MastoUnexpectedError` to "unexpected response", and never echo unknown messages. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/federation.ts:28` — 401 is classified as `forbidden`, giving "{home} won't let this account like that post — it may be followers-only…" when the real cause is an expired token. → Add an `unauthenticated` failure: "Your session on {home} expired — sign in again". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/federation.ts:30` — Every 5xx is labelled `timeout` ("{origin} was slow to answer"), which is wrong for 500/502/503. → Add a `server-error` case. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/federation.ts:72` — The `authorize_interaction` escape hatch is Mastodon-only. Users whose home is GoToSocial, Akkoma or Pleroma get a 404 page. → Offer it only for Mastodon homes (from the instance version), or use `/ostatus_subscribe?acct=` on Pleroma-family servers. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/authErrors.ts:6` — Common cases are unmapped or mis-mapped. `invalid_scope` has no copy. Timeouts ("signal timed out" from `oauthFetch`) fall to the generic line. "Missing OAuth credentials" maps to "Something interrupted sign-in" (`:33-35`), and the `invalid_client` copy says "contact your server admin" although a retry re-registers (`instances.ts:866-881`). → Add `invalid_scope` and timeout cases, and rewrite `invalid_client` as "Try again — NeoSpace will reconnect to this server". · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/utils/mastoGuard.ts:42` — Every property access allocates a new `Proxy` (no cache), so each `client.v1.accounts.$select(id).fetch()` chain builds several proxies, on hot timeline paths. → Memoise wrapped children per target in a `WeakMap`. · mech

## Groups (hashtag groups) & ranked-feed algorithms

### app/stores/groups.ts
- [ ] [CODE·H·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1027-1037` — `searchGroups` returns plain new objects for tags not already in `groups`; `joinGroup` (`:836-848`) only updates/pushes store entries, so the search-result card's `group.isMember` stays `false`. User taps Join → spinner → button reverts to "Join" although the follow succeeded; tapping again re-follows. → Have `searchGroups` push unknown tags into `this.groups` (non-featured, non-trending) and return the store object, or have GroupCard read membership via `groupsStore.getGroup(tag)?.isMember ?? group.isMember`. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/stores/groups.ts:583-628` — Nothing re-runs `initializeGroups` when `activeAccountId` changes (only mount-time callers in layout/pages), and the merge at `:599-618` carries `isMember` from the previous account. When the new account is a guest (`:624` skips `fetchFollowedTags`) or `fetchFollowedTags` fails (`:803-806`), the old account's joins stay "Joined" indefinitely; the old account's custom followed tags also linger as groups. → Watch `activeAccountId` in the store (or layout) and call `initializeGroups(true)`; reset `isMember=false`, drop non-featured/non-trending entries and clear `followedTags`/`timelineCache` before refetching. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* No `activeAccountId` watcher exists, so state stays stale on the current screen. But `initializeGroups` is account-keyed (`groupsInitializedFor`, :583-590), and the next visit to /groups, Explore or a group page re-inits and recomputes `isMember` from the new account's followed tags. "Indefinitely" holds only when `fetchFollowedTags` fails or nobody is authenticated (`activeAccount` falls back to any authed slot). Corrected: "stale until the next mount-time init; permanent only on fetch failure". Impact: Medium.
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:616-618` — On re-init only featured groups get `trending: false`; every other previously-trending tag is copied with `trending: true` and its stale `postsCount`, so tags that dropped off the server's trends stay in "Trending on {server}" and the Trending category until reload. → Reset `trending` (and `postsCount`) for all carried entries before `fetchTrendingTags`, and drop non-member, non-featured entries that no longer trend. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:91,804` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: `followedTagsError` is set but no component reads it (grep: only the store). A failed load still makes "Your groups" vanish and every group look un-joined with no message or retry. → Surface it on /groups (banner "Couldn't load your groups · Retry") and in the sidebar groups section. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1141-1153` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED: the first `.replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, '$1 $2')` splits before the "keep NixOS" check can run: NixOS → "Nix OS", JavaScript → "Java Script", TikTok → "Tik Tok", iPhone → "I Phone", macOS → "Mac OS". → Only split camelCase when the tag is mixed-case *and* not in a small keep-list, or split only on `_` and leave camelCase intact (`#JavaScript` is a name). Add a unit test. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1040,1059` + `app/utils/hashtag.ts:4` — "Start following #q" uses `isValidHashtag` (`/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/`, ≤64), while the route/API guard `isGroupTagName` (`groups.ts:32`) allows any script, digits-first and 100 chars. `#日本`, `#café`, `#100DaysOfCode` are never offered as a group in search, yet `/groups/日本` works. → Use `isGroupTagName` (plus a "has at least one letter" rule) for the offer. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:797,843,1132` — Followed tags and newly joined tags are hard-coded `category: 'other'`, while trending/search use `guessCategory`. A tag shown as "tech" in search jumps to "Other" after Join and its tint changes. → Use `this.guessCategory(name)` in all three places. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/stores/groups.ts:688-698` — Trending tags are hidden with `key.includes(kw)` over all v2 filter keywords, ignoring `wholeWord` (a "ai" filter hides #rain, #taiwan), filter `context`, expired filters (`expiresAt`), and whether filters have loaded yet (init runs right after `instances.initialize`; a missed race keeps the unfiltered list for the 5-min TTL). → Honour `wholeWord` with token match, skip expired / non-`public` contexts, and re-filter when `settingsStore.filters` changes. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:816-866,871-903` — join/leave don't refuse a call while `pendingTags[key]` is set. Join + Undo/Leave fired close together race: the first to finish deletes the pending key, and the server ends in whichever order the requests landed while the UI shows the other. → Early-return (or queue) when the tag is already pending; let the latest intent win by sequence number. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1057-1071` (+ `app/composables/useGroupSearch.ts:53`) — Search errors (offline, 401, 429) are swallowed and turned into a fake "Start following #q" card, so the UI can't tell "no such tag" from "search failed", and the composable's `catch` is unreachable. → Re-throw (or return `{ results, error }`) and show "Couldn't search — check your connection · Retry" in /groups. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:946,991` — `hasMore = statuses.length === 20` ends the group feed early ("You've seen all the posts! 🎉") whenever the server returns a short page (filtered/hidden statuses, GoToSocial/Akkoma page caps), and keeps going one extra empty round otherwise. → Use the Link header via `cursorPage` (already imported and used for followed tags) for `maxId`/`hasMore`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:919,949` — Group-timeline errors share `state.error` with `initializeGroups` (`:596` sets `error = null`). Any background re-init (drawer open, TTL expiry, explore page) wipes a shown timeline error, and the page flips from "Try again" to "No posts yet". → Use a separate `timelineError` field. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:556,562,1076` — Group identity is `toLowerCase()` only. Mastodon normalizes tags with NFKC and folds Latin accents (café ≡ cafe), so a followed `café` and a trending `cafe` appear as two groups with different membership. A decomposed `café` URL also misses the cache. → Add a shared `tagKey()` (NFKC, lowercased, accents stripped for Latin) and use it for all comparisons and cache keys. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1039-1054` — An exact-match tag is only put first when the server *didn't* return it. If `#baking` is result #6, the user's exact query is buried under `#bakingbread`, etc. → Move an existing exact match to index 0 too. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1030` — Every search result gets the boilerplate description "Join #x as a group", and the `history` the search API returns is thrown away (`tagHistorySummary` in utils/hashtag already formats "N people · M posts · last 7d"). → Use `tagHistorySummary(tag)` as the description and fill `postsCount`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:66,720-727` — `postsCount` (7-day uses) is computed for trending/featured groups but never rendered anywhere (grep: no template reads it). Trending tiles say just "Trending". → Show "1.2K posts this week" (Intl compact number) on tiles and cards, or stop computing it. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:1120-1136` — `addCustomGroup` is dead (no callers) and pushes an unvalidated tag (no `isGroupTagName`) if ever used. → Delete it. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:453` — An orphaned doc comment ("Rough bucket for live tags…") sits above the `timelineRequestId` comment and documents nothing. → Remove it. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/groups.ts:48-49,543` — `GroupCategory` includes `'local'` and `'trending'`, but nothing assigns either as a category (trending uses the flag), and `GROUP_CATEGORIES` has no 'local'. `getByCategory`'s `g.category === 'trending'` branch is dead. → Drop both from the union and remove the dead branch. · mech

### app/utils/guessCategory.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/guessCategory.ts:60-61` — The tag is lowercased *before* tokenizing, so camelCase tags (`CatsOfMastodon`, `LinuxGaming`, `IndieDev`) become one token and land in "Other". Most real tags are concatenated words, so few trending tags get bucketed. → Split camelCase/digit boundaries first, then lowercase. Optionally do a prefix/suffix match against keywords ≥4 chars for all-lowercase concatenations (`catsofmastodon` → cats). · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/guessCategory.ts:31,37,42` — False positives: `indie` → gaming (indie music/film), `steam` → gaming (STEAM education, steam trains), `ask`/`help` → social, `world` → news. → Drop the ambiguous words or require a second signal. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/guessCategory.ts:61` — The tokenizer `[^a-z0-9]+` shreds non-ASCII tags (`café` → `caf`, `fotografía` → `fotograf`, `ía`), so non-English tags always land in "Other". → Tokenize on `[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u`. · mech

- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:35-37` — The store's `trendingGroups` caps at 12 *before* this page filters out joined tags, so with several trending tags joined the rail shows far fewer than 10. → Filter `!isMember` inside the getter (or pass a limit) before slicing. · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:270` — Category filter toggles are wrapped in `<nav aria-label="Group categories">`. They're `aria-pressed` filter buttons, not navigation links, so a landmark is wrong. → Use `<div role="group" aria-label="Filter by category">`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:17,277` — The selected category lives in a local ref, so returning from a group page always resets to "All Groups" and scroll-restores into a different list. → Sync `selectedCategory` with `?cat=` via `router.replace`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:167-181` — The jump chips are `<button @click=router.push>`, so you can't middle-click, cmd-click or long-press them to open in a new tab, and they lack link semantics. → Use `NuxtLink` with the same styling. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:433-437` — `.section-lede` CSS is unused (no element has the class). → Delete it, or use it for the lede proposed above. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/groups/index.vue:442-444` (store) / `:271-281` — A "Trending" category chip repeats the "Trending on {server}" rail directly above it, and its card list is the same ≤20 tags. → Drop the Trending chip from `GROUP_CATEGORIES`, or make the rail link to it. · decision

### app/components/GroupCard.vue
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:82` (+ `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:178-180`) — Undo after leaving calls `void groupsStore.joinGroup(leftTag)` with no catch. A failed rejoin is an unhandled rejection with no toast, and the user believes they're back in. → Wrap it in try/catch and show "Couldn't rejoin #tag · Retry". · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·M] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:63-69` (+ `[tag].vue:158`) — Every join failure shows "Couldn't join group · Retry". On servers without tag following (older GoToSocial/Akkoma: 404/501) or with a 422, Retry can never work. → Map status codes: 404/501 → "Your server doesn't support following hashtags" (no Retry). 429 → "Slow down — try again in a minute". · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:172-174,402-414` — The member check badge is absolutely positioned at top/right 0.5rem (1.25rem square). It overlaps the top-right corner of the Leave button in column 3 (card padding 0.875–1rem), and `.group-card__actions` (z-index 2) paints over it, so it looks clipped. → Move the badge onto the icon (bottom-right of `.group-card__icon`), or drop it since the button already says "Joined". · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:206-214` — `&:hover` comes after `&--member` and overrides its background, so a joined card loses its accent tint on hover and looks un-joined. → Add `&--member:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--neo-accent) 14%, var(--neo-bg-card)); }`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:127-135` — Trending cards replace the category label with the word "trending" (and tiles' tagline is just "Trending" inside the "Trending on…" rail), so the category and the tag name are both lost. → Show the `#tag` and a stats line on tiles, and keep the category with a 🔥 marker. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:16,22,109,124,216-229,139` — The `compact` prop and its CSS are dead (no caller passes it). `@click.stop` on actions is a leftover from the old clickable article. → Remove both. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/GroupCard.vue:188,255` — `--neo-radius-md` falls back to `4px` on the card but `8px` on the icon, so the corners don't match when the token is missing. → Use one fallback. · mech

### app/pages/groups/[tag].vue
- [ ] [CODE·H·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:62-63,283-307` — The page tracks join/leave with local `isJoining`/`isLeaving` instead of `groupsStore.isTagPending(tag)`. Because `joinGroup` flips `isMember` optimistically, the Join button is swapped for an *enabled* Leave button while the follow request is in flight (and "Joining..." at `:305` never shows for known groups), so a quick second tap fires leave during join. → Disable both buttons on `groupsStore.isTagPending(tag)` (as GroupCard does) and remove the local refs. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:99,324-333` — `onGroupPosted` prepends every posted status to the group feed, but the composer uses the account's default visibility, and on phones (`:compact="isMobile"`) the visibility selector is hidden (`RealComposeBox.vue:1216`). Unlisted/followers-only posts never reach `#tag`'s timeline, yet the user sees theirs "in the group". → Force `initial-visibility="public"` for group compose (or show a visible "Only public posts appear in groups" notice and the selector). Prepend only when `status.visibility === 'public'`. · judgment ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:102-109` — A cache hit (`restoreTimeline`) returns early and never fetches newer posts, so returning to a group after 30 minutes shows a stale feed with no "new posts" affordance or refresh. → After restoring, fetch with `sinceId` (or `minId`) in the background and prepend, or show a "N new posts" pill. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:204-206` — Back always `router.push('/groups')`. Arriving from a post's hashtag, a Home column or the sidebar, Back goes to the hub instead of where you were, and adds a history entry. → Use `router.back()` when `window.history.state?.back` exists, else `/groups`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·M·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:1020-1032,423` — For guests the fixed `.post-hint` (≈52–90px tall when it wraps) sits over the bottom of the feed, but the page only reserves `1.5rem` (+ safe area), so "Load More Posts" / "You've seen all the posts" are covered. → Add bottom padding equal to the hint's height when `!isAuthenticated`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:570,1073` — REGRESSED/NOT FIXED (old audit `[tag].vue:595-598,816,824`): `font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', 'Fira Code', monospace` is still hard-coded twice. → Use `var(--neo-font-mono)` (or the project's mono token). · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:43-56,257` — Ad-hoc groups (any tag not in the store) always get `category: 'other'`, and the header uses `category` while GroupCard uses `trending` colour, so the same tag is tinted differently on the card and on its page. → Use `groupsStore.guessCategory(tag)` for ad-hoc groups and the same trending→colour rule as GroupCard. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:318-321` — The membership banner ("You're a member! …") shows on every visit forever, uses a straight apostrophe (curly elsewhere), and "member" overstates what following a tag means. → Show it once after Join (or make it dismissible), and reword: "You follow #tag — its posts from accounts your server knows show up in Home." · judgment
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:235,265` — `SubviewChrome` renders the group name as the h1, and the header repeats the same name as an h2 directly beneath. → Hide the chrome title until the header scrolls out, or make the header show only `#tag`/description. · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:305-306,397-398` — The visible "+ Join" is read "plus Join". Copy is inconsistent: "Joining..." / "Loading..." (three dots) vs "Leaving…" (ellipsis), and "Load More Posts" / "Log In" (`:413`) in Title Case vs sentence case elsewhere. → Use an icon for "+", the ellipsis character, and sentence case throughout. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:64,197-198` — `loadMoreError` is assigned but never rendered (only a toast shows), so the Load More button gives no inline error state. → Render it under the button with Retry, or delete the ref. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:348,376-380` — The search placeholder "Search posts…" implies a server search, but it only filters loaded posts. The "No matches" state offers no way to load more and search again. → Placeholder "Find in loaded posts…". Add a "Load more" button to the no-match state when `hasMore`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:881-899` — The scoped `.timeline-error button` rule paints "Try again" solid accent, overriding the `neo-btn--ghost` class it's given, so it doesn't match other ghost retry buttons. → Delete the element rule and rely on `neo-btn` variants. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:370-374` — The empty state repeats itself ("No posts yet" / "No posts visible from your server yet."). → h3 "Nothing here yet", body "Your server hasn't seen any #tag posts. Use #tag when you post to start it." · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·M] `app/pages/groups/[tag].vue:391-400` — The group feed only pages with a manual "Load More Posts" button, unlike the app's other timelines. → Add an IntersectionObserver sentinel that calls `handleLoadMore`, and keep the button as a fallback. · judgment

### app/utils/rankers.ts
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:199` — `mentionsYou` is also true when `norm(m.acct).split('@')[0] === self.split('@')[0]`, so any mention of `@alice@other.example` counts as mentioning you (`alice`). X then adds `75 × 0.6 = 45` ("the author may reply to you"), which outranks everything. → Compare full accts only: normalize your own acct to `user@host` and mention accts the same way (local → `@host`). · mech
  - *Verifier:* The local-part fallback does flag `@alice@other.example` as mentioning local `alice` and adds 75×0.6. Realistically it only affects posts mentioning someone who shares your username, which skews a few posts in the X ranker. Impact: Medium (ranking quality).
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:207-213` — `isFollowed`/`isMutual`/`likedAuthorScore` fall back to `acct.split('@')[0]`. If you follow a local `bob`, every remote `bob@x` is "in your network" / "a friend", gets FB closeness 1.0 and passes X's social-proof gate. → Store and compare fully-qualified accts (add the instance host to local accts in `loadContext`/`tasteFrom`), and remove the local-part fallback. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* The local-part fallback is real: following local `bob` makes every `bob@x` in-network and mutual. It's a ranking-quality bug limited to username collisions. Impact: Medium.
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:182-184` — Counts aren't clamped (followers is). A negative/garbage `favouritesCount` from a misbehaving server makes `p()` return −∞, and `log1p(msi)` returns NaN when msi < −1. `-Infinity * 0` (old posts) gives NaN, and a NaN in the sort comparator scrambles the order. → `Math.max(0, Number(x) || 0)` for all three counts, and guard `Number.isFinite(score) ? score : 0`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:186,422` — Future-dated posts (clock skew, or a malicious server setting `createdAt` years ahead) are clamped to `ageH = 0`, so they never decay, always qualify as TikTok "fresh" exploration picks, and sit at the top indefinitely. → Treat `created > now + 10 min` as untrusted: use the fetch time, or `ageH` of the post's position, or demote it. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:334-351` — In the Facebook ranker, a home post that's there because someone you follow (not mutual) boosted it is scored as a stranger (0.3) and labelled "suggested". Only mutual boosters count. → Treat `boostedBy` followed as closeness ≥ `FB_CLOSENESS.following` with the reason "shared by someone you follow". · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:89` — X's sources include `'local'`, but `rankX` (`:264`) discards every out-of-network post that has no tag or liked-author hit and isn't from `'trend'`, so nearly every local candidate fetched each refill is thrown away. → Drop `'local'` from X's sources, or let local posts through with an out-of-network discount like trends. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:276-282,292,303` — The "why" reasons are internal jargon ("social proof", "worth opening", "reply-heavy", "author diversity"), and "author diversity" is a *demotion*, not a reason you're seeing the post. → Rewrite as viewer-facing phrases ("Popular with people you follow", "Lots of replies", "From a trending topic") and never append demotions to `why`. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:313-316` — The in/out-of-network interleave recounts `mixed.filter(x => x.inNetwork)` on every push (O(n²)). With pools of several hundred unserved posts re-ranked on each refill, that's wasted work. → Keep a running `inCount`. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:118-124` — `stripHtml` turns every entity into a space, so Mastodon's `don&#39;t` becomes "don t" and `&amp;` becomes a gap. That pollutes `likedWords`/interest matching and the engagement-bait regex ("like if you&#39;re…"). → Decode entities (a shared helper, also used by `utils/algorithms.ts`) before tokenizing. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:128` — `contentWords` assumes space-separated words: CJK/Thai posts become one giant "word" (or a few), so TikTok-style interest matching never fires for those languages. → Use `Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: 'word' })` when available. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:484-487` — `viewerLang` comes from `navigator.language` only, ignoring the account's posting language and chosen UI language, so a bilingual user is penalised (×0.8) for their own language. → Seed it from `settingsStore` preferences (`posting:default:language`) and accept a set of languages. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/rankers.ts:223-224` — The engagement-bait regex is English-only. Non-English bait ("teilen wenn", "comparte si", "いいねしたら") is never demoted. → Document the limitation in the Facebook description, or add the top few languages. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/RealPostCard.vue:776` (cross-cutting) — The "why you're seeing this" line is hidden in Flick mode (`!isFlip`), which is the natural way to view TikTok-style feeds, so the ranker's explanation disappears exactly there. → Show a compact reason chip in the flip overlay. · judgment

### app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts
- [ ] [CODE·H·M] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:95-110` (+ `app/utils/rankers.ts:260-264`) — In-network is decided only by `ctx.following`, which is capped at `FOLLOW_MAX = 400`, comes back `[]` when the request fails (`soft`), and is empty when `currentUser` isn't loaded yet (key falls to `'guest'`, `:72`, and is cached for 15 min). X then **drops** home-timeline posts from people you do follow ("no social proof"), down to an empty feed. → Treat `_rankSource === 'home'` (non-boost) as in-network in `rankX`. Don't cache a guest context for a signed-in account. Mark the context partial on failure and retry. · judgment
  - *Verifier:* `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:95-110` / `app/utils/rankers.ts:276` — Home posts from follows beyond 400, or from everyone when the `following` fetch soft-fails, are dropped by X's social-proof gate, and a failed context is cached for 15 min. But the "currentUser not loaded → guest key" path can't happen, because `activeAccount` requires `user` (`instances.ts:267-274`). Corrected: drop that sub-claim and keep the >400 and failure-cached cases. Impact: High on failure (near-empty feed), otherwise Medium.
- [ ] [CODE·H·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:39-49,97,100` — `pageAll` pages following/followers with `maxId = lastAccount.id`, but Mastodon paginates these endpoints by the *follow relationship* id (Link header), not account id. Page 2+ is wrong (skips, repeats or ends), so mutuals/following are wrong for anyone with more than 80 follows. → Use `cursorPage(... .$raw(...))` and `nextMaxId` from the Link header, as `fetchFollowedTags` already does. · mech ✓
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:77-80` — `followedTags` is copied from `groups.followedTags` at context-load time. At app start `initializeGroups` is still in flight, so the set is empty and cached for 15 min: the "tag" source is marked exhausted (`:164-166`), and X's tag social-proof and FB's "your group" never fire. Joining a group also has no effect for 15 min. → Await `groups.initializeGroups()` before reading, and read `groups.followedTags` live in `fetchSource`/rank instead of snapshotting. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·M] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:179-226` — The composable's state (`pool`, `queue`, `served`, `cursors`) is shared and unguarded. TimelineColumn's refresh resets `isLoadingMore` and starts a new generation while an older `nextPage` is still awaiting `refill`, so the stale call later pushes old pages into the *new* pool, overwrites `queue`, and marks new-session posts as served (they never display). → Add a session token captured in `nextPage`/`refill` and bail after each await if it changed. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:173-175` — When a source throws (no `/trends/statuses` on GoToSocial/Akkoma, 401 on auth-gated local timelines) the catch returns `[]` but leaves the cursor `undefined`, so every refill re-requests the failing endpoint. → Set `cursors[source] = null` on 404/401/403/501, and use backoff for others. · mech
- [ ] [PERF·M·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:168-171` — The "tag" source re-fetches the *first page* of each followed tag on every rotation (no `maxId` per tag) up to 3 times. Rounds 2–3 return only already-pooled posts and spend a request each. → Keep a per-tag `maxId` cursor and mark a tag exhausted when it returns nothing new. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:195` — When a ranker returns no reasons (TikTok text posts with low engagement and no interest hit leave `why` empty), the label renders as "TikTok · " with a dangling separator. → Join only non-empty parts, or fall back to a reason like "Something new". · mech
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:59,186-196` — `pool` grows without bound for the session, and every refill re-runs `features()` (HTML strip, regex, tokenizing) on every unserved post. → Cache features per identity, and evict served entries older than a few pages. · judgment
- [ ] [PERF·L·S] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts:221` — A fresh load runs two full refills (up to 5 sources × 2), plus up to 12 context requests (5+5 follow pages, favourites, bookmarks): ~22 requests before the first post. On slow networks the column spins for seconds. → Rank and serve after the first refill, and run the second in the background. · judgment

### app/utils/algorithms.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:64,82` — `TAG_RE = /^[a-z0-9_]+$/i` silently drops non-ASCII tags from include/exclude lists (`#café`, `#日本`, `#straße`). The recipe saves without them, so "Must include #café" shows everything. → Reuse `isGroupTagName` (Unicode letters/marks/numbers). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:147-151` — Keyword filters use plain substring matching: exclude "ai" hides "said", "again", "rain"; include "art" matches "start", "party". (The groups code already fixed the same issue for categories.) → Match on word boundaries (`\b` with the `u` flag, or token sets), and allow an explicit `*` for prefix. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:104-115` — This `stripHtml` decodes only `&nbsp; &amp; &lt; &gt;`. Mastodon encodes apostrophes and quotes as `&#39;`/`&quot;`, so a keyword like `don't` or `"breaking"` never matches. → Decode numeric and named entities (DOMParser or a shared helper). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:147` — Keyword filters only look at `content`. The CW/`spoilerText`, media alt text and poll options are ignored, so "Exclude keywords: spoilers, politics" lets through posts whose CW says exactly that. → Include `spoilerText` (and alt text) in the haystack. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:440` — The single-quote phrase regex `'([^']{2,64})'` grabs text between apostrophes. "cat's pics and dog's pics" adds the include keyword "s pics and dog", so the feed shows nothing. → Only treat `'` as a quote when preceded by start/space and followed by space/punct at the end, or support only double/curly quotes. · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:251-264` — `recipeSummary` omits exclude tags/keywords (and all but the first include keyword). The import preview and editor preview never show what a recipe *hides*, so a shared link can quietly filter out a topic. → Append "−#politics", "−'giveaway'" (and "+N more") to the summary. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:85,96,99` — Tag and keyword lists are silently truncated to 12 entries and keywords over 64 chars are silently dropped. Nothing in the editor says so. → Expose the limits as constants and show "12 max" helper text and a warning in AlgorithmSheet. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:210` — A payload with `v !== 1` (e.g., a link from a newer NeoSpace) gets the same generic "invalid or corrupted" error, and there's no migration hook. → Return a typed error (`'version'`) and show "This link was made with a newer NeoSpace — refresh to update". · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/utils/algorithms.ts:411` — Source detection treats any "local" as the Local timeline, so "local news without replies" becomes a Local-timeline recipe with keyword "news". → Require phrasing like "on local", "local timeline" or "this server". · judgment

### app/stores/algorithms.ts
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/stores/algorithms.ts:244-248` — Importing when you already have 24 custom algorithms silently **deletes your oldest one**, with no confirm or undo, and any column using it then errors with "This algorithm was deleted…". → Refuse with "You have 24 algorithms — delete one to import" (or confirm, naming the victim), and remove its columns consistently. · mech
  - *Verifier:* True: at `MAX_CUSTOM`=24, `importPayload` silently removes the oldest custom algorithm, and `import.vue:48` shows no confirm. It's only reachable with 24 custom algorithms, though. Impact: Medium (silent data loss in a rare state).
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/algorithms.ts:230-242` — The import dedupe ("twin") ignores `ranker`. Importing a TikTok-ranked link whose name and filters equal an existing X-ranked recipe returns the X one, and the user never gets what was shared. → Add `r.ranker === recipe.ranker` (and description) to the twin check. · mech
- [ ] [CODE·M·S] `app/stores/algorithms.ts:97-101,72-80` — No `storage` event listener and hydrate-once: two tabs editing recipes overwrite each other's list wholesale (last `saveCustom` wins with a stale in-memory array). → Listen for `storage` on `STORAGE_KEY` and re-hydrate, and merge by id/`updatedAt` on save. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/stores/algorithms.ts:75-79` — Quota/private-mode save failures are swallowed, so the toast says "Saved", the recipe works until reload, then vanishes. → Return a boolean from `saveCustom` and toast "Saved for this session only — storage is full". · mech
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/stores/algorithms.ts:51-64` — Untrusted localStorage `mediaOnly`/`noReblogs`/`noReplies` are spread as-is (`"false"` is truthy), and a custom entry whose id equals a builtin id (`builtin-x`) replaces the builtin and can then be deleted. → Coerce booleans with `=== true`, and skip custom ids starting with `builtin-`. · mech

### app/pages/algorithms/import.vue
- [ ] [SEC·M·S] `app/pages/algorithms/import.vue:70-73` — "Curated by @{authorAcct} · {authorName}" comes straight from the unauthenticated link payload. Anyone can mint a link "curated by @Gargron@mastodon.social" (bidi/control characters allowed) to borrow trust for a feed that hides topics. → Label it as unverified ("Says it's from @x"), strip bidi/control characters, and link the acct to the real profile. · judgment
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/algorithms/import.vue:53-54` — `ensureFocusedView` can remove the user's last column when the board is full, or return `null`, yet the page always toasts "Added …" and navigates home. → Check the return value, and warn/confirm before evicting a column ("Your board is full — replace {column}?"). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/pages/algorithms/import.vue:66-87` — There's no warning for guests importing a recipe that needs sign-in (home source, or X/Facebook ranker with `needsAuth`). The column then errors, or (ranker + local source) silently shows only trending posts. → Show "Sign in to use this algorithm" with a login link (`returnTo` this URL) when `!hasAuthenticatedInstance && (s === 'home' || RANKERS[r]?.needsAuth)`. Also gate `RANKERS.needsAuth` in `TimelineColumn.vue:113`, which only checks `source`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/algorithms/import.vue:74-76` — "This adds a filtered view on *your* timelines" is wrong for ranked recipes, which pull trending, federated and followed-tag posts from beyond your timelines. → Branch the note on `preview.ranker`. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/pages/algorithms/import.vue:48-54` — Re-importing a link you already have (`twin`) still toasts "Added …", as if it were a new feed. → Toast "Already in your feeds — opened it" when the twin path was taken (return a flag from `importPayload`). · mech

### app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue
- [ ] [CODE·H·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:237-256` — "Share" calls `algorithms.upsert` as a side effect. When editing a builtin, `id` is `undefined` every time, so **each Share click forks a new saved copy** (same name) until the 24 cap, after which Share fails with "Couldn't prepare share link". It also saves unsaved drafts the user may have meant to discard. → Encode the share link from the in-memory form without persisting, or persist once and set `algorithms.editingId` to the fork's id. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·H·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:373,196,238` — Merely *focusing* the Describe textarea sets `sentenceDrivesForm = true`. Save/Share then re-parse the sentence (pre-filled with the recipe's description, `:172`) and overwrite every manually edited rule. Editing the "X-style For You" builtin replaces its rules with keywords parsed from the ranker's marketing description. → Flip `sentenceDrivesForm` only on an actual `input` event in the textarea, and don't pre-fill `sentence` with the description when editing. · mech ✓
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:97-100` — `liveSummary` splits the raw fields instead of using `normalizeTagList`/`normalizeKeywordList`, so the preview shows tags that will be dropped on save (`#café`, `c++`, the 13th tag). → Run the same normalizers in the preview, and mark dropped entries inline ("#c++ isn't a valid tag"). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:434-460,488-490` — With a ranker selected, the Source pills stay fully interactive while the hint says "Source above is ignored", and the hint lists internal ids ("federated, trend, tag, local, home"). → Disable or hide the Source fieldset when `ranker` is set, and map ids to labels ("trending posts, your followed tags, Local…"). · mech
- [ ] [UX·M·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:30-35,317` — Escape, backdrop tap and Cancel close the sheet and discard all edits without asking. Rules forms are long, and a stray tap loses them. → Track dirty state against `resetFromRecipe()` values and confirm "Discard changes?". · judgment
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:42,144` — `initialFocus: '.algo-sheet__sentence'`, but when editing, the sheet opens on the Rules tab where the textarea is `v-show`-hidden (`display:none`), so initial focus fails and keyboard users start nowhere predictable. → Pick the initial focus by tab (`.algo-sheet__name` for Rules). · mech
- [ ] [A11Y·M·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:389-404` — Parsed chips ("Local", "No boosts", "−politics") appear visually as you type, but there's no live region, so screen-reader users get no feedback that their sentence was understood. → Add a polite `role="status"` sr-only summary ("Understood: Local, no boosts, excluding politics"), debounced. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:220-222` — "Couldn't save — too many custom algorithms?" guesses with a question mark. The real cap (24) is known. → "You can keep up to 24 algorithms. Delete one to save this." · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:206-207` — Saving an edited builtin silently creates a custom copy with the **same name**, and the open column stays on the builtin, so the picker shows two "Media" entries and the user's edit seems to have no effect. → Suffix the fork's name ("Media (custom)"), switch open columns to the fork, and toast "Saved as a copy". · judgment
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:264-282` — On desktop browsers with `navigator.share`, cancelling the OS share sheet throws `AbortError`, which falls through to the clipboard and toasts "Share link copied", which the user didn't ask for. → Return silently on `AbortError`. · mech
- [ ] [DESIGN·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:888-890` — The selected pill changes `gap` and `padding-left` and inserts a check icon, so pills shift width and the row reflows on every selection. → Reserve the icon slot (fixed width) on all pills and keep padding constant. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:475` — Each ranker's explanation lives only in a `title` tooltip, which touch and keyboard users never see until they select it. → Show a one-line description under each pill, or a radio-card layout. · judgment
- [ ] [CODE·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:561` — `liveSummary || 'Start describing to preview rules'` — `recipeSummary` always returns at least the source/ranker, so the fallback copy is dead. → Show the fallback when no filter fields are set, or delete it. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:567` — After sharing, the URL is dumped as a raw base64 paragraph with no copy button or link styling. → Render a read-only input with a "Copy" button. · mech
- [ ] [UX·L·S] `app/components/AlgorithmSheet.vue:457` (+ `app/utils/algorithms.ts:59,256`) — "For You" means *your home timeline* in Source and summaries, while the ranked builtins are named "X-style For You" / "TikTok-style For You" (ranked multi-source). One term means two different feeds. → Rename the source to "Home" (or "Following"). · decision

### tests (gaps)
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·S] `tests/rankers.test.ts:42` — Tests set `selfAcct = 'me@home.social'`, but production uses `user.acct` (local form `me`, `useRankedFeed.ts:85`), and no test covers the local-part collisions in `mentionsYou`/`isFollowed`. → Add cases with the real shapes: `selfAcct='alice'` with a mention of `alice@other`, and following `bob` with a post from `bob@x`. · mech
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·S] `tests/rankers.test.ts` — No coverage for hostile or degenerate inputs: negative/NaN counts, future `createdAt`, empty candidate lists, the empty-`why` label, or NaN in the sort. → Add a property-style test asserting every score is finite and the output is a permutation of the filtered input. · mech
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·M] `app/composables/useRankedFeed.ts` — Zero tests for the ranked-feed session (cursor pagination, `dry` detection, served-never-moves, refresh during refill). → Add vitest with stubbed masto clients for those four behaviours. · judgment
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·S] `tests/rankers.test.ts:164-177` — Algorithm-recipe tests cover only ranker round-tripping. There are none for `statusMatchesRecipe` (entities, CW, word boundaries), `parseAlgorithmSentence` (apostrophes, "local news"), `importPayload` (twin vs ranker, 24-cap eviction) or non-ASCII `normalizeTagList`. → Add a `tests/algorithms.test.ts` covering each. · mech
- [ ] [TOOLING·M·S] `tests/discovery.test.ts:87-120` — Group tests cover only the timeline cache and `isGroupTagName`. There are none for join/leave optimistic rollback, search-result membership after join, account-switch reset, `formatTagAsName` (NixOS/JavaScript), or trending-flag refresh. → Add store tests with a mocked client. · mech
